### [1] [Spheriverse: 3D Scene Understanding from Spherical Observations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09012)
*Fei Teng,Sheng Wu,Mengfei Duan,Guoqiang Zhao,Junhui Ma,Kai Luo,Siyu Li,Hao Shi,Zhiyong Li,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spheriverse数据集（64,400对球面图像-激光雷达）及SphereOcc占用预测框架，通过Cartesian-Spherical表示重建模和球面证据重查询弥合球面与笛卡尔空间差异，在语义占用预测上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面观测提供全局视觉上下文，但球面图像处于角度域而物理世界用笛卡尔坐标表示，导致跨空间表示差距，使几何对应和语义证据聚合变得困难。

Method: 构建Spheriverse数据集（644个序列，64,400对时间对齐的球面图像-LiDAR），并建立三个基准。提出SphereOcc：CSRR通过区域调制将球面距离-方位角几何融入笛卡尔体素特征；SER基于体素内容和距离-高度-方位角几何自适应地从球面图像特征中检索语义证据。

Result: SphereOcc在语义占用预测达到13.91% mIoU和24.65% GeoIoU，分别比TPVFormer和SurroundOcc高1.70和2.10个百分点，且在五个场景、多种空间分区和减小视场下均排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模球面-笛卡尔几何关系与自适应语义检索，SphereOcc有效提升密集预测性能，数据集和代码将公开以推动研究。

Abstract: Spherical observations provide global visual context for 3D scene understanding. However, visual information is encoded in an angular domain, whereas the physical world is represented in Cartesian coordinates. This cross-space representation gap complicates geometric correspondence and semantic evidence aggregation. To delve into this challenge, we introduce Spheriverse, comprising $64,400$ temporally aligned spherical image-LiDAR pairs organized into 644 sequences. The dataset spans diverse scenes, illumination, and weather conditions, with fine-grained semantic classes. We further establish benchmarks for semantic occupancy prediction, semantic mapping, and 3D object detection, evaluating 30+ methods through overall and scene-wise comparisons. For dense prediction, we propose SphereOcc, an occupancy framework that couples spherical geometry modeling with semantic evidence retrieval. Cartesian-Spherical Representation Remodeling (CSRR) incorporates spherical range-azimuth geometry into Cartesian voxel features through region-wise modulation. Spherical Evidence Re-querying (SER) then conditions queries on voxel content and range-height-azimuth geometry to adaptively retrieve relevant semantic evidence from source spherical image features. SphereOcc achieves 13.91% mIoU and 24.65% GeoIoU, outperforming the respective best-performing methods, TPVFormer and SurroundOcc, by 1.70 and 2.10 percentage points. It also ranks first in both metrics across all five scenes, with consistent advantages across the evaluated spatial partitions and reduced fields of view. The established benchmark and source code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [2] [FRAME: Factored Retrieval via Attribute Readouts for Object-Centric Scene Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08886)
*Woosang Jeon,Sanghyeok Choi,Minwoo Kim,Taehyun Jung,Taehyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出属性组合检索问题，并介绍FRAME方法，通过将语言查询转换为属性权重、从对象嵌入中估计各属性证据，再按查询聚合证据来排序对象，实现高效准确的多属性场景记忆检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导机器人需要持久场景记忆来执行指令和引用对象，但现有检索多强调空间或关系引用，而日常对象引用常涉及多个持久属性（如类别、材质、尺寸、外观），该能力尚未被直接形式化研究。

Method: 形式化属性组合检索，提出受控评估协议以固定场景记忆和属性定义目标，将检索与感知及注释歧义分离；提出FRAME方法，将语言转化为查询相关属性权重，使用学习读头估计对象嵌入中的各属性证据，并按查询聚合这些证据进行对象排序。

Result: 在未见场景和对象资产上，FRAME优于代表性场景记忆检索基线，同时将对象评分简化为轻量矩阵-向量计算，提升了效率。

Conclusion: 属性组合检索可作为语言引导机器人的一种互补场景记忆能力，持久对象属性可作为可组合证据，支持准确高效的多属性检索。

Abstract: Language-guided robots need persistent scene memories to follow instructions, revisit objects, and resolve references to objects encountered over time. While much of language-guided scene-memory retrieval has emphasized spatial or relational references, many everyday object references specify objects by multiple persistent attributes, such as category, material, size, or surface appearance. We formalize this problem as attribute-compositional retrieval, where a fixed object-centric scene memory is queried with natural language to retrieve the object satisfying the requested attributes. To investigate this capability directly, we introduce a controlled evaluation protocol with fixed scene memories and attribute-defined targets, separating retrieval from perception and annotation ambiguities. We then propose FRAME, which turns language into query-relevant attribute weights, uses learned readouts to estimate per-attribute evidence from object embeddings, and ranks objects by aggregating this evidence according to the query. Across held-out scenes and object assets, FRAME outperforms representative scene-memory retrieval baselines while reducing post-decomposition object scoring to lightweight matrix-vector computation. These results position attribute-compositional retrieval as a complementary scene-memory capability for language-guided robots, showing that persistent object attributes can be exposed as composable evidence for accurate and efficient multi-attribute retrieval.

</details>


### [3] [FIRE3D: Feed-forward Interactive 3D Scene Reconstruction Within A Minute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08848)
*Hongchi Xia,Tianhang Cheng,Wei-Chiu Ma,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FIRE3D 是一个统一框架，可从单张 RGB 图像或 RGB 视频在不到一分钟内生成可直接用于仿真交互的 3D 场景资产，无需测试时优化，速度比现有方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么需要耗时的测试时优化，要么缺乏对象级完整性和物理解耦能力，难以满足游戏和交互应用对快速、仿真就绪 3D 场景的需求。

Method: FIRE3D 采用前馈端到端网络，从 RGB 捕获估计的 RGB-D 观测中预测组合式场景表示，包括每个对象的 6-DoF 位姿、包围盒、网格和纹理；场景被建模为离散实体集合，实现物理解耦。

Result: 在姿态精度、几何完整性和纹理质量上达到竞争性或最先进水平，同时比现有交互就绪方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: FIRE3D 为快速生成仿真就绪场景提供了高效方案，兼顾质量与速度，适用于游戏和交互应用。

Abstract: We present FIRE3D, a unified framework that takes a single RGB image or casual RGB video and transforms it into simulation-ready 3D scene assets for games and interactive applications in under a minute. At the core of FIRE3D is a feed-forward, end-to-end network that predicts a compositional scene representation from posed RGB-D observations estimated from the RGB capture, including the 6-DoF pose, bounding box, mesh, and texture for every object. By modeling the scene as a collection of discrete entities, FIRE3D produces amodally complete and simulation-ready environments where objects are physically decoupled and ready for interaction. Our framework requires no test-time optimization, runs orders of magnitude faster than prior interaction-ready methods, and provides object-level completeness beyond existing feed-forward 3D approaches. We demonstrate competitive or state-of-the-art results across pose accuracy, geometry completeness, and texture quality across various datasets while being orders of magnitudes faster. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [4] [AirAnchor: Bridging Local and Global Spatial Information for Zero-Shot Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08442)
*Shanwei Fan,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu,Yingxuan Teng,Siqi Dai,Lin Cheng,Guoliang Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AirAnchor 是一种用于空中视觉语言导航（Aerial VLN）的新范式，通过空间锚点桥接局部与全局空间信息，并集成到统一框架中，显著优于现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本空中导航方法通常只在单一空间尺度上操作，要么依赖在线构建的局部表示，要么依赖离线构建的全局记忆，缺乏对局部与全局空间信息的综合运用，限制了导航决策的准确性。

Method: AirAnchor 包含三个核心组件：查询驱动的空间锚点定位（识别决策相关锚点并组织为局部表示）、持久对象空间记忆（增量维护对象知识库作为全局记忆并检索地标先验）、以及空间信息导航代理（将局部和全局信息显式集成到智能决策框架中）。

Result: 在 AerialVLN 数据集上的大量实验表明，AirAnchor 大幅超越现有零样本基线，验证了其有效性和效率。

Conclusion: AirAnchor 通过空间锚点统一局部与全局空间信息，实现了全面的空间定位，为空中 VLN 提供了一种新的有效范式。

Abstract: Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation requires drones to follow natural-language instructions and navigate through complex urban environments. Accurate navigation relies on both local and global spatial information, which support immediate action grounding and long-horizon path planning, respectively. However, existing zero-shot methods typically operate at a single spatial scale, relying either on local representations constructed online from current observations or on global memories built offline from historical experience. To address this limitation, we propose AirAnchor, a new paradigm that bridges local and global spatial information through spatial anchors and integrates both into a shared navigation framework, enabling comprehensive spatial grounding for decision-making. AirAnchor consists of three core components: (1) Query-Driven Spatial Anchor Grounding, which identifies decision-relevant anchors from visual observations and organizes them into local spatial representations; (2) Persistent Object Spatial Memory, which incrementally maintains an object knowledge base as persistent global spatial memory and retrieves landmark-related spatial priors; and (3) a Spatially-Informed Navigation Agent, which explicitly integrates both local and global spatial information into an agentic framework for decision-making. Extensive experiments on AerialVLN demonstrate that AirAnchor substantially outperforms existing zero-shot baselines, validating the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed paradigm.

</details>


### [5] [GALoc: Gravity Aligned Wireframes for Depth-Free Monocular Floorplan Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08385)
*Jeahn Han,Minji Kim,Jeongbin Sohn,Jonghyeok Park,Matthias Wuest,Pyojin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GALoc是一种基于几何优先的室内定位框架，利用重力对齐的线框替代深度预测，通过构造线性约束矩阵和全局搜索求解相机位姿，实现与平面图的匹配，在可见墙体几何充足时优于基于深度的方法，并在结构缺失场景中主动放弃预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度网络的室内定位方法在杂乱场景中表现脆弱，而平面图本身是紧凑且外观不变的理想定位源，因此需要一种不依赖深度预测、而是利用图像中几何约束（如垂直性和共面性）的稳健方法。

Method: GALoc从单目RGB、相机内参、相对位姿和IMU方向出发，构建编码垂直性和共面性的线性约束矩阵，通过全局搜索找到最小奇异值最小的相机规范（gauge），再以闭式FOV一致变换将校正后的线框投影到鸟瞰图，最后通过无度量的SE(2)搜索与平面图匹配。

Result: 在Structured3D上进行端到端评估，在Gibson上添加校准噪声，并在真实自采序列上验证。当墙体几何充分可见时，GALoc匹配或超越基于深度的基线：在Gibson的100步序列中，GALoc的0.1m顺序定位成功率达88%，而基线仅为68%，同时在结构缺失场景中选择放弃预测。

Conclusion: GALoc证明了在室内定位中优先使用几何结构（线框）而非深度预测的有效性，能够提高鲁棒性并在无结构信息时安全弃权，为实用室内定位提供了更可靠的新方案。

Abstract: Floorplans are compact, appearance-invariant maps ideal for indoor localization, yet existing methods rely on depth networks that are brittle in cluttered scenes. We propose GALoc, a geometry-first framework that replaces depth prediction with gravity-aligned wireframes that satisfy verticality and coplanarity by construction. Given monocular RGB, camera intrinsics, relative poses, and IMU orientation, GALoc constructs a linear constraint matrix encoding verticality and coplanarity, and finds the camera gauge minimizing its smallest singular value via global search. The rectified wireframes are projected into bird's-eye-view layouts through a closed-form, FOV-consistent transformation and matched against the floorplan via metric-free SE(2) search. We evaluate end-to-end on Structured3D, with calibrated noise on Gibson, and on real-world author-collected sequences. When sufficient wall geometry is visible, GALoc matches or outperforms depth-based baselines -- achieving 88% sequential localization success at 0.1m over 100-step sequences on Gibson vs the baseline's 68% -- while abstaining in structure-blind scenes.

</details>


### [6] [Drive by Hindsight and Foresight: Tool-Grounded Synergistic Reasoning over Hierarchical Memory for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08217)
*Baojie Chen,Zijun Jia,Jing Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个将分层驾驶记忆与主动工具调用紧密耦合的闭环推理框架，通过场景级短期记忆和进化式长期记忆提升7B模型的时空感知与推理能力，在DriveLMM-o1上以80.03推理分数和79.09% MCQ准确率超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在自动驾驶中仍存在幻觉、时空感知弱、泛化受限；CoT、RAG或静态工具注入虽丰富上下文，但模型无法主动感知场景信息或积累经验，缺乏动态闭环学习能力。

Method: 构建三层贡献：(i)分层驾驶记忆（场景短期记忆+可复用经验长期记忆）；(ii)记忆-工具协同推理框架（推理时根据场景状态自适应调用工具，离线将可复用经验固化到长期记忆）；(iii)数据生成与两阶段训练管道（多步教师rollout验证记忆-工具轨迹，经SFT和GRPO训练7B模型）。

Result: 在DriveLMM-o1上推理分数80.03，MCQ准确率79.09%，比最强基线高7.74个MCQ点；STSBench准确率提升24.2点，离线长期记忆固化在参数冻结情况下额外带来3.57点MCQ提升，跨基准泛化强。

Conclusion: 分层记忆组件互补有效：短期记忆增强时空理解，离线长期记忆凝练驱动经验，支持全参数冻结下的持续自进化，为自动驾驶闭环推理与记忆机制提供新范式。

Abstract: VLMs have shown promise for autonomous driving, yet still suffer from hallucination, weak spatio-temporal perception, and limited generalization. Recent methods improve reasoning and decision-making through CoT explanations, retrieval-augmented generation or the static injection of tool outputs. Although these mechanisms enrich the context, the model neither proactively perceives scene information nor accumulates experience after answering. To overcome these limitations, we present, to our knowledge, the first synergistic framework that tightly couples hierarchical memory with proactive tool invocation in a closed reasoning loop. Our contributions are threefold. (i) Hierarchical Driving Memory: a scene-level short-term memory maintains the dynamic scene state, and an evolving long-term memory retrieves reusable experience and tool strategies. (ii) Memory-Tool Synergistic Reasoning Framework: guided by the scene state and retrieved experience, the model adaptively invokes tools to refine its reasoning at inference time and consolidates reusable experience into a long-term memory pool offline. (iii) Data Generation and Two-stage Training Pipeline: verified memory-tool trajectories built by multi-step teacher rollout are used to train with SFT and GRPO. Our 7B model reaches an overall reasoning score of 80.03 and MCQ accuracy of 79.09% on DriveLMM-o1, surpassing the strongest baseline by 7.74 MCQ points and generalizes strongly across benchmarks. Notably, ablation and analysis studies validate the effectiveness of each component and further reveal the complementary roles of hierarchical memory. Short-term memory strengthens spatio-temporal understanding, improving STSBench accuracy by 24.2 points, while offline long-term memory consolidation yields an additional 3.57-point MCQ gain with all parameters frozen, demonstrating continual self-evolution through accumulated driving experience.

</details>


### [7] [Observe Before You Alert: Adaptive Driver Alerting with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08130)
*Yuhang Wang,Lingyao Li,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VLAlert框架，将驾驶警报生成建模为SILENT、OBSERVE、ALERT三动作策略，通过观察动作自适应收集证据，并利用视觉语言模型生成安全证据，在真实驾驶数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事故预测模型通常直接输出二值风险分数，依赖阈值处理模糊场景，未能显式建模何时收集证据与何时发出警报的决策问题。

Method: VLAlert使用Qwen3-VL-4B生成安全证据，并通过结构化信念池的隐藏状态形成紧凑表示，用于危险估计和策略预测。其观察动作作为内部证据收集决策，可延迟不确定警告并影响下一观测窗口，构建轻量级感知-行动循环。

Result: 在VLAlert-Bench验证集上，VLAlert的效用指标DAUS达0.4878（对比Open-BADAS的0.4752）；AUROC、AP_tick、F1_t和平衡精度分别从0.610/0.176/0.276/0.581提升至0.689/0.195/0.297/0.648。在221个ADAS接管片段上，R@5s从74.2%提升至88.7%，F1从0.585提升至0.686。

Conclusion: 自适应观察机制和安全聚焦的视觉语言模型表示能有效改善面向驾驶员的警报决策，验证了延迟证据收集对不确定性处理的价值。

Abstract: Driver alerting from dashcam video requires sequential decision-making under partial observability: a system must decide not only whether a scene is risky, but also when the evidence is sufficient to warn. Most existing accident anticipation models output a binary risk score, leaving ambiguous scenes to be handled by thresholding. We propose VLAlert, a vision-language alerting framework that casts warning generation as a tri-action policy over SILENT, OBSERVE, and ALERT. The OBSERVE action acts as an internal evidence-gathering decision that delays uncertain warnings and changes the next observation window, creating a lightweight perception-action loop for adaptive alerting. VLAlert uses Qwen3-VL-4B as a safety-evidence generator and pools hidden states from structured belief spans to form compact representations for danger estimation and policy prediction. We evaluate VLAlert on VLAlert-Bench, a unified per-tick benchmark from four real-world dashcam alert datasets, and further test transfer to held-out naturalistic ADAS takeover clips. On VLAlert-Bench validation, VLAlert achieves the highest deployment-oriented utility among tested baselines, with DAUS 0.4878 compared with 0.4752 for Open-BADAS, and improves AUROC, AP_tick, F1_t, and balanced accuracy from 0.610, 0.176, 0.276, and 0.581 to 0.689, 0.195, 0.297, and 0.648, respectively. On 221 held-out ADAS-TO-Critic clips, VLAlert improves R@5s from 74.2% to 88.7% and F1 from 0.585 to 0.686. These results indicate that adaptive observation and safety-focused VLM representations provide measurable gains for driver-facing alert decisions.

</details>


### [8] [GIFT: Goal-Injected Fine-Tuning for Efficient Manipulation Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07006)
*Xiaoyuan Fang,Shuo Feng,Yuxuan Wang,Enhua Cheng,Peng Zhou,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GIFT（Goal-Injected Fine-Tuning）轻量高效微调框架，通过零初始化卷积将生成的目标图像特征逐步注入预训练VLA模型，并改进图像编辑生成一致性目标图，仅需单轮微调即在多个基准上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型过度依赖初始观察和语言指令，鲁棒性不足；利用生成模型预测目标图像作为高层视觉指导可增强鲁棒性，但已有基础模型缺乏系统的目标图像条件注入，且计算训练成本高。

Method: GIFT框架：1) 通过零初始化卷积（参数从零逐步增长）将目标图像特征加入观察，避免噪声干扰预训练策略，随训练渐进融入目标信息；2) 改进图像编辑方法，从初始观察和指令生成语义与视觉一致的目标图像。

Result: 仅用单轮微调：在SIMPLER两个设置上分别超过基座模型6.0%和13.4%，在LIBERO上提升4.7%，证明高效性与有效性。

Conclusion: GIFT是轻量且通用的VLA微调方案，能无缝集成多种预训练模型，以极低成本赋予模型目标感知能力，显著提升跨任务性能。

Abstract: Compared with relying solely on initial observations and language instructions, predicting goal images with generative models as high-level visual guidance can significantly enhance the robustness of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, most existing foundation models have not systematically incorporated goal image conditioning due to the high computational training cost. To this end, we propose Goal-Injected Fine-Tuning (GIFT), a lightweight and efficient fine-tuning framework that seamlessly integrates generated goal images into multiple representative pretrained VLA models. Our approach introduces goal image features into observations via a zero-initialized convolution which progressively grows parameters from zero and prevents harmful noise from disrupting the pretrained policy during fine-tuning. As training proceeds, goal information is gradually incorporated, enabling efficient goal understanding without disrupting model stability. We further introduce a refined image editing method to generate semantically and visually consistent goal images from initial observations and task instructions. Experiments show that goal-aware VLA models achieve substantial performance gains across tasks: with only a single epoch of fine-tuning, GIFT outperforms the base model by 6.0% and 13.4% on two SIMPLER settings, and by 4.7% on LIBERO, demonstrating both efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [9] [Joint-Conditioned Stereo Surface Reasoning for Interaction Field Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06955)
*Yanlin Jin,Yifan Yang,Bowen Yang,Kai Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种关节条件立体表面推理方法（JSSR），通过联合预测3D关节、直接交互场和每视图端点证据，并利用校准候选搜索和手共享候选支持，解决手-物体交互场预测中的最近表面点估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手-物体交互场预测需要为每个手关节找到最近的物体表面点，但图像区域通常较小且部分遮挡。现有方法未充分利用关节与表面端点之间的条件关系以及手部共享的表面证据。

Method: 将任务视为关节条件下的表面端点估计，构建时间立体网络联合预测3D关节、直接交互场和每视图端点证据。使用校准候选搜索评估端点假设，结合关节特定的图像兼容性和跨视图对应，并通过手共享候选支持让关节利用共同表面证据，最后用学习残差门控在观察模糊时控制几何校正。

Result: 该系统的构建方法在SHOW3D交互场挑战赛排行榜上排名第三。

Conclusion: JSSR方法通过关节条件推理和立体对应有效改进了手-物体交互场预测，验证了共享表面证据和自适应校正的实用性。

Abstract: Predicting hand--object interaction fields requires locating the nearest object-surface point for each hand joint, often from small and partially occluded image regions. We view this task as joint-conditioned surface-endpoint estimation: each joint has its own nearest endpoint, while endpoints from the same hand can draw on shared local surface evidence. This structure motivates Joint-Conditioned Stereo Surface Reasoning (JSSR). A temporal-stereo network jointly predicts 3D joints, a direct interaction field, and per-view endpoint evidence. Calibrated candidate search evaluates endpoint hypotheses using joint-specific image compatibility and cross-view correspondence. A hand-shared candidate support lets joints draw on common surface evidence, and a learned residual gate controls the geometric correction when observations are ambiguous. Our system built on this method ranked third on the SHOW3D Interaction Field Challenge leaderboard.

</details>


### [10] [Contextual Observer Grounding: Evaluating Situated Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06880)
*Mimo Shirasaka,Haochen Zhang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出POVBench基准，评估VLM在具身指令中从情境线索推断说话者视角并进行空间定位的能力，发现现有VLM即使视角明确仍面临挑战，分解观察者相对推理可提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身任务（如机器人）需要理解说话者情境视角下的空间关系，人类可从共享环境知识、活动上下文和常识推断，但VLM是否具备这种'情境观察者基础'能力尚不清楚。

Method: 构建POVBench数据集，包含3D场景和自然语言查询，区分Inferred、Stated、Given三种观察者基础形式；向模型提供多视角观测和语言句子，要求定位未见或欠指定目标；在多种SOTA VLM上测试，并探索显式分解观察者相对空间推理的影响。

Result: SOTA VLM在基于方向语言的目标定位上仍具挑战，即使观察者基础显式给出也难；但显式分解观察者相对空间推理能显著提升定位准确性。

Conclusion: 现有VLM的情境观察者基础能力不足，需要更明确的观察者-相对空间分解来提升具身指令理解和目标定位能力。

Abstract: Reasoning over language instructions in embodied tasks such as robotics often requires understanding spatial relations from a speaker's situated perspective. Humans infer such perspectives from shared environmental knowledge, activity context, and commonsense. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) appear capable of spatial reasoning, but their ability to infer a speaker's viewpoint from contextual cues and interpret situated spatial relations from that viewpoint remains unclear. We call this capability contextual observer grounding. To study this capability, we construct the Point-of-View Benchmark (POVBench), a dataset of 3D scenes and queries that disentangles Inferred, Stated, and Given forms of observer grounding in natural embodied communication. Given multi-view observations and a natural-language sentence, models must localize unseen or underspecified targets from situated spatial and contextual cues. Across state-of-the-art VLMs, localizing targets from directional language remains challenging, even when observer grounding is made explicit. We find that explicit breakdowns of observer-relative spatial reasoning improve target localization. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [11] [Physico-Geospatial Grounded Scene Interpretation for Mobile Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06629)
*Nicolas Schuler,Janik Kurtz,Lea Dewald,Marcel Sauber,Félicia Teferle,Jürgen Graf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种通过LLM融合OpenStreetMap、传感器位置时间及度量信息，增强预训练VLM场景解析输出的方法，在建筑和路径表面接地任务中取得初步成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让机器人代理在动态非结构化环境中理解场景时，不仅依赖视觉，还需集成物理-地理空间世界知识（如关系、时空推理），而现有VLM缺乏此类外部知识融合能力。

Method: 不修改预训练VLM，而是将其场景解读输出与语义描述、OpenStreetMap建筑及街道数据、传感器获得的位姿时间度量信息结合，通过LLM进行信息融合，生成物理-地理空间接地自然语言描述。

Result: 在校园户外试点评估集上，建筑接地F1分数为0.83，路径表面接地F1分数为0.64。

Conclusion: 该方法在概念上能够生成具有物理-地理空间接地的自然语言描述，展示了融合多源空间信息增强VLM解释的可行性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in deep learning allow robotic agents to interact with dynamic and unstructured environments. Of special interest is the integration of physico-geospatial world knowledge into such systems, either by using physics-aware machine learning models, knowledge graphs to model relationships or spatio-temporal and logical reasoning. In the present work, we introduce an approach to augment the output of pre-trained, unmodified VLMs used for scene interpretation by integrating semantic descriptions, OpenStreetMap building data and street information with positional, temporal and metric information obtained from our sensory systems, fusing this information using LLMs. We apply this concept to an outdoor recording within a university campus, achieving an F1-Score of 0.83 in the task of grounding buildings and 0.64 for path surface grounding on our pilot evaluation set. The results demonstrate the conceptual capability of the proposed solution to deliver physico-geospatial grounded natural language descriptions. Code and results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [12] [CST-WM: A Causally Structured World Model for Embodied Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06302)
*Junyi Hu,Shuaihang Yuan,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对具身视觉跟踪中的因果幻觉问题，提出因果结构化世界模型CST-WM，通过分解潜在状态并阻断从动作到目标证据的直接注入，使动作仅通过机器人运动和观测变化影响目标证据，结合模型预测控制实现稳定跟踪与目标重新获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉跟踪本质上是关于未来目标可观测性和表观尺度的预测决策问题。现有动作条件预测模型存在任务特定的因果幻觉：模型利用机器人控制与目标相关观测之间的强相关性，学习从当前动作到目标证据的直接因果效应，而非通过机器人运动及观测变化间接影响。这种捷径产生语义错误的未来预测，导致跟踪规划和重获取不可靠。

Method: 提出CST-WM，将潜在状态分解为目标证据分支、机器人分支和观测分支，并因子化状态转移。在转移中阻断动作到目标证据分支的直接注入，但保留动作对机器人运动和观测更新的影响。将CST-WM与基于rollout的模型预测控制（MPC）结合，统一支持稳定跟随和临时目标重新获取。

Result: 在EVT-Bench和Habitat 3.0上，覆盖标准跟踪、目标丢失恢复和跨数据集迁移场景，相比反应式基线和其他世界模型，显著提升跟随质量、距离范围控制、安全性和重获取能力。线下诊断表明CST-WM具有更好的多步rollout保真度、更强的规划价值一致性，并大幅减少直接动作泄漏。

Conclusion: 对于具身视觉跟踪，仅进行未来预测是不够的；预测结构本身必须与目标证据进入规划的因果关系一致。CST-WM通过因果建模验证了结构对齐的重要性，为可靠跟踪规划提供了新思路。

Abstract: Embodied visual tracking requires a robot not only to react to the current view, but to choose actions that preserve or recover future evidence of a moving target under ego-motion, occlusion, and distractors. It is therefore a predictive decision problem over future target observability and apparent scale. A central difficulty is a task-specific form of causal hallucination: in action-conditioned prediction, a model can exploit the strong correlation between robot control and target-related observations by hallucinating a direct causal effect from the current action to target evidence, rather than letting action influence that evidence only through robot motion and the resulting observation change. The shortcut yields plausible futures with the wrong semantics for tracking-oriented planning and re-acquisition. We propose CST-WM, a causally structured world model that decomposes the latent state into target-evidence, robot, and observation branches and factorizes the transition so that direct action injection into the target-evidence branch is blocked, while action remains available to robot motion and observation updates. Combined with rollout-based model-predictive control, CST-WM supports both stable following and temporary target re-acquisition in one planning framework. On EVT-Bench and Habitat 3.0, covering standard tracking, target-loss recovery, and cross-dataset transfer, it improves following quality, distance-range control, safety, and re-acquisition over reactive and world-model baselines; offline diagnostics show better multi-step rollout fidelity, stronger planning-value consistency, and substantially reduced direct action leakage. For embodied visual tracking, future prediction alone is not enough: the predictive structure itself must align with how target evidence enters planning.

</details>


### [13] [MobileVLA-R1 2.0: RL-Enhanced Reasoning for Mobile Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06251)
*Ting Huang,Yue Huang,Zeyu Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MobileVLA-R1 2.0，一种通过强化学习和显式推理-动作耦合的VLA框架，在导航、四足控制和人形移动操作任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统难以弥合高层语义推理与低层运动/操作控制之间的鸿沟，常依赖隐式推理或单一动作预测，导致长程决策连贯性差且动作适应性不足。

Method: 框架结合监督式思维链对齐和强化学习，学习多粒度具身轨迹推理；引入推理条件动作解码器，将多模态推理映射为任务级动作目标，再经机器人控制器转换为具体指令，实现推理与执行的显式解耦和统一接口。

Result: 在VLN-CE上平均SR提升1.6点，在真实世界G1移动操作任务中完整任务成功率较MobileVLA-R1提升10.0点，并在Unitree Go2/G1上展示跨平台鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式结构化推理与动作解耦能有效提升VLA的推理-动作一致性和跨平台可迁移性，为移动机器人长程指令跟随提供可行方案。

Abstract: Grounding natural-language instructions into reliable and executable actions remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language-action (VLA) systems on mobile robots, due to the persistent gap between high-level semantic reasoning and low-level locomotion and manipulation control. Existing approaches often rely on implicit reasoning or monolithic action prediction, making it difficult to maintain coherent long-horizon decision making while producing precise and adaptable robot actions. To address this challenge, we propose MobileVLA-R1 2.0, an RL-enhanced VLA framework that explicitly couples structured embodied reasoning with executable mobile robot control. The framework learns multi-granularity reasoning over embodied trajectories through supervised Chain-of-Thought (CoT) alignment and reinforcement learning, improving reasoning-to-action consistency beyond purely behavioral supervision. To support both locomotion and manipulation, we further introduce a reasoning-conditioned action decoder that maps multimodal reasoning representations to task-level action targets, which are subsequently translated into embodiment-specific commands by robot controllers. This design provides a unified perception-reasoning-action interface while decoupling high-level action generation from robot-specific actuation. We conduct extensive evaluations on language-guided navigation, quadruped control, and humanoid mobile manipulation, covering VLN-CE, QUARD, and real-world deployments on Unitree Go2 and G1 robots. MobileVLA-R1 2.0 consistently outperforms strong VLA baselines, achieving an average 1.6 point improvement in SR on VLN-CE and a 10.0 point improvement in full-task success on real-world G1 mobile manipulation tasks over MobileVLA-R1, while demonstrating robust long-horizon instruction following and closed-loop execution across different robotic platforms.

</details>


### [14] [Time-Aware Assistive Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05596)
*Masaki Kuribayashi,Zhongkai Shangguan,Eshed Ohn-Bar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向复杂户外环境的自我中心辅助导航任务的大规模多模态基准，发现现成MLLM在生成及时、安全导航指令方面存在根本局限，即使微调也难以解决；通过直接监督指令背后的原因，可显著提升开放循环、闭环及仿真到现实泛化性能，但仍存在时间推理、安全关键物体感知、关系与距离理解等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言智能体通常关注说什么，而不关注何时说。在实时辅助决策场景中（如引导视障人士穿越城市环境），响应时机不当会分散用户注意力或增加认知负担，因此需要研究具身代理的实时响应能力。

Method: 构建大规模多模态基准，评估现成MLLM；提出一种简单有效的模型修改，即对指令背后的底层原因进行直接监督（预测每条指令的reason），以增强模型的实时导航指令生成能力。

Result: 现成MLLM即使在大量数据微调下也难提供安全且及时的导航指令；添加原因监督后在开放循环、闭环、仿真到现实泛化场景中均获得显著性能提升；但分析显示时间推理、安全关键物体意识、关系与距离理解仍存在持续挑战。

Conclusion: 尽管原因监督有助于提升辅助导航性能，但当前MLLM在时间敏感和安全关键的实时交互中仍不足，该基准和方法的公开可促进可扩展辅助智能体的发展。

Abstract: Can interactive vision-and-language agents learn not just what to say but also \textbf{\textit{when}} to say it? Current language models rarely plan over whether and when to realize a real-time response to a user. However, providing accurate and timely support for human decision-making, such as when guiding visually impaired individuals through urban environments, requires careful real-time responsiveness--poorly timed responses can distract users or add unnecessary cognitive load. As a machine intelligence challenge for Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based agents, we introduce a large-scale multimodal benchmark for an egocentric, assistive navigation task in complex outdoor environments. Using this benchmark, we uncover a fundamental limitation of off-the-shelf MLLMs in delivering safe and time-sensitive navigation instructions, even with model fine-tuning on substantial amounts of data. We then demonstrate that a simple yet effective modification of the model, including direct supervision to predict the underlying reason for each instruction, yields significant performance gains across open-loop, closed-loop, and sim-to-real generalization settings. However, our analysis highlights persistent challenges in temporal reasoning, safety-critical object awareness, and relational and distance understanding. To advance the development of scalable assistive agents, we will release our simulation, benchmark, and code (available at the project website: this https URL ).

</details>


### [15] [SimpleMemVLA: A Simple but Effective Native-Video Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05533)
*Cheng Yin,Wang Xu,Junpeng Yang,Sikyuen Tam,Hanyu Liu,Yuan Yao,Xiangrui Zeng,Junbo Cui,Yequan Wang,Zhouping Yin,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SimpleMemVLA，无需专用记忆模块，直接将完整采样的历史以时间戳视频格式输入VLM骨干，利用生成子任务的隐藏状态作为动作头的历史通道，在四个记忆基准上达到SOTA且不损害通用控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期操控具有部分可观测性，现有记忆机制（检索库、学习压缩器、循环状态）必须在知道未来决策需求前决定保留什么，这源于旧假设认为分钟级历史过大而无法直接处理。现代VLM骨干已打破该假设，因此无需专用记忆模块。

Method: SimpleMemVLA将历史采样完整保留，并以时间戳视频格式传递给预训练的VLM骨干；通过生成子任务的隐藏状态作为唯一历史通道连接到标准流匹配动作头。利用连续决策共享大部分历史的特点，预填充共享前缀以保持接近单帧VLA的延迟。

Result: 在四个记忆基准上取得新SOTA，且不影响通用控制性能。在保持骨干和训练设置不变的情况下，大幅优于检索、压缩和循环状态机制；因果干预证实策略确实读取历史。

Conclusion: 现代VLM骨干足以直接处理长历史，SimpleMemVLA证明了简化记忆设计可实现更优性能，为长期操控提供了新方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation is partially observable: the information needed to choose the next action may appear only in observations from minutes earlier. Existing memory mechanisms: retrieval banks, learned compressors, recurrent states must decide what to keep from the past before knowing what a future decision will require. This was motivated by the assumption that minute-scale history is too large to process directly, which modern VLM backbones no longer make true. In this work, we introduce SimpleMemVLA, a VLA without a dedicated memory module. It keeps the sampled history intact and passes it to the backbone in the timestamped video format the backbone was pretrained to process; the hidden states of a generated sub-task then form the only channel from history to a standard flow-matching action head. Since consecutive decisions share most of their history, prefilling the shared prefix during action execution keeps latency close to a single-frame VLA. SimpleMemVLA sets a new state of the art on four memory benchmarks without cost on general-purpose control. Holding the backbone and training setup fixed, it outperforms retrieval, compression and recurrent-state mechanisms by a wide margin, and causal interventions confirm that the policy genuinely reads its history. Code available at this https URL

</details>

### [16] [Electrospun Fields: 3D Nano-Fiber Material Computation as Design Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05487)
*Wai Lok Wan,Ayah Mahmoud,Sergio Mutis,Avantika Velho,Annie Xing,Behnaz Farahi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出了一种机器人静电纺丝平台与设计方法，可在非平面三维导电几何体上沉积纳米纤维膜，通过六轴机械臂控制发射器沿表面法线运动，解决传统固定发射器在凹面结构上沉积受限和桥接问题，并开源了组装说明与路径生成代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静电纺丝依赖固定发射器和平面接地收集器，仅适用于平面基底：在凹面几何体上，电场屏蔽使纤维无法到达凹陷区域，且材料会跨越高起的特征形成桥接，限制了对三维复杂结构的应用。

Method: 设计了一种集成六轴UR20机械臂的定制末端执行器，工具上安装步进驱动注射泵以独立于方向保持聚合物流量稳定，并将高压直流（最高25kV）直接引至机械臂安装的针头，使机械臂成为可移动发射器，实现对位置、方向、工作距离和移动速度的完全运动学控制。路径规划持续沿表面法线重新定向发射器，从而可进入固定轴系统无法到达的凹面拓扑。通过四种生物相容聚合物（PEO、PVA、角蛋白-PEO、丝素-PEO）建立操作窗口，并利用覆盖几何基元、混合结构和多种晶格的三维打印导电支架系统分类收集器几何对纤维沉积的影响；还演示了可编程接地（选择性激励阵列中的引脚）以实现无需改变物理几何的沉积引导。

Result: 表征了沉积行为：建立了四类聚合物体系的沉积速度、射流稳定性、纤维尺寸、排列和耐久性操作包络；揭示了收集器几何形状（方形、三角形、六边形等）对纤维排列、密度和跨孔桥接的影响；验证了可编程接地可通过电信号控制沉积位置，而无需改动物理结构。所有组装说明和路径生成代码已作为开源仓库发布。

Conclusion: 该方法将静电纺丝从平面限制中解放出来，通过机器人运动学与电场控制结合，实现了对非平面三维导电结构的保形纤维沉积，为复杂几何表面的纳米纤维功能化提供了可行方案，并具备可复现性和扩展性。

Abstract: We present a robotic electrospinning platform and design method for depositing nanofiber membranes onto non-planar, three-dimensional conductive geometries. Conventional electrospinning relies on fixed emitters and planar grounded collectors, which restricts deposition to flat substrates: on concave geometries, field shielding prevents fibers from reaching recessed regions, and material bridges across elevated features instead. We address this with a custom end-effector integrated with a six-axis UR20 arm. The tool carries a localized stepper-driven syringe pump that maintains consistent polymer flow independent of orientation, and routes high-voltage DC (up to 25 kV) directly to a robot-mounted needle, turning the arm into a mobile emitter with full kinematic control over position, orientation, working distance, and traversal velocity. Toolpaths that continuously reorient the emitter along surface normals give access to concave topologies unreachable by fixed-axis systems. We characterize the resulting deposition behavior in two parts. A catalog of four bio-compatible polymer systems (PEO, PVA, keratin-PEO, silk-PEO) establishes the operating envelope, reporting deposition speed, jet stability, fiber size, alignment, and durability for each. A taxonomy of 3D-printed conductive scaffolds spanning geometric primitives, hybrid compositions, and square, triangular, and hexagonal lattices links collector geometry to fiber alignment, density, and cross-void bridging. We also demonstrate programmable grounding, in which selectively energized pins in an array steer deposition without changing physical geometry. Assembly instructions and toolpath-generation code are released as an open-source repository.

</details>

### [17] [PlannerForge: LLM Agents for Scenario-Based Testing of Motion Planners in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08965)
*Yuan Gao,Sebastian Müller,Mattia Piccinini,Marc Kaufeld,Yuchen Zhang,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Qunying Song,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PlannerForge，首个用统一LLM智能体框架覆盖自动驾驶场景测试全流程的工作，支持从场景生成到ADS评估、增强与基准测试，并验证多模型性能与成本优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全验证依赖场景测试，但现有流程是碎片化模块流水线，各工具缺乏交互；LLM智能体虽在感知、规划等子系统中表现良好，却没有工作以统一框架覆盖整个场景测试流程。

Method: PlannerForge是一个LLM智能体框架，扩展了场景生成到ADS评估的全部阶段，并新增ADS增强与ADS基准测试两个LLM增强阶段；使用10种开源/商业LLM、5种提示条件评估所有任务，并支持端到端模块链式执行。

Result: 最佳任务得分0.88-1.00；开源20-35B模型在多数任务上媲美商业API；端到端链保留83%（商业）/78%（开源）种子查询；自然语言生成可执行场景193/200（优于Scenario Factory 2.0的144/200）；属性实现率92-96%；选择准确率92.0%（优于BM25的67.5%）；物理有效编辑≥94%（优于31%）；成本调优在N=400时使规划成功率从50.4%提升至70.2%，碰撞率从19.0%降至8.4%。

Conclusion: PlannerForge验证了统一LLM智能体框架在全流程场景测试中的可行性，开源模型可逼近商业API，且通过成本调优能在无需领域微调情况下显著提升ADS安全性。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of autonomous driving is a critical challenge. Scenario-based testing is a systematic process used to validate Autonomous Driving Systems (ADSs), but it remains a fragmented modular pipeline in which scenario generation, retrieval, modification, ADS execution, and results analysis are performed by separate tools with little interaction. Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise across ADS sub-systems such as perception, planning, and control. However, no prior work covers the whole scenario-based testing pipeline for ADSs with a unified LLM-agent framework. We present PlannerForge, an LLM-agent framework that extends all scenario-based testing stages (from Scenario Generation to ADS Assessment) and adds two further LLM-enhanced stages: ADS Enhancement and ADS Benchmarking. We evaluate PlannerForge with 10 off-the-shelf LLMs across all tasks (Generation, Selection, Modification, Module Routing, Planner Testing, and Enhancement) under 5 prompt conditions. Best-per-task scores range from 0.88 to 1.00, and open-source 20-35B backends match commercial APIs on most tasks. Open-source models such as Qwen3.6:35B match commercial APIs on three of the five tasks. Chaining the modules end-to-end retains 83% / 78% of seed queries (commercial / open). It outperforms Scenario Factory 2.0 (Finkeldei et al., 2025) on natural-language generation (193 vs. 144 executable of 200) and realises 92-96% of requested city, road and vehicle attributes. It outperforms BM25 (Robertson and Zaragoza, 2009) at rank 1 selection (92.0% vs. 67.5%) and From-Words-to-Collisions (Gao et al., 2025) on physically valid edits (>=94% vs. 31%). At N=400, cost-tuning lifts planner success from 50.4% to 70.2% and cuts collisions from 19.0% to 8.4%, without domain-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [18] [ARC-Bench: Closed-Loop Replanning Masks Broken Action Ranking in Frozen JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05461)
*Zhengshu Zhang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 我们提出了ARC-Bench，一个无泄漏、固定候选的协议，用于直接审计冻结的JEPA式潜空间中的动作排序能力。应用在官方JEPA-WM检查点上发现，潜空间距离与真实代价的排序一致性严重且结构性失败；这种缺陷在闭环重规划下被掩盖，降低重规划频率会导致成功率大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无奖励的潜世界模型假设潜空间中的距离接近性可以正确地对动作进行排序（即潜距离排序与真实代价排序一致）。然而这一核心假设从未被直接检验，现有评估依赖闭环成功率，可能掩盖潜在缺陷。

Method: 设计ARC-Bench协议，采用固定候选、无泄漏的评估方式，直接度量冻结的JEPA式目标是否按真实代价正确排名动作。对官方发布的JEPA-WM检查点进行导航和操作任务审计，并通过更换视觉骨干（DINOv2替换为V-JEPA 1/2，含ViT-L/G尺度）、控制来源/训练量/匹配预算/指标循环性来排除琐碎解释，同时通过降低重规划频率检验闭环掩盖效应。

Result: 假设严重失效：操作审计中最高分候选几乎总是次优的，迷宫域中也出现相同排序反转；即使换成视频预训练骨干缺陷依然存在。高频闭环重规划能维持表面成功率，但降低重规划频率后成功率在导航和操作域均崩溃，且被重规划挽救的回合富含首步规划排序严重失败案例，证明闭环成功率系统性高估了潜表示的排序能力。

Conclusion: 必须直接审计潜世界模型的动作排序能力，而不能依赖闭环成功率或其变体（自适应/摊销/重规划）来间接评估。ARC-Bench提供了所需的度量工具，并揭示了重规划掩盖机制，为未来改进潜空间规划器提供了依据。

Abstract: Reward-free latent world models plan by scoring candidate actions with distances in a frozen latent space: an action is preferred if its predicted future embedding lands closer to the goal embedding. This silently assumes that latent closeness is action-rankable, i.e., that ordering candidates by latent distance agrees with ordering them by true cost. We audit this assumption directly. We introduce ARC-Bench, a no-leak, fixed-candidate protocol that measures whether frozen JEPA-style objectives rank candidate actions correctly, and apply it to official released JEPA-WM checkpoints across navigation and manipulation-style control. The assumption fails, severely and structurally: on the official manipulation audits the top-scored candidate is almost always suboptimal, and the same inversion appears in the maze domains. A controlled visual-backbone extension shows that the defect persists when DINOv2 is replaced by video-pretrained V-JEPA 1 and V-JEPA 2 encoders at ViT-L/ViT-G scale. Provenance, undertraining, matched-budget backbone controls, and metric-circularity controls rule out trivial explanations. We then explain why this defect has stayed invisible: closed-loop replanning masks it. When we reduce the planner's replanning frequency, success collapses in both a navigation and a manipulation domain, and the episodes rescued by frequent replanning are enriched for severe first-plan ranking failures in the PointMaze first-plan diagnostic. Closed-loop success rates therefore systematically overstate the rankability of frozen latent representations. ARC-Bench supplies the measurement, and the masking mechanism the explanation, for methods that adapt, amortize, or replan around latent-space planners without directly auditing released JEPA-WM action rankability.

</details>

### [19] [Can People Distinguish Human and AI Agency in Humanoid Teleoperation? A Preliminary Study of Agency Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06434)
*Xiang Li,Koya Dendo,Keigo Minamida,Yuto Nakamura,Per Ola Kristensson,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出“Ghost-in-the-Loop”远程操作框架，允许人类或AI控制机器人的语音、面部表情和手势。初步在线研究表明，参与者在短暂交互中难以区分人类与AI控制，判断主要基于自然度、时间协调性和多模态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人们能否区分人形远程操作中的人类与AI代理，并理解影响代理感知的因素。

Method: 开发Ghost-in-the-Loop远程操作框架，支持人类操作员或AI控制机器人的语音、面部表情和手势，保持统一体现。进行初步在线研究（N=50），让参与者观看由人类或AI生成的交互片段并判断控制来源，同时收集定性反馈。

Result: 参与者经常难以区分两种条件，判断受感知自然度、时间协调性以及语音、面部表情和手势的一致性影响。

Conclusion: 研究为具身人机交流中的代理感知提供了初步见解，并激励未来对混合人机远程呈现系统的研究。

Abstract: Can people distinguish between human and AI agency in humanoid teleoperation? To explore this question, we developed \textit{Ghost-in-the-Loop}, a teleoperation framework that supports both human-operated and AI-generated control of a robot's voice, facial expressions, and gestures while maintaining a consistent embodiment. We conducted a preliminary online study ($N=50$) in which participants viewed short interaction clips generated by either a Human Operator or an AI Control and judged the perceived source of control. Results suggest that participants often struggled to distinguish between the two conditions in brief interactions. Qualitative responses indicate that judgments were primarily influenced by perceived naturalness, temporal coordination, and consistency across speech, facial expression, and gesture. These findings provide initial insights into agency perception in embodied human--AI communication and motivate future investigations of blended human--AI telepresence systems.

</details>

### [20] [SUN: Reaching for Novelty in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08642)
*Wenyan Yang,Arsenii Mustafin,Dominik Baumann,Joni Pajarinen,Simone Parisi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的目标选择框架SUN，通过后继值函数同时考虑目标的新颖性与可达性，以提升强化学习中的探索效率，并在多种环境中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的探索仍是根本挑战。现有目标条件RL方法在选择目标以扩大状态覆盖时，未能同时兼顾新颖性和可达性，常常需要手动权衡、顺序应用或忽略其中一方，导致探索效果受限。

Method: 引入可达性感知的目标选择框架，提出SUccessor-to-Novelty（SUN）指标，由后继值函数导出，用于识别既新颖又可达的目标。证明SUN在极限下能恢复基于计数的探索奖励，可约束短时命中概率，并理论上排除不可达目标。进一步设计自适应目标选择策略，并采用轻量级伪计数以避免传统方法的计算开销，且可与任何离策略RL算法无缝集成。

Result: 在包含不可达/难达状态、不可逆转移、障碍、迷宫及无界空间的标准和新颖环境中，SUN均一致优于当前最先进的方法，验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SUN通过联合建模新颖性与可达性，为RL探索提供了理论上有保证且实用的目标选择机制，可作为通用组件提升多种离策略算法的性能。

Abstract: Exploration in reinforcement learning (RL) remains a fundamental challenge. Recent goal-conditioned RL strategies (which select goals to encourage broader state coverage) have shown promising results, but none scores a goal by novelty and reachability jointly: the two signals are traded off by hand, applied in sequence, or one is neglected outright. In this paper, we introduce a reachability-aware goal-selection framework that explicitly integrates these two aspects, and that can be seamlessly incorporated into any off-policy RL algorithm. To this aim, we propose SUccessor-to-Novelty (SUN), an indicator derived from successor value functions to identify goals that are both novel and reachable. We prove that SUN recovers count-based bonuses in the limit, bounds short-horizon hitting probabilities, and provably rejects unreachable goals. We further present an adaptive goal-selection strategy that leverages these properties, and an accurate yet lightweight pseudocount to avoid the overhead of classic methods. We back up all our claims with thorough benchmarks: SUN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in standard and novel environments with unreachable or hard-to-reach states, irreversible transitions, obstacles, mazes, and unbounded spaces.

</details>


### [21] [Foundation Models for Generalizable Semantic and Goal-Oriented Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07853)
*Boliang Liu,Wint Yi Poe,Riccardo Trivisonno,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FMSGOC 框架，利用视觉-语言基础模型先验和扩散模型，在极低比特率下实现高语义保真度与泛化能力，优于端到端基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G 语义通信面临低比特率下泛化差的问题，现有系统易过拟合训练数据，试图压缩整个信号导致性能急剧下降。

Method: 解耦“发送什么”与“如何重构”：视觉-语言基础模型选择稀疏、目标对齐的语义锚点并传输，预训练扩散模型经掩码补全微调后在接收端重构图像。

Result: CIFAR-10 上达到 0.039 BPP，余弦相似度 0.87-0.90；ImageNet 上未见过输入余弦相似度 0.83-0.86；感知相似度 CIFAR-10/ImageNet 分别为 0.1278/0.1558，均优于强基线。

Conclusion: FMSGOC 通过基础模型先验有效缓解过拟合，提升极低比特率下的语义通信效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Semantic and goal-oriented communication is increasingly studied for 6G, but generalization beyond seen data remains a key weakness under tight rate budgets. Many existing systems overfit their training data and degrade sharply at very low bit rates because they attempt to compress the entire signal. We introduce Foundation Model-Guided Semantic and Goal-Oriented Communication (FMSGOC), a framework that uses broad visual-linguistic Foundation Model priors to mitigate overfitting. It further improves rate efficiency by concentrating bits on sparse, goal-aligned anchors and relying on generative foundation-model priors to reconstruct the masked regions. By decoupling what to send from how to reconstruct, a vision-language foundation model selects and transmits a sparse set of semantic anchors, while a pretrained diffusion model, fine-tuned for masked completion, reconstructs the image at the receiver. In our experiments, FMSGOC reaches 0.039 bits per pixel (BPP), maintains high semantic fidelity (cosine similarity 0.87-0.90 on CIFAR-10), remains robust on previously unseen inputs (0.83-0.86 on ImageNet), and shows good perceptual similarity (0.1278/0.1558, CIFAR-10/ImageNet), outperforming strong end-to-end baselines at lower bit rates.

</details>


### [22] [Noisy-Space Policy Gradient for Diffusion Policies in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06882)
*Mahmoud Selim,Cristina Cipriani,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于噪声空间动作价值函数的扩散策略强化学习框架，通过简单动作空间的价值估计优化噪声潜在变量，避免反向传播通过去噪过程，并在离线RL基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在连续控制中表现强大，但与强化学习的结合在概念和算法上存在挑战，现有方法难以有效整合扩散模型与RL目标。

Method: 引入噪声空间动作价值函数（Q-function），通过去噪过程下执行动作的分布为扩散潜在变量赋值。推导出噪声空间策略梯度（NSPG），仅利用干净动作空间的价值估计优化噪声潜在变量。进一步构建KL正则化的噪声潜在策略改进，目标退化为扩散兼容的回归形式，避免对去噪过程反向传播。

Result: 在基于状态输入的D4RL基准和基于视觉的OGBench任务上，所提出的噪声空间目标为离线强化学习中的扩散策略训练提供了有原则且有效的依据。

Conclusion: 噪声空间目标是一种训练扩散策略的可行且高效方法，弥合了扩散模型与强化学习之间的差距，并为后续研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Diffusion policies offer a powerful and expressive parameterization for continuous control. Yet, their integration with reinforcement learning remains conceptually and algorithmically challenging. In this work, we address this gap by introducing a noisy-space action-value (Q-)function that assigns values to diffusion latents through the distribution of executed actions induced by the denoising process. We show that this construction admits a precise semantic interpretation and derive a noisy-space policy gradient (NSPG) that optimizes noisy latents using only clean action-space value estimates. Building on this result, we formulate a KL-regularized policy improvement over noisy latents and show that the resulting objective admits a diffusion-compatible regression form, avoiding backpropagation through the denoising process. Empirical results on state-based D4RL benchmarks and vision-based OGBench tasks demonstrate that the proposed noisy-space objective provides a principled and effective basis for training diffusion policies in offline reinforcement learning. Project webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [23] [EgoNeMo: Transferable Map of Pedestrian Dynamics via Egocentric LiDAR Scan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06195)
*Azusa Sawada,Allan Wang,Hideo Saito,Aaron Steinfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可迁移的运动动态图（MoD）框架，仅利用单次3D LiDAR点云即可在未知环境中重建行人运动分布，克服传统MoD依赖逐地点轨迹积累的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoD方法虽然对行人轨迹预测和机器人导航至关重要，但具有场地特异性，需要在每个新位置耗费大量轨迹数据，难以泛化到未知环境。

Method: 采用神经隐式建模训练连续的LiDAR-based MoD估计器；提出位置平衡采样策略缓解轨迹数据稀疏和时间偏差；设计多任务学习架构联合预测运动分布与空间频率得分图，并引入可见性感知损失补偿不完整观测。

Result: 在高度稀疏训练数据下，该方法能仅凭单次瞬时LiDAR扫描有效重建未知位置的运动图，并提升下游轨迹预测的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了MoD的跨环境泛化，减少了数据依赖，增强了在未知场景中的实用性与下游任务性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a transferable Map of Dynamics (MoD) framework that generalizes to unknown environments using only egocentric 3D LiDAR point clouds to overcome the long-standing limitation of traditional MoD methods. While MoDs are essential for encoding human motion characteristics to enable accurate pedestrian trajectory prediction or safe robot navigation, traditional approaches suffer from site-specificity, requiring exhaustive trajectory accumulation at every new location. Extending recent advances in neural implicit modeling, our framework trains a continuous, LiDAR-based MoD estimator across diverse environments. To mitigate the inherent sparsity and temporal bias of real-world trajectory data, we introduce a position-balanced sampling strategy and a multi-task learning architecture that jointly predicts motion distributions and a spatial frequency score map. The latter is further augmented by visibility-aware losses to compensate for incomplete observation data. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively reconstructs underlying motion maps even in unknown locations from a single instantaneous LiDAR scan, despite highly sparse training data. Finally, we show that our improvements enhance the reliability of downstream trajectory prediction.

</details>

### [24] [Deception in Reach-Avoid Game with Unknown Heterogeneous Attackers Speed Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06953)
*Xiangkai Wu,Shaolin Tan,Wei Wang,Zhen Han*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究信息不对称下两攻一守的 reach-avoid 博弈，攻击者速度异质且私有，提出临界速度对框架分析防守者猜测行为，证明慢速欺骗策略可误导防守者取得优于完全信息博弈的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注同质速度不确定性的攻防博弈，而现实中的攻击者速度往往是异质的，且私有信息导致能力配置不确定性，进而产生捕获顺序歧义。为了刻画这种更一般的信息不对称场景，需要解决异质速度下防守者的最优响应和攻击者的信息利用问题。

Method: 建立临界速度对框架，用于刻画不同能力配置何时导致不同的最优捕获顺序，并据此分析防守者的猜测行为、设计攻击者的信息限制策略。通过理论推导和数值可视化验证。

Result: 在特定初始条件下，攻击者可通过慢速欺骗策略诱使防守者做出次优决策，从而获得优于完全信息博弈的收益；数值结果揭示此类困境条件广泛存在。

Conclusion: 异质速度的私有信息赋予攻击者策略优势，信息不对称性可被利用来提升攻击方收益，为攻防博弈中的信息设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: This letter investigates a reach-avoid game involving two Attackers and one Defender, where the Attackers aim to maximize the number reaching the target region while the Defender seeks to minimize it. In contrast to conventional complete information formulations, we consider an information asymmetry scenario where the Attackers' heterogeneous maximum speeds are privately known but publicly disclosed to lie within continuous ranges. Existing studies on uncertain speeds, however, have primarily focused on homogeneous settings, whereas heterogeneity extends the uncertainty from a common capability level to the relative capability configuration of the Attackers. To address the resulting capture-order ambiguity over infinitely many possible speed combinations, we establish a critical speed pair framework that characterizes when different capability configurations induce different optimal capture orders, and enables the analysis of the Defender's guessing behavior and the design of information-limiting strategies for the Attackers. We demonstrate that under certain initial conditions, the Attackers can mislead the Defender into making suboptimal decisions through a slow-speed deception strategy, achieving superior payoffs compared to the complete information game. Numerical visualizations reveal the widespread occurrence of such dilemma conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [25] [Observability Analysis of Joint Steering and Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05498)
*Subodh Mishra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对平面自行车模型车辆，联合估计车辆位姿、平面LiDAR外参和转向角偏差时，采用李导数非线性可观测性分析，发现静止、直线、恒曲率及直线加圆弧组合运动下的不可观测方向，并证明直线与曲线组合可实现七状态系统的局部弱完全可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为标定轨迹的选择提供理论基础，确保轨迹能够充分激励转向参数和传感器外参，避免退化导致估计失败。

Method: 使用基于李导数的非线性可观测性分析方法，构建可观测性矩阵并分析其零空间，考察静止、直线、恒曲率及直线-圆弧组合等不同运动模式。

Result: 单独静止或单一运动基元均存在不可观测方向；而直线与圆弧组合运动能够消除所有已识别的退化方向，实现系统局部弱完全可观测。

Conclusion: 该分析为选择同时激发转向和外部参数的可观测标定轨迹提供了理论基础，具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: This technical report studies the local weak observability of a planar bicycle-model vehicle when vehicle pose, planar LiDAR extrinsic calibration, and steering-angle bias are estimated jointly. A Lie-derivative-based nonlinear observability analysis is used to examine stationary, straight-line, constant-curvature, and combined straight-plus-arc motion. The resulting observability matrices and nullspaces describe how pose, LiDAR translation and yaw offsets, and steering bias become coupled under different motion primitives. Stationary motion and individual motion primitives retain unobservable directions, whereas the combination of straight and curved motion removes the identified degeneracies and yields full local weak observability of the seven-state system. The analysis provides a theoretical basis for selecting calibration trajectories that sufficiently excite both steering and sensor-extrinsic parameters.

</details>


### [26] [ComVLA: Communication-Aware Split Inference for VLA Models in 6G-Connected Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07838)
*Boliang Liu,Wint Yi Poe,Jingyun Di,Riccardo Trivisonno,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ComVLA框架，利用语言指导在无线信道上自适应调整VLA模型的视觉token预算，实现低计算量、低延迟和信道容量约束下的语义通信，用于6G连接机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型过大无法在机器人上运行，需卸载到云端，但无线链路限制了传感数据传输。现有语义通信需要信道特定重训练，而token剪枝忽略了信道状态。关键洞察是语言中的密集语义信息能指示哪些视觉token重要，因此可利用语言指导适配token预算到信道容量。

Method: ComVLA框架利用语言指令选择对任务重要的视觉token，根据信道容量动态调整token数量，从而在无线传输中压缩数据。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，传输32个token而非512个，推理计算减少74%，推理延迟降低22%（相比OpenVLA-OFT），平均任务成功率下降1.5个百分点（95.4% vs 96.9%），并在Rayleigh和Rician衰落信道下保持容量预算约束。

Conclusion: 结果表明，协同设计VLA推理和无线通信是6G连接机器人的可行方向。

Abstract: Connected robotics is an emerging 6G application where mobile robots follow natural-language instructions to manipulate physical objects. The Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models that enable this are too large to run on the robot; a common trend is to offload inference to the cloud. The wireless link, however, limits how much sensing data the edge can transmit per control step. Two recent lines address this constraint: semantic communication codecs compress sensor data but require channel-specific retraining, and VLA token pruners select tokens from image but ignore the channel. Our insight is that the dense semantic information contained in the language already indicates which visual tokens matter. We propose ComVLA, a framework that uses this language guidance to adapt the VLA token budget to the channel capacity. Transmitting 32 tokens instead of 512 on the LIBERO benchmark, ComVLA cuts inference compute by 74% and inference latency by 22% versus the original OpenVLA-OFT baseline, at a cost of 1.5 pp in average task success (95.4% vs. 96.9%), and it stays within the capacity budget under Rayleigh and Rician fading. These results demonstrate that co-designing VLA inference and wireless communication is a practical direction for 6G-connected robotics.

</details>


### [27] [Dex-X: Learning Visual-Tactile Dexterous Manipulation From Human Videos with Simulated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07747)
*Ruoqu Chen,Feixiang Ruan,Liu Cao,Zihao Wang,Botian Xu,Shiqin Tong,Jiajun Liu,Mingzhi Pei,Chenyu Zhang,Wanli Xing,Kaifeng Zhang,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEX-X 提出了一种从人体视频中学习可部署的视觉-触觉灵巧操作策略的框架，通过仿真重建手物交互并提供触觉监督，实现零样本仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体视频是灵巧操作行为的丰富来源，但缺乏触觉信息，而触觉对于接触丰富的交互至关重要。因此需要解决如何在没有机器人端数据收集的情况下，从人体视频演示中学习可部署的视觉-触觉策略的问题。

Method: DEX-X 利用仿真作为触觉补全引擎，从单目人体演示中重建手物交互，物理接触动力学提供原始视频缺失的触觉监督。基于恢复的触觉信息训练视觉-触觉策略，并蒸馏为基于点云观测和触觉感知的可部署策略。

Result: 教师策略在仿真中六类任务平均成功率65.9%，蒸馏后的策略在真实世界中立方体抓取成功率达93%，挑战性桌面清洁任务达53%。在物体抓取任务中观察到对未知物体几何形状的零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 模拟交互是连接人体视频与可部署灵巧操作策略的关键桥梁，提供可扩展的机器人技能学习所需的物理监督，支持从互联网规模人体视频数据中学习。

Abstract: Human videos are an abundant source of dexterous manipulation behaviors, but they lack tactile information that is crucial for contact-rich interaction. This raises a fundamental question: can robots learn deployable visual-tactile dexterous manipulation policies from human video demonstrations without robot-side data collection? We present DEX-X, a framework for learning visual-tactile dexterous manipulation from human videos through simulation. Our key insight is that simulation can serve as a tactile completion engine. Given monocular human demonstrations, DEX-X reconstructs hand-object interactions in simulation, where physically grounded contact dynamics provide tactile supervision unavailable in the original videos. Leveraging this recovered tactile information, we train visual-tactile dexterous manipulation policies and distill them into deployable policies operating on point-cloud observations and tactile sensing. We demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a dexterous hand-arm platform across diverse grasping and contact-rich tool-use tasks. The teacher policy achieves 65.9% average success across six task categories in simulation, while the distilled visual-tactile policy achieves 93% success on real-world cube picking and 53% on the challenging table-cleaning task. Zero-shot generalization to unseen object geometries is also observed on object-picking tasks. Our results suggest that simulated interaction is a key bridge between human videos and deployable dexterous manipulation policies, providing the missing physical supervision needed for scalable robot skill learning from Internet-scale human video data.

</details>


### [28] [ICI-VLA: In-Context Imitation with Spatiotemporally Aligned Demonstrations for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07581)
*Songhua Yang,Ziyu Liu,Xuetao Li,Ruqi Xiao,Kangxin Zhu,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ICI-VLA框架，通过检索并利用时空对齐的上下文演示（in-context demonstrations），使文本生成式VLA策略在推理时无需梯度更新即可实现少样本测试时自适应，在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0及物理任务上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的VLA策略需要通过额外梯度更新来适应新操作场景，限制了在任务数据或算力稀缺时的快速部署。

Method: ICI-VLA保留原生文本生成接口，仅离线训练，推理时冻结策略；将长轨迹分解为短且有语义标签的示例，用DTW挖掘正样本训练RD-Encoder，使检索到的演示与当前子任务阶段和几何对齐；并引入Target Action Masking上下文损坏目标，减少直接复制动作、增强对当前观测的依赖。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率97.7%，在RoboTwin 2.0上平均60.4%，比此前最高平均基线高19.3个百分点；跨4个物理任务达83.2%。

Conclusion: 固定VLA策略可在测试时通过条件化于时空对齐的演示获得显著收益，为少样本快速部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are commonly adapted to new manipulation settings through additional gradient updates, which limits rapid deployment when task-specific data or compute is scarce. We present ICI-VLA, a training and retrieval framework that equips a text-action VLM with few-shot test-time adaptation through in-context demonstrations. Unlike mainstream VLA designs based on action-specific multimodal fusion, ICI-VLA retains the native text-generation interface. ICI-VLA updates its parameters only during offline training; at inference, the policy remains fixed and conditions action generation on retrieved micro-demonstrations. The framework decomposes long trajectories into short, semantically labeled examples and trains an RD-Encoder with positives mined by Dynamic Time Warping (DTW), aligning the retrieved context with the phase and geometry of the current subtask. We further introduce Target Action Masking, a context-corruption objective designed to reduce direct action copying and increase reliance on the current observation. ICI-VLA reaches average success rates of 97.7% on LIBERO and 60.4% on RoboTwin 2.0, exceeding the highest reported baseline average on RoboTwin 2.0 by 19.3 percentage points. It also achieves 83.2% across four physical tasks. These results indicate that a fixed VLA policy can benefit from conditioning on spatiotemporally aligned demonstrations at test time.

</details>


### [29] [Anti-Gravity Walking by a Flying Humanoid Robot via Thrust-Rate Input Whole-Body Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07544)
*Kazuki Sugihara,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于反重力双足行走的实时全身模型预测控制（MPC）框架，通过使用推力变化率作为控制输入并引入足底接触力下界，实现了飞行人形机器人在天花板等反重力环境中的稳定行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行人形机器人需要在地面飞行和行走之外，具备在复杂三维空间（如天花板、反重力环境）中移动的能力，以扩展其应用范围。然而，行走过程中接触切换引起的动态结构不连续变化可能导致推力尖峰，进而造成控制不稳定。

Method: 1. 将推力变化率（thrust-rate）作为控制输入，构建全身MPC，确保接触切换时推力轨迹的连续性，同时保留最优控制问题的稀疏结构以实现快速计算。2. 针对反重力环境中缺乏自然支撑力的问题，引入足底法向接触力下界，并在双支撑相中平滑转移这些下界。

Result: 通过仿真和硬件实验验证了所提框架的有效性，实现了飞行人形机器人的反重力行走。据作者所知，这是首次在可变形空中机器人上演示多接触全身MPC，并实现超越地面的行走。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了所设计的MPC框架能够有效应对反重力行走中的控制挑战，为飞行人形机器人在复杂三维环境中的移动提供了可行方案，并展示了其实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Flying humanoids are expected to perform tasks in diverse environments, while their existing locomotion is mainly limited to aerial flight and ground walking. The capability to move in complex three-dimensional space can greatly expand their application range. For such walking motion on ceilings and similar anti-gravity environments, whole-body MPC is effective. However, the discontinuous changes in dynamic structure accompanying contact switching during walking can induce thrust spikes, resulting in control instability. Therefore, in this work, we propose and implement a real-time whole-body MPC framework for anti-gravity bipedal walking. First, we formulate whole-body MPC using the time derivative of thrust, namely thrust-rate, as the control input. This formulation guarantees continuity of the thrust trajectory during contact switching while preserving the sparse structure of the optimal control problem for fast computation. Second, we address the lack of natural support forces in anti-gravity environments. We introduce lower bounds on the foot-normal component of the contact force, and smoothly transfer them during the doublesupport phase. Finally, we implement the proposed framework and demonstrate anti-gravity walking by a flying humanoid through simulation and a hardware experiment. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of multi-contact whole-body MPC for a transformable aerial robot and walking by a flying humanoid beyond the ground.

</details>


### [30] [Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Contact-Rich Assembly via Proprioception-Anchored Cross-Modal Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07534)
*Yuhan Wang,Yurou Chen,Hongye Jiang,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACE方法，利用本体感觉作为锚点，通过预测本体感觉状态转换来训练跨模态编码器，无需真实世界微调即可实现接触丰富的装配任务的高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的装配任务需要亚毫米精度和力/力矩感知，模拟到现实的差距（视觉、接触动力学、F/T测量）常限制策略迁移，但本体感觉在模拟和硬件间较为一致。

Method: PACE（Proprioception-Anchored Cross-Modal Encoder）通过预测本体感觉状态转换来监督时间视觉和F/T表示，促使编码器抑制与关节运动无关的静态域特定因素（如光照、纹理、传感器偏差），保留任务相关的运动线索。

Result: 在四个接触丰富的装配任务中，PACE在真实世界的平均成功率达93.3%，模拟到现实的性能仅下降2.7个百分点，且对扰动保持鲁棒，优于基于位姿和 Learned-Fusion 的基线。

Conclusion: PACE证明了利用本体感觉一致性可以显著提高策略迁移的鲁棒性和准确性，无需真实世界微调或目标位姿跟踪，为接触丰富的装配任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich assembly remains challenging because it requires submillimeter spatial accuracy and reliable interpretation of forces during sustained contact. Although simulation-based reinforcement learning offers a scalable training paradigm, discrepancies in visual observations, contact dynamics, and force/torque (F/T) measurements often limit policy transfer. We observe that proprioception is comparatively consistent across domains because calibrated joint positions and consistently computed joint velocities align closely between simulation and hardware. Based on this observation, we present PACE (Proprioception-Anchored Cross-Modal Encoder), which supervises temporal visual and F/T representations by predicting proprioceptive state transitions. Static domain-specific factors, including lighting, texture, and sensor bias, contain little information about joint motion; the proposed objective therefore encourages the encoder to suppress these factors while retaining task-relevant motion cues. Policies trained on frozen PACE features are deployed on hardware without real-world fine-tuning or object-pose tracking. Across four contact-rich assembly tasks, PACE attains an average real-world success rate of 93.3\% and only a 2.7-percentage-point sim-to-real drop, meanwhile remaining robust to perturbations that substantially degrade pose-based and learned-fusion baselines.

</details>


### [31] [PhysReal: Learning Real-World Deformable Object Physics via Hybrid Constitutive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07532)
*Yinan Deng,Jianqiao Song,Yisi Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Jiahui Wang,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhysReal，一种视频驱动的框架，通过集成空间变化的混合专家-神经本构模型与可微分MPM模拟器和3DGS渲染器，从稀疏视觉观测中学习真实可变形物体的物理动态，实现高精度动态重建和未来状态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测中学习物理合理的动态对交互式世界模型和具身智能体至关重要。然而，真实可变形物体的动力学常源于复杂、空间异质的材料响应，使得建模极具挑战。

Method: PhysReal整合了空间变化的混合专家-神经本构模型、可微分MPM模拟器和3DGS渲染器。解析专家模型提供可解释的物理先验，神经本构残差捕捉超出预定义公式的材料响应。通过空间分布块参数化本构场，实现局部材料变化的连续表示。采用渐进式课程学习，顺序优化全局材料属性、空间变化局部参数和神经残差，并辅以运动与掩码监督。

Result: 在多种可变形物体交互上的大量实验表明，PhysReal在动态重建和未来状态预测方面取得了优越性能，并展现出在下游机器人应用中的强大潜力。

Conclusion: PhysReal通过结合物理先验与神经残差及渐进优化策略，有效解决了真实可变形物体物理建模难题，为视频驱动的物理学习提供了新途径，并支持机器人应用。

Abstract: Learning physically plausible dynamics from visual observations is essential for interactive world models and embodied agents. However, modeling real-world deformable objects remains challenging because their dynamics often arise from complex, spatially heterogeneous material responses. To address this challenge, we propose PhysReal, a video-driven framework for learning and simulating the underlying physics of real deformable objects. PhysReal integrates a spatially varying hybrid expert-neural constitutive model with a differentiable MPM simulator and 3DGS renderer. Analytical expert models provide interpretable physical priors, while neural constitutive residuals capture material responses beyond predefined formulations. Spatially distributed patches parameterize the constitutive field, enabling a continuous representation of local material variations. To organize the identification of this model from sparse visual observations, we adopt a progressive curriculum that sequentially optimizes global material properties, spatially varying local parameters, and neural constitutive residuals, together with complementary motion and mask supervision. Extensive experiments on diverse deformable-object interactions demonstrate that PhysReal achieves superior performance in dynamic reconstruction and future-state prediction, while showing strong potential for downstream robotic applications.

</details>


### [32] [P$^2$Calib: Utilizing Pattern Priors for LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07516)
*Xiangcheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出P²Calib，利用目标板的CAD模型先验（孔半径和矩形布局）改进LiDAR-相机外部标定中四孔法的孔心提取，显著降低配准残差和重投影误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于四孔靶标的LiDAR-相机外部标定中，LiDAR侧孔心提取受稀疏角覆盖和混合像素干扰，成为精度瓶颈。

Method: 引入已知孔半径作为拟合约束以避免稀疏角下的中心漂移，并利用四孔刚性矩形布局作为全局一致性约束修正残余误差，集成到交互式标定工具中。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，联合配准残差较基线降低90%和82%，留出重投影误差降低96%和77%。

Conclusion: P²Calib有效提升标定精度，代码和数据将公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Target-based LiDAR-camera extrinsic calibration is a prerequisite for multi-sensor fusion in robotics. However, in the widely adopted four-hole pipeline, calibration accuracy is bottlenecked by LiDAR-side hole-center extraction, which suffers from sparse angular coverage and mixed-pixel corruption. This paper presents P$^2$Calib, which exploits pattern priors, geometric constraints specified by the CAD model of the target board, to improve calibration accuracy. First, we incorporate the known hole radius as a fitting constraint to prevent center estimates from degrading under sparse angular coverage. Building on the improved hole estimates, we further enforce the rigid rectangular layout of the four holes as a global consistency constraint to correct residual errors across holes. Both priors are integrated into an interactive calibration tool that provides a complete extrinsic calibration pipeline. Experiments on simulated and real datasets show that P$^2$Calib lowers the joint registration residual by 90\% and 82\% and the held-out reprojection error by 96\% and 77\% over the baseline. Code, this https URL , and data will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [33] [Generation of Vectorized Maps Beyond Vehicle View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07511)
*Clara Gomez,Alberto Jaenal,Antonio Artuñedo,Jorge Godoy,Jorge Villagra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出超越视野的矢量化地图生成问题，并设计BeyondFormer方法及数据集，证明学习方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高清地图构建成本高，在线重建受限于传感器视野，导致车辆前方地图范围不足以支持安全规划。

Method: 提出BeyondFormer，利用车载传感器感知到的视野内矢量化地图，生成合理的超出视野的地图延续，并构建专门数据集进行训练与评估。

Result: 实验显示BeyondFormer在多种场景下性能稳定，验证了基于学习的方法用于地图预测的可行性。

Conclusion: 证明了任务可行性，讨论了方法局限并指出未来研究方向，为扩展到更复杂驾驶条件提供基础。

Abstract: Autonomous driving relies on High Definition (HD) maps for safe navigation. Traditional HD maps construction is costly in hardware, data and human resources, which together with its update limitations hinders scalability. Recent works have proposed online alternatives for HD vectorized mapping from onboard sensors. However, sensor field of view is limited, and the range of the reconstructed maps ahead of the vehicle is insufficient for safe planning. This paper aims to address this limitation by proposing the novel beyond-view vectorized map generation problem: given vectorized maps of the area sensed by the vehicle (in-view), to generate plausible map continuations. To experimentally assess its feasibility, we propose BeyondFormer, which, to the best of out knowledge, is the first work designed towards beyond-view map generation. Given the novelty of the problem, we generate the first dataset specifically designed for it and evaluate the proposed approach. The results demonstrate consistent performance across diverse scenarios, establishing learning-based methods as a promising direction for map forecasting in autonomous driving. Beyond demonstrating the feasibility of the task, we provide an extensive discussion of the method's limitations and identify key future research directions for scaling it to more complex driving conditions. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [34] [CosmoH2G: A Hand-to-Gripper Transfer Dataset and Baseline Method for Object Manipulation with Complex Spatial Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07498)
*Hongxiang Zhao,Mutian Xu,Zeyu Jin,Yiming Hao,Shuguang Cui,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从人手演示到机器人夹具的隐式数据驱动迁移方法，通过大规模配对数据集和两阶段框架，实现复杂空间操作（如旋转、翻转）的稳定精确迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手到夹具迁移方法局限于简单平面任务，难以处理复杂空间轨迹（涉及旋转或翻转）。

Method: 构建可扩展采集流程，使用手持夹具采集6,189个高空间复杂度配对演示；提出两阶段框架：先预测稀疏夹具关键帧，再生成完整连续动作序列；对平移采用后优化以减少累积漂移。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，该方法在复杂空间操作上显著优于传统基线，实现稳定精确的迁移。

Conclusion: 验证了隐式数据驱动结合关键帧预测与平移后优化可有效处理复杂空间手到夹具迁移。

Abstract: Transferring human hand demonstrations to robotic grippers has recently emerged as a cost-effective solution for robot learning. However, existing methods are largely confined to simple, planar tasks and fail to handle complex spatial movements (e.g., intricate trajectories involving rotations or flips) that are essential for robot manipulation. Motivated by this gap, we adopt an implicit, data-driven approach guided by fine-grained hand-pose motions. To this end, we introduce a scalable acquisition pipeline to collect hand-gripper paired demonstrations, governed by a rigorous protocol that prioritizes motion complexity and leverages a handheld gripper for seamless action mimicry. This yields a large-scale paired dataset comprising 6,189 episodes across 1,254 unique objects, exhibiting significantly higher spatial complexity than existing benchmarks. However, learning such complex mappings remains challenging. We observe that naive end-to-end generation of full gripper pose sequences is insufficient, as minor trajectory deviations compound rapidly under intricate dynamics. To address this, we propose a two-stage framework: Stage I predicts sparse gripper keyframes (initial and terminal) to simplify the mapping objective, while Stage II generates the full continuous action sequence conditioned on these keyframes. Furthermore, to mitigate cumulative drift, we keep the gripper's orientation being learned while post-optimizing its translation based on the grasping heuristic and kinematic consistency. In both simulation and real-robot experiments, our framework enables stable and precise hand-to-gripper transfer of complex spatial manipulations, significantly outperforming traditional baselines. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [35] [Functional-SLAM: Interaction-Aware Mapping with Online Functional Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07497)
*Xinggang Hu,Chenyangguang Zhang,Zihan Zhu,Ruida Zhang,Xiangkui Zhang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Functional-SLAM，首个在线递归维护功能场景图的SLAM框架，利用锚点关键帧几何、功能上下文约束与功能拓扑辅助回环，实现实时构建稳定功能地图并提升位姿精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM系统缺乏细粒度机器人交互所需的功能关系建模，功能3D场景图虽能表示关系但依赖离线重建，无法用于实时探索和交互。

Method: 构建在线SLAM状态的功能场景图，结合锚点关键帧几何与功能上下文约束持久维护节点，通过时序关系累积多帧证据提交稳定功能边，并在重复外观或退化纹理场景中补充功能性拓扑作为回环候选。

Result: 实验表明Functional-SLAM能在线高效构建稳定的功能地图，相比离线方法大幅提升运行时间，保持高竞争力精度，并通过功能拓扑辅助回环进一步提升位姿估计精度。

Conclusion: 将功能关系建图融入在线SLAM是可行且有效的，能在满足实时性的同时保持高精度，有利于支持机器人的细粒度交互任务。

Abstract: Existing SLAM systems lack modeling of the functional relations required for fine-grained robotic interaction. Functional 3D scene graphs can represent relations between objects and interaction elements, but existing methods rely on offline reconstruction, making them inadequate for real-time interaction in real-world exploration. To address this limitation, we propose Functional-SLAM, the first framework that continuously and recursively maintains a functional scene graph as an online SLAM state. The framework combines anchor-keyframe geometry with functional-context constraints for persistent node maintenance, accumulates multi-frame evidence through temporal relations to commit stable functional edges, and supplements visual loop-closure candidates with functional topology in scenes with repetitive appearance or degraded texture. Experiments show that Functional-SLAM efficiently constructs stable functional maps online, substantially improving runtime over offline methods while maintaining highly competitive accuracy. Compared with peer SLAM systems, it further improves pose estimation accuracy through functional-topology-assisted loop closure. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [36] [Wearable Multimodal Human-Machine Interface for Integrated Hand Intentions Decoding in Dynamic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07495)
*Jiaxuan Li,Yinshi Wu,Xiao Zhang,Hongyu Wang,Renzhen Le,Zhenzhi Ying,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成高密度表面肌电（sEMG）与惯性测量单元（IMU）的可穿戴多意图解码人机接口（MI-DHMI），在非约束上肢运动下同时实现高精度手势识别、抓握力估计和手部姿态解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作抓取接口难以同时满足可穿戴性与多意图解码的多样性，尤其在光学挑战性环境下，需要既能佩戴又能精确解码手部意图（手势、抓握力、手姿态）的接口，但现有方案常顾此失彼。

Method: 设计MI-DHMI，结合高吞吐量sEMG传感器与手戴/前臂IMU，提出统一的多模态深度学习框架，通过低噪声硬件设计及注意力机制筛选与手指运动真正相关的sEMG成分，抑制非约束上肢运动引起的信号变异性，提升解码鲁棒性。

Result: 在非约束腕/前臂运动下，手势识别准确率>97%，抓握力估计R²=0.95，手姿态解码与真实一致，优于基线设备与算法；消融实验验证框架有效性；两项在线实验（倒水、抓取）证实高稳定性任务中的优越表现。

Conclusion: 该接口为全可穿戴多意图解码系统提供了新方案，能有效支撑普适遥操作，推动人机交互研究发展。

Abstract: Under ubiquitous teleoperation environments with optically challenging conditions, an interface for tele-operated grasping that combines wearability with precise decoding of hand intentions (hand pose, gestures, and grasping force) is essential. Yet, existing interfaces often fall short in meeting these demands, compromising either the diversity of multiple intentions decoding or wearability. To address this, we developed a novel Multiple Intentions Decoding Human-Machine Interface (MI-DHMI) that integrates high-throughput surface electromyography (sEMG) sensors with hand-mounted and forearm-mounted inertial measurement units (IMUs). The developed interface is supported by a unified framework for simultaneous multiple intentions decoding. By employing multimodal deep learning and hardware design with a low noise floor, the decoding framework selectively focuses on the sEMG components that are genuinely associated with finger movements. This effectively reduces decoding errors caused by sEMG variability during unconstrained upper-limb motions, thereby significantly enhancing robustness. Even under unconstrained wrist and forearm motion, the interface achieves a gesture recognition accuracy exceeding 97%, grasping force estimation with $R^2 = 0.95$, and hand pose decoding consistent with the actual hand pose, outperforming baseline devices and algorithms. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed decoding framework. Finally, two online experiments were conducted to validate the device, demonstrating its superior performance in high-stability tasks, including a pouring task and object grasping. The developed interface provides a new solution of a fully wearable, multiple intentions decoding system, offering effective support for ubiquitous teleoperation and contributing to the advancement of human-machine interaction research.

</details>


### [37] [Measuring Language Transfer in Robot Policies: Adding Greek to a Cosmos3 Vision-Language-Action Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07470)
*Ayoub Kirouane,Georgios Giaples,Christos Petrocheilos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究将希腊语添加到开源视觉-语言-动作（VLA）机器人模型中的可行性。仅使用机器改写的指令翻译，不改变架构。核心发现是评估指标比翻译本身更具挑战性：传统指标（如颜色直方图）和单任务基准会误导结论。在判别性90任务套件上，希腊语专用训练相对控制组最多提升2.7分，而双语训练稳定提升6.7-7.1分，达到英语性能的五分之二。模型会过拟合翻译者的措辞，使用七种改写可减半该惩罚。语言适应的世界模型热启动和文本塔解冻均降低性能。结论提出两个实用要求：在信任指标前构建“保证空对照组”（guaranteed null），以及在低资源语言结果中跨随机种子重复实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型主要在英语上训练和评估，而大多数语言没有机器人演示语料。本文探索将希腊语加入开源VLA模型，仅通过机器改写指令，不改变架构，以评估低资源语言本地化的可行性。

Method: 使用机器改写的希腊语指令进行训练，不修改模型架构。构建判别性九十任务套件，每个机械臂（arm）使用三种随机种子。设置实验：多语言文本塔（无希腊语演示）、希腊语专用训练、双语训练、控制组（错误指令）。同时测试七种指令改写短语的影响，以及热启动自语言适应的世界模型和解冻文本塔的变体。

Result: 多语言文本塔在无希腊语演示时性能处于错误指令基线水平；希腊语专用训练最高超出控制组2.7分；双语训练超出控制组6.7-7.1分，约为英语性能的五分之二。模型对翻译者的措辞过拟合，七种改写训练可减少约一半过拟合惩罚。语言适应的世界模型热启动和解冻文本塔均导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 低资源机器人策略本地化需要两个实用条件：一是在信任任何指标前，先构建一个“保证空对照组”（如故意错误的指令）来验证指标有效性；二是跨随机种子重复低资源语言实验，因为单次运行受种子变异影响极大。这些结果强调了鲁棒评估协议的重要性。

Abstract: Robot foundation models are trained and evaluated predominantly in English, and robot demonstration corpora do not exist for most languages.
