We study the addition of Greek to an open vision-language-action stack using only machine-rephrased instructions and no architecture changes. The main challenge is measurement rather than translation. Several plausible instruments produce false conclusions: a color-histogram metric rewards noise, a single-goal benchmark scores 84.6% under correct Greek and 82.6% under deliberately wrong instructions, training loss fails to predict Greek success, and single-run comparisons are dominated by seed variation. On a discriminative ninety-task suite with three seeds per arm, a multilingual text tower without Greek demonstrations remains at its wrong-instruction floor, while Greek-only training exceeds its control by at most 2.7 points. Bilingual training yields a consistent 6.7-7.1 point margin over its control and reaches about two fifths of English performance. The policy also overfits the translator's phrasing; training on seven phrasings per task approximately halves this penalty. Warm-starting from a language-adapted world model and unfreezing the text tower both degrade performance. The results support two practical requirements for low-resource robot-policy localization: build a guaranteed null before trusting a metric, and replicate low-resource-language results across seeds.

</details>


### [38] [SMaRT-Tug: Structured Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Physics-Based Tugboat-Barge Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07445)
*Junkai Lu,Jiadong Zhao,Jiacheng Zhang,Wenqi Zhao,Hao Gen Chia,Qun Shen Png,Germaine Ee,Chengyang He,Yifeng Zhang,Nathanael Tan,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于物理的GPU加速仿真与学习框架，用于协同拖船操纵。在该仿真器中训练了带结构化控制先验的分散式MAPPO策略，在直线航行、转向和减速任务上均优于PID和集中式PPO基线，并实现了对更恶劣海况、高级机动以及更多拖船数量的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化拖船操作对港口物流和船舶操纵至关重要，但协同推动大型船舶面临耦合水动力学、低阻力、强环境扰动、欠驱动驳船动力学和接触丰富的交互等挑战。传统控制方法依赖简化模型和固定配置，适应性有限；学习方法则缺乏可扩展且物理逼真的训练环境。

Method: 构建了一个物理模拟器，包含自定义浮力模型、波浪建模和水动力阻力，支持大规模多智能体训练。在该模拟器中训练解码的MAPPO策略，并引入结构化控制先验以提升训练稳定性并保持可行的推动配置。

Result: 在直线过境、转向和减速任务中，分散式学习框架比PID控制器和集中式PPO基线更可靠、更精确。此外，尽管仅用两个智能体训练，但实现了对更严峻海况、高级机动的零样本泛化，以及对三或四艘拖船规模的零样本扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了结合物理仿真与结构化先验的分散式多智能体强化学习能够有效处理协同拖船操纵，且具备良好的泛化和可扩展能力，为自动化港口操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous tugboating is central for automating maritime operations such as port logistics and vessel maneuvering, where multiple tugboats must cooperatively transport/manipulate a larger vessel. Collaborative pushing in this setting is challenging due to coupled hydrodynamics, low resistance, strong environmental disturbances, underactuated barge dynamics, and contact-rich interactions. Conventional control methods often rely on simplified models and fixed configurations, which limit their adaptability, while learning-based approaches are constrained by the lack of scalable and physically realistic training environments. We address these challenges by introducing a physics-based, GPU-accelerated simulation and learning framework for collaborative tugboat manipulation. Our simulator incorporates a customized buoyancy model, wave modeling, and hydrodynamic resistance, and supports large-scale multi-agent training under marine dynamics. In this simulator, we train a decentralized MAPPO (Multi-Agent PPO) policy augmented with a structured control prior (SCP) to improve training stability and maintain feasible pushing configurations. We evaluate our learned policy on straight-line transit, turning, and deceleration tasks, where we show that our decentralized framework yields more reliable and accurate maneuvering performance compared to a PID-based controller and a centralized PPO baseline. We further demonstrate zero-shot generalization to more challenging sea states and advanced maneuvers, as well as zero-shot scalability to larger teams of three and four tugboats despite training with only two agents.

</details>


### [39] [Open-Set Ego-Noise Separation for Legged-Robot Audition via Annotation-Free Adaptation and Pretrained-Model Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07440)
*Koki Shoda,Jun Younes Louhi Kasahara,Aoba Koyanagi,Qi An,Atsushi Yamashita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于腿足机器人听觉的开集自我噪声分离框架，通过无标注适应和预训练模型迁移，无需单独录制噪声或手动标注即可去除机器人自身噪声并保留未预定义的环境声音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人行走产生的自我噪声（脚步声、关节反弹、电机噪声）严重污染声学记录，而现有方法需要人工标注或单独录制纯自我噪声数据，缺乏适应新机器人的自动化手段。

Method: 框架包含两个阶段：1) RecurGraph 利用聚类嵌入和音频嵌入图传播，从未标注录音中自动选择以自我噪声为主的片段；2) 将所选片段与大规模声音事件数据集中的多样环境声音混合，构造配对训练数据，并迁移通用的零样本神经分离器 Transfer-DiT，以适应目标机器人实现开集分离。

Result: 在双足和四足机器人实验中，该方法展现出可靠的片段选择能力，并在分离质量和下游任务性能上优于基线分离器。

Conclusion: 验证了在无标注、无单独噪声数据条件下，通过无标注适应与预训练模型迁移实现开集自我噪声分离的可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes an open-set ego-noise separation framework for legged-robot audition via annotation-free adaptation and pretrained-model transfer. The framework removes robot-specific ego-noise while preserving environmental sounds whose classes are not specified in advance. Acoustic sensing provides cues about a robot's surroundings beyond the visual field, but walking-induced ego-noise from footstep impacts, joint-backlash rattling, and motor noise severely contaminates the recordings. The framework first uses RecurGraph to select ego-noise-dominant clips from the unlabeled recordings by aggregating clip embeddings into an embedding centroid and propagating scores over an audio-embedding graph. The selected clips are mixed with diverse environmental sounds from a large-scale sound-event dataset to provide paired mixture--target supervision for open-set separation. Transfer-DiT then adapts a general-purpose zero-shot neural separator to achieve high-fidelity open-set ego-noise separation for the target robot. Experiments with bipedal and quadrupedal robots show reliable clip selection and improvements in separation quality and downstream task performance over baseline separators. These results demonstrate the feasibility of annotation-free adaptation without separately recorded ego-noise-only data or manual clip-level annotations.

</details>


### [40] [CALM: Configuration-Aware Human Intervention Boundaries During Robot Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07430)
*Xinting Gao,Sipu Zhu,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人身体构型（如手臂位置）显著影响人类在接近过程中的干预行为，研究提出配置感知极限模型（CALM）以生成配置相关的安全边界，并表明通过重新配置可实现原本不可达的接近目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人身体构型如何影响接近过程中的人类干预尚未被充分探索，现有研究多关注距离或速度，忽视了构型的作用。

Method: 进行41名被试的受试内实验，测量四种人形手臂构型和两种空间尺度下的最终停止距离、主观舒适度和眼动追踪响应；提出配置感知极限模型（CALM）将停止距离分布转化为配置相关的人群覆盖率边界。

Result: 手臂完全前伸使停止距离增加约31-36厘米；空间尺度主要影响舒适度和瞳孔响应，未显著改变停止距离；在80%覆盖率下估计边界为0.88至1.47米；一维规划分析表明，重新配置可实现名义20%干预概率约束下手臂完全前伸无法达到的1.10米接近目标。

Conclusion: 身体构型应被视为规划变量，并需区分物理安全、行为干预和主观成本，这为安全的人机接近规划提供了新视角。

Abstract: How robot body configuration shapes human intervention during approach remains underexplored. We conducted a within-participants study with 41 participants, measuring final stopping distance, subjective comfort, and exploratory eye-tracking responses across four humanoid arm configurations and two spatial scales. Full forward arm extension increased stopping distance by approximately 31-36 cm relative to arms-down. Spatial scale primarily affected comfort and pupil responses without a detectable stopping-distance shift. We introduce the Configuration-Aware Limit Model (CALM), which translates stopping-distance distributions into configuration-dependent population-coverage boundaries. Estimated boundaries at 80% coverage ranged from 0.88 to 1.47 m. In an illustrative one-dimensional planning analysis, reconfiguration enabled a 1.10 m approach goal that was unreachable with arms remaining fully extended under the same nominal pointwise 20% intervention-probability constraint. These findings support treating body configuration as a planning variable while distinguishing physical safety, behavioral intervention, and subjective cost.

</details>


### [41] [TANGO: Humanoid Navigation in Cluttered Environments with a Whole-Body Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09158)
*Anqi Li,Yuxin Chen,Zhaobo Li,Zhuo Cao,Junli Ren,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TANGO，首个基于视觉-语言导航的全身人形机器人穿越框架，直接从自然语言指令和第一视角 RGB 预测 29 自由度关节动作，在仿真中训练并可零样本部署到真实 Unitree G1 机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将人形机器人导航简化为 2D 路径规划，忽略了杂乱室内环境中所需的全身几何适应（手臂、躯干、步态协调）。现有工作缺乏支持语言指令与全身控制联合学习的框架。

Method: 全仿真训练管线：全局路径规划生成无碰撞穿越行为，运动学全身运动生成、障碍感知运动编辑和基于强化学习的跟踪，提供动态可行的关节动作监督，训练语言条件下的全身策略。

Result: 仿真实验在视觉语言导航上达到 SOTA，并在需要障碍协商的挑战场景中优于模块化基线；零样本部署到 Unitree G1 人形机器人，无需真实导航数据即可在杂乱场景中稳健执行语言引导穿越。

Conclusion: TANGO 证明语言条件的人形全身导航可通过仿真训练有效迁移到真实世界，为复杂 3D 空间中的语义导航提供新范式。

Abstract: We study the problem of navigating cluttered indoor environments with a humanoid robot. Unlike conventional methods that model navigation as a 2D path planning problem, humanoid traversal in cluttered environments requires continuous geometry-aware whole-body adaptation, including coordinated arm placement, torso adjustment, and gait modulation for collision-free movement through complex 3D spaces. We introduce TANGO, the first whole-body vision-language navigation framework for language-conditioned humanoid traversal in cluttered environments. Given a natural-language instruction and egocentric RGB observations, TANGO directly predicts 29-DoF joint-space actions for downstream whole-body control. We train TANGO entirely in simulation by synthesizing diverse collision-free traversal behaviors via global path planning, kinematic whole-body motion generation, obstacle-aware motion editing, and RL-based tracking. This pipeline provides dynamically feasible action supervision for learning language-conditioned whole-body policies. In extensive simulation experiments, TANGO demonstrates state-of-the-art performance in vision-language navigation, while outperforming strong modular baselines in navigating challenging scenes requiring obstacle negotiation. Lastly, we deploy TANGO zero-shot on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot, and observe robust language-guided traversal in cluttered real-world scenes without training on any real-world navigation data.

</details>


### [42] [Proxy Policy Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09148)
*Chuanruo Ning,Tianrui Wang,Wei-Chiu Ma,Kuan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出代理策略引导（PPS），一种推理时适应方法，通过两个轻量代理策略的速度差来引导冻结的通用基础策略，在不微调基础模型的情况下实现新任务适配，同时保留其广泛能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在大规模数据上具备广泛的操作先验，但针对新任务的专门化仍是部署瓶颈。现有方法如微调容易损害基础策略的通用能力，且需要访问模型参数或大量计算。

Method: PPS训练两个轻量代理策略：参考代理建模冻结基础策略在目标任务观测上的行为，任务代理（从参考初始化）捕捉任务监督下的行为变化。两者在速度空间上的校准差异作为残差，在每一步去噪时引导冻结的基础采样器。通过条件分析确保残差隔离任务监督引起的变化，且仅需基础策略的前向速度预测。

Result: 在8个真实世界和4个仿真操作任务上，PPS将当前最优pi 0.5基础策略的平均绝对成功率提升53%，在基础策略从未解决的任务上实现从0到1的突破，同时保持其广泛能力，并优于LoRA微调、从零训练专家、残差策略及先前的推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: PPS是一种轻量、无需修改基础模型参数的推理时适应方法，能有效利用有限演示专门化通用策略，同时保留其恢复失败等广泛能力，为机器人策略部署提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies carry broad manipulation priors from large-scale data, but specializing them to a new task remains the deployment bottleneck. This requires eliciting task-specific behavior from limited demonstrations without degrading their broad capabilities. We introduce Proxy Policy Steering (PPS), an inference-time adaptation method that resolves this challenge by training two lightweight proxy policies whose calibrated velocity-space difference steers the frozen base sampler. A reference proxy models the frozen base's behavior on target-task observations, and a task proxy, initialized from the reference, captures how this behavior changes under task supervision. Their difference forms a calibrated velocity-space residual that steers the frozen base sampler at every denoising step. We identify the conditions under which this residual isolates the change induced by task supervision, and validate them empirically. Because the base is never directly modified, its broad capabilities remain available at inference, including behaviors such as recovery from failure that the demonstrations themselves do not exercise. Adaptation requires only forward velocity predictions from the base, making PPS lightweight to train and applicable even without access to the base's parameters. On 8 real-world and 4 simulation manipulation tasks, PPS lifts the state-of-the-art pi 0.5 base policy by 53% absolute success rate on average, with zero-to-one gains on tasks the base never solves, while preserving the base's broad capabilities. PPS outperforms LoRA fine-tuning, from-scratch specialists, residual policies, and prior inference-time steering methods.

</details>


### [43] [DeCAL: Towards Physically-Grounded Dexterous Vision-Language-Action Models via Contact-Aware Latent Co-Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09119)
*Yankai Fu,Ning Chen,Junkai Zhao,Heng Zhang,Guocai Yao,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeCAL，一种基于物理的灵巧操作视觉-语言-动作模型，通过MoT架构、自适应视觉-触觉融合和潜空间联合想象，实现接触丰富的灵巧操作，平均成功率71%，进展成功率83.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作涉及接触丰富且细粒度的物理交互，现有VLA模型因严重视觉遮挡和复杂接触动力学而面临挑战；近期引入触觉的工作多采用同质多模态融合，缺乏自适应触觉整合与物理动力学显式建模。

Method: DeCAL基于Mixture-of-Transformers架构，分设专家处理理解、想象和动作生成并保持高效信息流；提出自适应视觉-触觉融合（利用接触感知门控动态调节触觉交互）；提出视觉-触觉潜空间联合想象以共同建模视觉与触觉动力学，赋予策略隐式物理世界知识。

Result: 在所有任务上达到最先进性能，平均成功率71%，进展成功率83.4%，并展现出对未见场景的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: DeCAL通过物理引导的建模和自适应触觉融合，有效提升了接触丰富灵巧操作的性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation involves contact-rich and fine-grained interactions with the physical world, posing significant challenges for existing vision-language-action (VLA) models due to severe visual occlusions and complex contact dynamics. While recent works have incorporated tactile sensing into robotic manipulation, most approaches still rely on homogeneous multimodal fusion, lacking adaptive tactile integration and explicit modeling of physical dynamics. In this work, we present DeCAL, a physically-grounded dexterous vision-language-action model that unifies understanding, imagination and action generation for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. Built upon a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, DeCAL leverages specialized experts for each capability while enabling efficient information flow among them. To effectively leverage tactile information, we introduce Adaptive Visuo-Tactile Fusion that dynamically regulates tactile interactions via a contact-aware gating strategy. Furthermore, we propose Visuo-Tactile Latent Co-Imagination to jointly model visual and tactile dynamics, equipping the policy with implicit physical world knowledge. Experimental results show that DeCAL consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks, attaining a 71% average success rate and an 83.4% progress success rate, while also demonstrating strong generalization to unseen scenarios. The website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [44] [Online, Reachability-Aware, Sampling-Based Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09073)
*Brendan Gould,Zhiyuan Zhang,Panagiotis Tsiotras,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于区间分析的在线可达集过近似方法，为采样型模型预测控制（MPC）提供硬安全保证，无需预计算即可实现与最先进可达性规划器相当的性能，并将安全违规降低99%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型MPC算法在机器人导航中广泛应用，但历史上缺乏硬安全保证；现有可达性方法需要昂贵或不可扩展的预计算步骤。

Method: 在线使用快速区间分析流水线计算保证的可达集过近似，并将其集成到采样型MPC框架中，以提供安全性保证并支持更高维度系统。

Result: 与最先进可达性规划器性能相当且无需预计算；可扩展到已有方法无法处理的系统；在赛车仿真中安全违规减少超过99%，并在真实硬件模型赛车上实现无碰撞控制。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持采样型MPC灵活性的同时赋予其硬安全保证，显著提升安全性并支持实际部署，是一种实用且可扩展的安全控制方案。

Abstract: Sampling-Based Model-Predictive Control (MPC) algorithms are a flexible class of controllers used for navigation on a wide range of robotic systems. Historically, such approaches have lacked hard safety guarantees, a shortcoming which we remedy in this work by computing guaranteed reachable-set overapproximations online with a fast, interval-based pipeline. We show that our method achieves similar performance to a state-of-the-art reachability-based planner without the need for the expensive pre-computation step, and can be scaled to systems that are infeasible using existing approaches. Finally, we demonstrate that our technique reduces safety violations by over 99% in a racing simulation and successfully controls a model racecar on real hardware experiments without crashes.

</details>


### [45] [Rethinking Learned Occupancy in Autonomous Active Mapping with Observation-Gated Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09069)
*Jiahui Zhang,Bonian Han,Gongbo Liang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在自主3D主动建图中，学习得到的占用补全地图同时用于表面增益评分与碰撞约束，但其不准确性会扭曲感知与规划。实验表明占用精度提升不单调改善覆盖率，据此提出一种观测门控滤波器，仅在多次视锥暴露且缺乏RGB-D支持时抑制预测，无需重训练即可改善失败场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间机器人的自主3D主动建图需要决定感知位置，学习式占用补全可扩展空间上下文，但同一预测地图同时用于评估表面增益和约束无碰撞运动，导致不支持的占用会扭曲机器人观察位置和可行区域。现有研究未充分分析这一耦合接口的影响。

Method: 在受控闭环基准中固定主动建图系统，仅改变面向规划器的占用来源（观测仅、学习、真值校正、真值）。基于诊断结果，提出观测门控滤波器：在未充分观测区域保留补全，仅在重复视锥暴露且附近无RGB-D支持时抑制预测。

Result: 提升占用精度并非单调改善闭环覆盖率：25个起始条件下，使用真值占用的规划比学习基线平均早12.7步达到70%最终覆盖率，但最终覆盖率仅提高0.031。观测门控滤波器在无需重训练或真值情况下改善了目标失败起始点。

Conclusion: 结果表明在通信间隙的自主运行期间应在线修正面向规划器的几何地图。当前研究基于基准RGB-D观测和足够精确的位姿估计，未来需评估行星感知条件和累计定位漂移的影响。

Abstract: Autonomous 3D active mapping requires a space robot to choose where to sense while building the geometry needed for navigation. Learned occupancy completion extends spatial context beyond the current field of view, but one predicted map often serves two planning roles: it scores expected surface gain and constrains collision-free motion. Unsupported occupancy can therefore distort both where the robot looks and where it believes it can travel. We study this coupled interface in a controlled closed-loop benchmark by holding the active-mapping system fixed and varying only its planner-facing occupancy across observation-only, learned, oracle-corrected, and ground-truth conditions. Improving occupancy accuracy does not monotonically improve closed-loop coverage: across 25 starts, planning with ground-truth occupancy reaches 70% of the learned baseline's final coverage 12.7 steps earlier on average, while increasing final coverage by only 0.031. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce an observation-gated filter that retains completion in insufficiently observed regions and suppresses predictions only after repeated frustum exposure without nearby RGB-D support. The filter improves both targeted failure-prone starts without retraining or ground truth. These results motivate online revision of planner-facing geometry during autonomous intervals between communication windows. The current study assumes benchmark RGB-D observations and sufficiently accurate pose estimates; planetary sensing conditions and accumulated localization drift remain to be evaluated.

</details>


### [46] [A Distributed Consensus Particle Filter for Target Tracking using Autonomous Surface Vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09066)
*Carter Noh,Kyle Crandall,Connor Yates,Corbin Wilhelmi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种对经典粒子滤波器的增强方法，用于多智能体远距离海上目标跟踪，在缺乏通信时通过策略性粒子扩散避免过度自信，并在湖上无人艇实验中验证性能不降且在某些边缘情况有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离海上目标跟踪常需要多智能体协同，但缺乏集中协调和间歇性通信，每个智能体需独立估计。在无外部数据时，局部估计可能过度自信，导致跟踪误差增大。

Method: 对经典粒子滤波器进行增强，在缺乏其他传感器节点信息更新时，强制粒子策略性扩散，以补偿潜在的信息缺失误差。

Result: 在湖上使用无人水面艇（USV）进行实验，证明增强方法不降低标称性能，并在某些特定边缘情况下提供优势。

Conclusion: 所提增强方法能有效应对间歇通信导致的过度自信问题，适用于多智能体远距离海上目标跟踪，具有鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Maritime target tracking over large distances often requires multi-agent teams without centralized coordination, and intermittent communication. Each agent must maintain an independent estimate that can take advantage of opportunistic communications availability when possible. This can lead to overly confident local estimates in the absence of external data. In this work, we propose an augmentation to a classical particle filter implementation that accounts for this potential source of error by forcing particles to spread strategically in the absence of informative updates from other sensor nodes. We demonstrate our method using Unmanned Surface Vessels (USVs) on a lake, and show that our augmentations do not deteriorate nominal performance, and provide an advantage in some specific edge cases.

</details>


### [47] [DYAD: A Multimodal Dataset of Co-Located Human Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09023)
*Akhil Ajikumar,Mahya Qorbani,Sakib Reza,Sean Andrist,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DYAD，一个跨人机协作的同步多模态数据集，记录在齿轮箱装配中人类助手与HoloLens2佩戴者的协助交互，链接求助请求、任务状态、干预类型与结果，支持三个子任务评估并提供丰富因果标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流程数据仅描述单一执行过程，交互数据侧重远程口头指令或简单共事，缺乏能够联合建模共地助手言语与物理干预、执行者请求、任务状态、帮助触发条件及结果的结构化数据。

Method: 构建DYAD数据集，包含20次会话，由一名训练有素的助手遵循指导优先策略协助HoloLens 2佩戴者；标注528个任务步骤区间、611次执行者请求及851条有效帮助记录，覆盖言语和物理协助。设计三个参考任务：因果步骤理解、前期模式预测和指导者响应生成，以评估特定组件而非端到端系统。

Result: 在829个有效模式事件上，基于四种子RGB的最强平均宏F1为0.548±0.007；加入因果元数据后提升至0.624，特权触发映射达0.915，表明RGB预处理未捕获这些关键信息。

Conclusion: DYAD的价值不在规模，而在于提供一种链接式交互结构，能够统一刻画求助、干预选择、执行和结果，并融合第一人称与工作区感知，为后续具身助手研究提供稀缺的细粒度标注资源。

Abstract: An embodied assistant working beside a person must track task state, recognize help seeking, choose how to intervene, and produce an appropriate response. Existing procedural datasets richly describe individual execution, while interactive datasets capture remote verbal instruction or undifferentiated co-working. They do not jointly link a co-located helper's verbal and physical interventions to performer requests, task state, assistance triggers, and outcomes. We introduce DYAD (DYadic Assistance Dataset), a synchronized multimodal record of human-human assistance during gearbox assembly. Across 20 sessions, one trained helper follows a guidance-first policy while assisting HoloLens 2 wearers. DYAD links 528 task-step intervals and 611 performer requests with 851 valid assistance records spanning verbal and physical help. DYAD's annotations span the assistance process; three reference tasks evaluate selected components rather than an end-to-end system: causal step understanding, pre-onset mode anticipation, and instructor response generation. On 829 eligible mode events, the strongest four-seed RGB mean is 0.548 +/- 0.007 macro-F1; causal metadata reaches 0.624 and a privileged trigger mapping 0.915, revealing information not recovered from pre-onset RGB. DYAD's contribution is not scale, but a linked interaction structure spanning help seeking, intervention choice, execution, and outcome under egocentric and workspace sensing.

</details>


### [48] [Model Predictive Control of Tensegrity Robots via Contact-Aware Graph Neural Dynamics Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08958)
*Nelson Chen,Patrick Meng,Charles Tang,Angelina Degay,Zachary Brei,Rebecca Kramer-Bottiglio,Kostas E. Bekris,Mridul Aanjaneya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络（GNN）学习动力学模型与模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制的混合控制器，用于三杆张拉整体机器人在复杂接触丰富环境中的导航。通过可微接触检测模块扩展GNN模型，并在闭环数据采集循环中迭代改进，结合运动原语提升机动性。实验表明该控制器在多个导航任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体机器人具有轻量、柔顺、适应复杂地形的优点，但其接触丰富的动力学和部分可观测性导致建模与控制困难。现有方法难以处理非水平地形、障碍物和自碰撞，且控制性能有限。

Method: 提出一个MPPI控制器，使用学习的GNN动力学模型。扩展了之前的GNN模型，加入可微接触检测模块以处理非水平地形、障碍物和自碰撞。学习模型与MPPI控制器在闭环数据采集循环中交替提升。引入混合MPPI策略，结合转弯运动原语提高机动性。

Result: 在MuJoCo的五个导航任务（墙体障碍、斜坡、窄通道、低净空结构、复合3D障碍课程）中，混合MPPI控制器在所学GNN动力学模型上比平地基线模型有更高的预测精度，并优于基于A*的重规划和纯MPPI变体，实现了鲁棒的导航性能。

Conclusion: 接触感知的学习动力学与基于采样的模型预测控制相结合，能够实现在复杂接触丰富环境中张拉整体机器人的鲁棒导航，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Tensegrity robots offer lightweight, compliant mobility over challenging terrain but remain difficult to model and control due to complex contact-rich dynamics and partial observability. This work presents a model predictive path integral (MPPI) controller for a three-bar tensegrity robot driven by a learned graph neural network (GNN) dynamics model. This work first extends prior GNN-based models with a differentiable contact detection module. The extension allows the dynamics model to reason over non-horizontal planar terrains, obstacles, as well as self-collisions. Then, the learned dynamics model and the MPPI controller operate in a closed data-collection loop, iteratively improving model accuracy and control performance. This work further introduces a hybrid MPPI strategy that combines MPPI with turning motion primitives to improve maneuverability. Experiments are performed in MuJoCo across five navigation tasks, which include, wall obstacles, inclines, narrow corridors, low-clearance structures, and a composite 3D obstacle course. The experiments demonstrate that the hybrid MPPI controller operating over the learned GNN dynamics model improves predictive accuracy over a flat-ground baseline model and achieves superior navigation performance compared to $A^*$-based re-planning and MPPI-only variants. Results show that the contact-aware learned dynamics combined with the sampling-based model predictive control enable robust tensegrity navigation in complex, contact-rich environments.

</details>


### [49] [Remotely Detectable Keyed Communication through Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08920)
*Benjamin Chang,Michael Amir,Manon Flageat,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过机器人动作本身传递消息的方法，在不降低策略性能的前提下，将任意短消息编码为预训练策略动作中的噪声，可从远程感知（如视频或动捕）解码，无需额外硬件或直接链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电子设备通过文本、音频或无线电信号通信，但机器人具有丰富的现实动作，若能通过动作本身传递消息，可提供一种不依赖无线信道的新通信方式，增强鲁棒性。

Method: 将消息编码为预训练策略动作中的噪声，系统性地刻画编码方案空间并导出设计启发式规则。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上验证，真实机器人以50Hz运行时，四台机器人联合恢复8位消息，总速率0.67 bits/s。

Conclusion: 该物理信道补充了标准无线通信，无需额外硬件或直接链路，为机器人通信提供了新途径。

Abstract: Messages from electronic devices are conventionally received as text, audio, or radio signals. But robots move with rich, articulate motion in the real world, opening up the possibility of transmitting messages through motion itself. In this paper, we consider the problem of motion-based communication, where we seek to modify a robot's movements so as to transmit messages detectable from remote sensing (e.g., video or motion capture), without degrading policy performance. We introduce a method for messaging through motion capable of encoding arbitrary message content over short payloads - such as an agent's current intent - as noise in any pre-trained policy's actions. This brings a new kind of robustness to robot communication: this 'physical' channel complements standard wireless communications channels but does not depend on them, requiring no extra hardware nor the establishment of a direct link to the robot. We systematically characterize the space of encoding schemes and derive design heuristics, then validate them across simulated environments and real-robot deployment; on real robots running at 50 Hz, four robots jointly recover an 8-bit message at an aggregate 0.67 bits/s.

</details>


### [50] [Visible-Reachable Workspace for Perception-Aware Humanoid Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08905)
*Boxi Xia,Zijiang Yang,Ryan Shin,Bokuan Li,Eric Wun-Hao Lu,Jacob Lee,Jiaxun Liu,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可见可达工作空间（VRW）作为设计阶段度量，同时考虑可达性与感知可见性。在31自由度人形机器人上，独立驱动的RGB-D相机将可见可达覆盖率从38%提升至97%；双相机将成对覆盖率从0.45提升至0.95，三相机仅增至0.97。双驱动相机设计在受控目标抓取基准中将平均完成时间降低17%，机械能降低19%。硬件实验验证了无需躯干重定位即可同时观察并操作前后/左右目标对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工作空间分析仅关注末端执行器的可达性，但视觉引导操作中，可达目标可能因所需姿态下不可见而无法执行，迫使机器人额外移动感知或身体。现有人形机器人在模仿人体形态时继承了这一固有限制，缺乏在设计阶段同时考虑感知配置与可达空间的度量。

Method: 引入可见可达工作空间（VRW），在可达关节配置上附加可见性条件，并扩展为空间分离工作区域的并发可见性。构建31自由度人形机器人，配备独立驱动的RGB-D相机；通过对比不同相机布局（固定、单驱动、双驱动、三驱动）评估VRW覆盖率及成对覆盖，并在两目标抓取基准中测试系统性能。

Result: 相机关节化使VRW覆盖率从38%增至97%；双相机使成对覆盖率从0.45升至0.95，第三相机仅提升至0.97。双驱动相机设计相比固定相机将完成时间减少17%，机械能减少19%。硬件实验成功实现前后和左右目标对的同步观察与操作，无需躯干重新定向。

Conclusion: 将可达性与感知配置联合评估可提升传感器驱动的类人操作设计质量。相机关节化显著扩展可见可达空间，双相机收益显著而第三相机边际收益极小。软件与人形硬件设计将开源，助力后续研究。

Abstract: Workspace analysis measures where a robot can place its end effector. For visually guided manipulation, reachability alone is insufficient: a kinematically reachable target may not be visible in the specific pose required to reach it. The robot must then redirect its sensing or move its body to acquire a view, turning a perception limitation into additional motion. Existing humanoids largely inherit this limitation when copying human form factors. We introduce the visible-reachable workspace (VRW), a design-stage measure that conditions visibility on feasible reaching configurations and extends it to concurrent visibility of spatially separated work regions. We apply VRW by building a 31-DoF humanoid with independently actuated RGB-D cameras. On the same robot, camera articulation increases visible-reachable coverage from 38% to 97%. With actuated camera layouts, a second camera raises pairwise coverage from 0.45 to 0.95, while a third changes it only to 0.97. In a controlled two-target reach-and-grasp benchmark, our dual-actuated design reduces mean completion time by 17% and mechanical energy by 19% relative to the same robot with its cameras fixed. Hardware experiments demonstrate simultaneous observation and manipulation of front/back and left/right target pairs without torso reorientation. The results suggest that reachability becomes a more informative design quantity for perception-driven humanoid manipulation when it is evaluated together with the sensing configurations that make the reachable space observable. We will open-source all software and the humanoid hardware design. Our website is this https URL

</details>


### [51] [CAST: Alternating State-Value Targets and Expanded Policy Gradients for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08853)
*Pietro Noah Crestaz,Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Nicolas Mansard,Andrea Del Prete*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CAST方法，用规划器引导的行为改进价值学习，同时用当前策略正则化状态值估计，在仿真和真实四足机器人上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习在机器人领域具有样本效率优势，但结合在线规划时，现有方法通常只学习当前策略的价值，未能充分利用规划器产生的更强行为。

Method: CAST用状态值批评者替代动作值批评者，训练目标混合规划器引导的真实转移和当前策略生成的想象转移，使价值函数对应规划器行为与当前策略交替的过程。

Result: 在DeepMind Control和HumanoidBench基准上超越多种SOTA方法，并成功迁移到物理Unitree Go2四足机器人完成动态倒立任务。

Conclusion: 交替状态值目标能有效结合规划器强行为与策略正则化，提升价值学习效率，并支持真实机器人部署。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) is a family of RL methods that learn a model of the environment and use it for action selection, making it well suited to robotics due to its sample efficiency. Combining learned models with online planning can further improve action selection, as the planner can exploit the model to find better actions than the learned policy alone. Recent methods combining learned policies with online planning typically learn the value of the policy rather than the stronger planner-guided behavior. We present CAST (Critic with Alternating State-value Target), which uses planner-guided behavior to improve value learning while regularizing the value estimate with the current policy. CAST replaces the action-value critic with a state-value critic, trained using a target that combines a real planner-guided transition and an imagined transition under the current policy. The resulting value function corresponds to an alternating process between planner-guided behavior and the current policy, allowing it to benefit from the stronger planner behavior while being regularised by the policy being learned. We evaluate CAST on the DeepMind Control and HumanoidBench Suites against several state-of-the-art methods, and demonstrate successful transfer to a physical Unitree Go2 quadruped performing a dynamic handstand.

</details>


### [52] [Real-time Puncture Detection and Recovery for Pneumatic Soft Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08804)
*Tejonidhi R. Deshpande,Tingyu Cheng,Josiah Hester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单惯性测量单元运动数据的软体驱动器穿刺检测系统，可识别穿刺腔室并评估严重程度，并展示故障恢复策略以维持驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人依赖柔性气动执行器，但其易受损（如穿刺、撕裂），限制实际部署，需要实时损伤检测与恢复机制。

Method: 利用单IMU运动数据提取特征，训练异常检测器（基于重构误差）检测穿刺，训练非线性模型估计严重程度；提出多腔室柔性弯曲执行器，通过选择性充气实现多样配置，并设计腔室扰动方案定位受损腔室。

Result: 算法能识别穿刺腔室并提供严重性评分，故障恢复策略可在损伤后维持驱动力，增强软体系统可靠性和安全性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的实时损伤检测方法有效提升软体机器人鲁棒性，支持实际应用中的安全运维。

Abstract: Soft robots offer safe and adaptive interaction with humans and unstructured environments through their inherent ability to deform and comply. Pneumatic actuators are one way to build soft robots. They are typically made from soft silicone materials and are especially effective for driving such systems, enabling smooth and adaptable motion. However, their compliant nature also makes them vulnerable to mechanical failures like punctures and tears, limiting practical deployment. To address this, we propose a puncture detection system for soft actuators using motion data from a single inertial measurement unit. Extracted features are used to train anomaly detectors for puncture detection and non-linear models to estimate severity. We also introduce a multi-chamber pneumatic soft bending actuator capable of diverse configurations via selective chamber inflation. Our algorithm identifies the punctured chamber and provides a severity score using a chamber perturbation scheme. Anomaly detectors are trained on normal operation data and detect damage through reconstruction errors, while severity is estimated by a separate model trained under slightly modified conditions. Finally, we demonstrate a failure recovery strategy to maintain actuation force post-failure. This approach enhances the reliability and safety of soft robotic systems through real-time, data-driven damage detection.

</details>


### [53] [Graph-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Agent Systems with Time-Varying Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08802)
*Xiao Sizhe,Dong Lijing,Bai Rui,Tan Xin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图的安全多智能体强化学习框架，用于时变拓扑下的协同导航，通过CBLF动作筛选层解耦安全与学习，结合注意力Actor与GAT中心化Critic，在真实差速机器人上验证了有限视野动态场景中的稳定性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体协同导航中，在感知受限（如离散LiDAR、有限视野）和时变通信拓扑下，如何严格保证物理安全并实现稳定策略学习的问题。

Method: 引入安全解耦机制，利用类控制障碍函数（CBLF）动作筛选层连接离散感知与连续安全约束；Actor采用注意力机制与值向量重构，通过协同跟踪误差矩阵编码相对几何关系以获得尺度不敏感策略；Critic使用图注意力网络（GAT）建模动态交互结构进行全局价值估计。

Result: 在真实差速驱动机器人平台上验证，实验显示在有限视野动态场景中，所提框架相比基线具有更优的稳定性和安全性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效将安全保证与学习过程解耦，同时适应时变拓扑和感知受限环境，为实际多机器人协同导航提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a graph-based safe multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework for cooperative navigation with time-varying topology. To address the critical challenge of ensuring safety in environments with sensing constraints, a safety-decoupled mechanism is introduced through a Control Barrier-Like Function (CBLF) action screening layer. This mechanism bridges the gap between discrete LiDAR perception and continuous safety constraints, ensuring that physical safety constraints are strictly satisfied regardless of the learning progress. Building upon this safety foundation, a unified structural architecture is proposed, integrating a attention-based actor and a Graph Attention Network (GAT) centralized critic. The actor utilizes a value vector reconstruction mechanism that explicitly encodes relative geometric relations through a collaborative tracking error matrix, enabling scale-insensitive policy learning under time-varying communication topologies. Meanwhile, the GAT-based critic models evolving interaction structures for accurate global value estimation. The proposed framework is validated on real differential-drive robot platforms, and experimental results demonstrate superior stability and safety in dynamic scenarios with limited fields-of-view.

</details>


### [54] [Ostrich: Taking Large Strides Through Stiff Contact in Differentiable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08800)
*Aleš Kučera,Karel Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ostrich 是一种 GPU 加速的刚体仿真器，通过非光滑牛顿迭代在大时间步长下解析硬接触与摩擦，并利用隐函数定理以 O(1) 每时间步内存计算梯度，实现了比现有方法更快、更稳定且内存高效的接触优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的接触优化需要同时具备仿真精度、梯度可靠性和低迭代成本。现有磁带式引擎（如 MJX、Newton Semi-Implicit）受限于小时间步长和随 T 线性增长的显存；替代模型虽固定内存但损失了几何梯度信息，因此需要一种能在较大时间步长下保持精度并高效计算可靠梯度的方案。

Method: Ostrich 采用非光滑牛顿迭代解决大时间步长（h~0.1s）下的硬接触和摩擦，通过对收敛残差应用隐函数定理求导，并复用前向 Schur 补来计算伴随，从而使每时间步内存为 O(1)，且完全在 GPU 上并行化。

Result: 在真实机器人越障轨迹中，Ostrich 在 50 倍于 MuJoCo 的时间步长下保持相同的 sim-to-real 精度；其梯度可从随机初始值收敛，而 MJX 下降缓慢、Newton Semi-Implicit 停滞；热启动迭代比 MJX 快 211 倍、比 Semi-Implicit 快 4.7 倍。在单张 24 GB GPU 上并行 8,192 个环境时，吞吐量是 checkpointed MJX 的 29 倍，且无需 checkpointing 即可避免两基线在少量环境下的显存耗尽。

Conclusion: Ostrich 在大时间步长下同时实现了精度、可靠梯度和低内存开销，使基于梯度的轨迹优化能够扩展到三角网格地形和 10 秒时域，突破了先前引擎在几何通用性或收敛/内存上的限制。

Abstract: Three properties determine whether a differentiable simulator can drive gradient-based optimization through contact: simulation accuracy, gradient reliability, and per-iteration cost. Tape-based engines such as MJX and Newton Semi-Implicit require timesteps small enough to keep contacts numerically tractable, and their backpropagation memory grows linearly with the number of timesteps T. Surrogate models bound memory by approximating contact away, but the resulting gradients lose the geometry the optimization depends on. We present Ostrich, a GPU-accelerated rigid-body simulator that resolves hard contacts and friction with non-smooth Newton iteration at large timesteps (h ~ 0.1 s), and differentiates the converged residual via the implicit function theorem, reusing the forward Schur complement to compute the adjoint at O(1) memory per timestep. On real-robot trajectories over a pallet obstacle, Ostrich holds MuJoCo's sim-to-real accuracy up to a 50x larger timestep. Its gradients converge from random initializations where MJX descends slowly and Newton Semi-Implicit stalls; a warm iteration runs 211x faster than MJX's and 4.7x faster than Semi-Implicit's. On the same scene Ostrich differentiates 8,192 parallel worlds on a single 24 GB GPU, sustaining 29x checkpointed MJX's optimization throughput; without checkpointing both baselines exhaust memory at far fewer worlds. We close with a gradient-based trajectory optimization demonstration over triangle-mesh terrain across a 10 s horizon, a setting where prior engines either restrict to primitive geometry or face the convergence and memory limits shown above.

</details>


### [55] [A Controlled Comparison of Manual and Teleoperated Intraocular Instrument Motion for an Input Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08770)
*Korab Hoxha,Mirza Imamovic,Angelo Henriques,M. Ali Nasseri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人显微手术中，遥操作界面虽保持任务结果，但显著改变了器械运动特征和手部活动；尽管完成了任务，执行过程与手动操作差异巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人显微手术输入设备常声称保留外科医生的技术，但缺乏实际测量验证；现有比较无法控制器械变量。

Method: 在共享插管约束、器械、眼模型和追踪源的条件下，16名参与者在商用眼科模拟器上分别手动和通过三自由度输入设备控制五关节机器人完成导航任务，比较任务结果与运动学指标。

Result: 任务结果相同且达到上限（无损伤），但遥操作耗时3倍、中位速度四分之一、角度范围不足一半、移动片段数3.5倍；经过四次练习，完成时间和碎片化显著改善但未稳定，活动度受限指标变化最小；手指活动和捏合变异分别翻倍和三倍。

Conclusion: 界面保留结果而重塑执行，减少器械自由度并未减少手动努力，而是重新分配了工作负荷；遥操作改变运动模式但可随练习适应。

Abstract: Input devices for robotic microsurgery are frequently described as preserving the surgeon's trained technique, but the claim is rarely measured. We compared manual and teleoperated intraocular instrument motion with the trocar constraint, the instrument, the eye model and the tracking source common to both conditions, so that the control interface was the only factor varied. Prior comparisons cannot hold the instrument fixed, because a robotic instrument is not the tool used manually. Sixteen participants performed a navigation task on a commercial ophthalmic simulator by hand and through a three-degree-of-freedom input device commanding a five-joint robot. Task outcome was equal but at ceiling: every participant acquired all five targets under both interfaces with no retinal or lens injury. Execution differed on every measure. Teleoperated trials took three times as long at a quarter of the median speed, covered less than half the angular working range, and were broken into 3.5 times as many separate movements. Completion time and movement fragmentation improved substantially across four trials of practice and had not plateaued; the measures set by the configured rate ceiling and joint limit changed the least. Finger activity doubled and pinch variability tripled, so reducing instrument degrees of freedom redistributed manual effort rather than reducing it. The interface preserves the outcome and reshapes the execution.

</details>


### [56] [FOCI Policy: Focus on Object-Centric Interactions for Relational Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08743)
*Ze Fu,Pinhao Song,Yutong Hu,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Foci Policy，一种以交互为中心的框架，通过提取紧凑交互片段并建模物体间相对SE(3)运动，在RLBench、COLOSSEUM及真实任务中以显著更少的数据实现强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体中心策略常受限于表示方式：要么过于简单难以捕捉交互动态，要么过于稠密导致学习效率低。许多刚性关系操作任务由短暂交互阶段主导，相对运动受严格约束，因此需要一种更高效的抽象。

Method: Foci Policy采用双重抽象：(1)时间上自动从演示中提取紧凑交互片段；(2)空间上将技能表示为任务相关物体间的相对SE(3)运动，从而对场景配置和机器人本体具有不变性。

Result: 在RLBench、COLOSSEUM和真实世界任务中，Foci Policy相比先前的物体中心和动作中心策略，使用大幅减少的训练数据即达到强性能。

Conclusion: 建模物体-物体交互为刚性关系操作提供了简单而高效的归纳偏置，验证了交互中心方法的有效性。

Abstract: Object-centric manipulation policies improve generalization by modeling object motion instead of directly predicting robot actions. However, existing methods are often limited by representations which are either too simplistic to capture interaction dynamics or too dense to learn efficiently. We observe that many rigid relational manipulation tasks are governed by short interaction phases where the relative motion between task-relevant objects is tightly constrained. Based on this observation, we propose \textsc{Foci Policy}, an interaction-centric framework that achieves a two-fold abstraction: (1) temporally, by automatically extracting compact interaction segments from demonstrations;(2) spatially, by representing skills as relative $SE(3)$ motion between task-relevant objects, yielding invariance to scene configurations and robot embodiment. Experiments on RLBench, COLOSSEUM, and real-world tasks show that \textsc{Foci Policy} achieves strong performance with substantially less training data than prior object-centric and action-centric policies. These results suggest that modeling object-object interactions provides a simple and efficient inductive bias for rigid relational manipulation. Project page: \href{ this https URL }{ this http URL }.

</details>


### [57] [DCLP++: Learning to Navigate with Footprint Clearance and Relative Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08711)
*Shanze Wang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DCLP++局部导航框架，以足迹净空（footprint clearance）为几何基础，将激光雷达回波映射到机器人填充足迹的最短欧氏距离，替代传感器距离。在矩形机器人、1m/s限速、20个移动障碍物的100个固定验证任务中，两种训练种子在200,000环境步后平均成功率达42%（传感器距离）和70%（足迹净空）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部导航方法常直接使用传感器原始距离或依赖静态-动态标签，忽略了机器人实际占用体积与动态环境的相对几何特征。作者认为足迹净空更能反映机器人本体与障碍物的真实接近程度，有助于提升动态环境中的导航性能。

Method: DCLP++将每个有效的LiDAR回波映射到填充机器人足迹的最短欧氏距离，再进行反向编码，即用“到占用本体的距离”替代“到传感器的距离”。策略输入不使用静态-动态标签，而是采用径向测量或模拟的平面相对速度作为短时特征。初步研究在矩形机器人、1m/s速度上限、20个移动障碍物场景下进行验证。

Result: 在100个固定验证任务上，两种训练种子在200,000环境步后平均成功率为：使用传感器距离时为42%，使用足迹净空时为70%。多种变体显示额外的混合增益。

Conclusion: 初步结果支持在评测环境中基于净空的观测的有效性，但可靠的动态运动收益及跨机器人迁移仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: We present DCLP++, a local navigation frameworkthat uses footprint clearance as the geometric basis for studying relative motion features in dynamic environments. Each valid LiDAR return is mapped to its shortest Euclidean distance from the filled robot footprint before reciprocal encoding, replacing distance from the sensor with distance to the occupied body. Radial measurementsor simulated planar relative velocities provide short-horizon features without static-dynamic labels in the policy input. A preliminary study uses a rectangular robot with a speed limit of 1 m/s among 20 moving obstacles. On 100 fixed validation tasks, two selected training seeds yield mean success rates of 42% with sensor rangeand 70% with footprint clearance after 200,000 environment this http URL variants show mixed additional gains. These results supportthe clearance-based observation in the evaluated setting; reliable motion benefits and transfer across robots require further evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [HiBRIDGE: A Hierarchical Bayesian Neural Network Framework for Interpretable Dialogue Management in Group-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08678)
*Massimiliano Nigro,Hatice Gunes,Micol Spitale,Fethiye Irmak Dogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiBRIDGE 是一个用于多人群机器人对话管理的分层贝叶斯神经网络框架，通过不确定性感知和结构化多阶段决策，在离线数据集上提升预测性能，并通过在线和现场实验证明其决策解释更易理解且支持实时交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多人群机器人交互中，机器人需要不断决定向谁发言和说什么。由于多种行为可能同时合理，且决策依赖交互上下文，现有方法在表示不确定性、以及将决策结构化为语义上有意义的中间步骤以便解释方面存在局限。

Method: 提出 HiBRIDGE，一种分层贝叶斯神经网络框架。贝叶斯公式支持不确定性感知和从有限数据中稳健学习；分层方法将行为选择建模为结构化的多阶段决策过程，并使用决策树替代模型来研究该结构能否支持更可解释的说明。

Result: 在三个离线群组人机交互数据集上，贝叶斯公式优于确定性对应方法和多个最新基线。在线研究（N=20）显示，分层模型导出的解释比平面模型更易于理解且更受偏好。现场研究（N=12）证明 HiBRIDGE 可实现自主实时群组交互，且分层和平面贝叶斯变体均获积极评价。

Conclusion: HiBRIDGE 结合了强大的预测性能与结构化决策过程，能够提供更可解释的机器人行为说明，在群组交互中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In multi-party human-robot interaction, a robot must continuously decide whom to address and what to say to participate effectively in the conversation. In real-world interactions, this is challenging because several behaviours may be plausible at the same time: a robot might continue a topic with one participant, involve another through a question, or address the whole group, with the appropriate choice depending on both whom it addresses and the interaction context. Current approaches remain limited in representing uncertainty when several behaviours are plausible and in structuring decisions into semantically meaningful intermediate steps that make robot decisions easier to interpret. Addressing these, we present HiBRIDGE, a hierarchical Bayesian neural network framework for group-robot dialogue management. Its Bayesian formulation enables uncertainty-aware prediction and robust learning from limited interaction data, while the hierarchical approach formulates behaviour selection as a structured, multi-stage decision process. We further use decision-tree surrogates to investigate whether this structure can support more interpretable explanations. Across three offline group-HRI datasets, our findings show that Bayesian formulations outperform their deterministic counterparts and several state-of-the-art baselines. Next, through an online study (N=20), we show that explanations derived from the hierarchical model are rated as more helpful for understanding robot behaviour and are preferred over those derived from the flat model. Finally, through our in-person study (N=12), we demonstrate the feasibility of HiBRIDGE for autonomous real-time group interaction, with both hierarchical and flat Bayesian variants positively perceived. Overall, HiBRIDGE combines strong predictive performance with a structured decision process that supports more interpretable explanations of robot behaviour.

</details>


### [59] [BIFTA: Brain-Inspired Few-Shot Tactile Adaptation for Unknown Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08673)
*Boheng Liu,Ziyu Li,Xia Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出脑启发的小样本触觉适应框架（BIFTA），通过双视图统计记忆、支持条件谱图和不确定性门控循环传播，将冻结编码器适应到未知触觉传感器，仅用10%目标数据即可大幅提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同触觉传感器在光学设计、弹性体力学和成像几何上差异巨大，导致在已知传感器上训练的模型在未知传感器上性能骤降。

Method: 借鉴大脑快速感官适应机制，BIFTA框架包含：双视图统计记忆保留预训练表征，支持条件谱图修复传感器相关的特征邻域，不确定性门控循环传播增强可靠的跨查询证据。

Result: 在SITR数据集上仅用10%标注目标数据，将平均Sparsh准确率从6.86%提升到87.09%，超过最强基线47.22个百分点，并在多个数据集、预训练骨干和触觉任务上泛化。

Conclusion: BIFTA实现了对未知触觉传感器的数据高效适应，为跨异构硬件的触觉模型迁移提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Advances in tactile sensing have made contact-rich perception possible, accelerating progress in robotic manipulation, material understanding, and embodied interaction. However, because optical design, elastomer mechanics, and imaging geometry differ substantially across tactile sensors, models trained on known sensor types can suffer an abrupt performance collapse on unknown sensors. To address this problem, we propose the Brain-Inspired Few-Shot Tactile Adaptation (BIFTA) framework; it draws on the brain's rapid sensory adaptation mechanism to adapt a frozen encoder to an unknown tactile sensor from a small labeled support set. BIFTA preserves pretrained representations through dual-view statistical memory, constructs support-conditioned spectral graphs to repair sensor-dependent feature neighborhoods, and applies uncertainty-gated recurrent propagation to strengthen reliable cross-query evidence. Extensive benchmarks across three tactile datasets show that BIFTA substantially improves adaptation to unknown sensors: with only 10\% labeled target data on SITR, it raises mean Sparsh accuracy from 6.86\% for the frozen source classifier to 87.09\%, exceeding the strongest implemented prior comparison by 47.22 percentage points, and these gains generalize across datasets, pretrained backbones, and tactile tasks. These results validate BIFTA for data-efficient adaptation to unknown tactile sensors and offer a promising route toward tactile models that transfer across heterogeneous hardware.

</details>


### [60] [Learning to build covering structures with continuous adjustments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08669)
*Gabriel Vallat,Maryam Kamgarpour,Stefana Parascho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的自适应机器人建造方法（HSAC），无需预定义计划，通过图神经网络和混合动作空间生成序列，并在真实双机器人平台上成功搭建拱形结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造依赖刚性高精度计划，无法应对物理建造中的公差、误差和意外变化，需要自适应策略。

Method: 采用图结构状态表示和混合参数化动作空间（离散选块+连续放置）；在GNN中引入单边连接以提高稳定性模拟效率；将SAC扩展到混合动作设置（称为HSAC），实现探索策略。

Result: 与混合PPO（HPPO）相比，HSAC具有更高的渐近性能和样本效率；对超参数鲁棒，可处理多达10个离散动作而无性能下降；在真实双机器人上成功闭环搭建3D打印块构成的拱桥，验证仿真到现实的迁移。

Conclusion: 该方法证明完全放弃预定义计划、自适应生成建造序列在物理机器人上可行，且性能优于现有方法，为复杂结构建造提供新范式。

Abstract: Robotic construction offers the potential to use materials more efficiently and create complex geometries, but current methods rely on rigid, high-precision plans that cannot accommodate the tolerances, inaccuracies, and unexpected changes inherent in physical fabrication. In this work, we introduce a reinforcement learning approach that forgoes predefined plans entirely, instead generating construction sequences adaptively as the structure is built. Our method operates on graph-structured state representations and a mixed (parameterized) action space, requiring both discrete block selection and continuous placement parameters. Because the stability simulation of a structure is computationally heavy, we develop an efficient exploration strategy by incorporating unilateral edges into graph neural networks, extending soft actor-critic (SAC) to this hybrid setting. We evaluate our algorithm, HSAC, against the prior method hybrid-PPO (HPPO), demonstrating significantly higher asymptotic performance and good sample efficiency. We also demonstrate HSAC's robustness to hyperparameter choices and its exploration capability, handling up to 10 discrete actions without performance degradation. Finally, we validate our approach on a physical two-robot setup, successfully building a spanning arch with 3D-printed blocks in closed-loop execution, confirming that policies trained in simulation transfer to real hardware.

</details>


### [61] [CASD: Chunk-Aligned Semantic Distillation for Multi-StageRobot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08638)
*Tinghe Ding,Jiahao Li,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CASD，为整个动作块生成语义目标，无需在线VLM调用即可提升多种机器人操作策略的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作块可跨越操作任务的多个阶段，但仅标注第一步只能描述当前阶段，缺乏对整个动作块语义监督，限制了策略学习。

Method: 离线VLM将演示分割为带描述的阶段；根据动作块内各阶段占用计算加权语义目标（含阶段转换）；训练CASD生成器从观察、状态和任务指令预测该目标；冻结生成器后训练以其预测为条件的策略。语义分支每次策略查询只运行一次。

Result: 在LIBERO上IDM+CASD平均成功率98.9%（基准98.0%）；RoboTwin 2.0上Joint+CASD达93.0%，优于90.6%；MolmoSpaces四分类操作DreamZero+CASD达47.9%，优于40.7%；但不同骨干集成效果有差异，Uncond低于对应参考。

Conclusion: CASD通过动作块级语义蒸馏有效提升跨阶段操作策略性能，但需视骨干网络而定，并非所有集成均能超过参考。

Abstract: An action chunk can span several stages of a manipulation task, yet a label for its first step describes only the current stage. We introduce Chunk-Aligned Semantic Distillation (CASD), which derives semantic targets for entire action chunks. An offline vision--language model segments demonstrations into described stages. Their occupancy within each action chunk determines a weighted semantic target, including transitions between stages. A CASD generator learns to predict this target from the current observation, robot state, and task instruction. We then freeze the generator and train a policy conditioned on its predictions. The semantic branch runs once per policy query, without online VLM calls or reasoning-trace decoding. Teacher matching on annotated LIBERO training episodes is above chance for both single-stage and boundary-crossing chunks. We evaluate three Fast-WAM variants and a DreamZero integration across four benchmarks, including distribution shifts on LIBERO-Plus. Compared with published references, IDM+CASD reaches 98.9\% versus 98.0\% average success on LIBERO, while Uncond falls below its reference. Joint+CASD reaches 93.0\% versus 90.6\% on RoboTwin 2.0, and DreamZero+CASD reaches a 47.9\% four-category MolmoSpaces manipulation average versus 40.7\%. Performance varies across backbone integrations.

</details>


### [62] [MFVINS: Multiple Fisheye Camera-Based Visual Inertial System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08626)
*Eunseong Jang,YuJin Chung,Sang Jun Lee,Jihyun Yoon,HyungGi Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多鱼眼相机与低成本IMU的视觉惯性系统MFVINS，通过IMU辅助的FAST特征跟踪、鱼眼畸变离群点剔除以及基于学习深度估计的物理有效重投影误差，实现实时鲁棒的SLAM，在遮挡和无纹理环境下优于现有VINS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目相机与IMU融合的VINS易受遮挡、光照变化及无纹理环境影响，导致误差累积。

Method: 使用多鱼眼相机，提出IMU辅助的FAST特征跟踪器，在归一化图像平面剔除鱼眼失真引起的离群点，并设计带物理有效性约束的重投影误差进行基于学习深度估计的捆绑调整。

Result: 在多种场景下验证有效性，相比先前VINS方法性能更优，且实现实时处理。

Conclusion: MFVINS通过多相机配置增强了遮挡与无纹理区域的鲁棒性，同时控制计算负担，具备实时应用潜力。

Abstract: A simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) method using a monocular camera and a low-cost inertial measurement unit (IMU) sensor is an effective way to fulfill a low-cost sensor configuration. Using this sensor configuration, visual-inertial system (VINS) focuses on fusing data from a camera and an IMU sensor to estimate the six degrees-of-freedom (DOF) of the sensor pose. Typically, VINS uses only a single camera as visual input, which lead to problems such as error accumulation due to occlusion, various illumination, and textureless environments. In this paper, we propose a new multiple fisheye camera-based visual-inertial system called MFVINS. We present an IMU-aided FAST feature tracker for multiple cameras that enables efficient extraction and robust matching of local features. Then, the proposed method filters out outliers caused by fisheye distortion on the normalized image plane. Subsequently, a new reprojection error with physical validity constraints is proposed for bundle adjustment using learning-based depth estimation. The proposed method is applied to various scenarios, and its effectiveness is demonstrated by comparing previous VINS methods. In particular, MFVINS is implemented in real-time process to leverage the advantages of using multiple cameras -- robustness against occlusion and textureless regions -- while reducing the computational burden.

</details>


### [63] [Estimating Semantic Ambiguity via Gaussian Context Distributions for VLM-Driven Traversability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08583)
*Ramona Häuselmann,Mario A.V. Saucedo,Christoforos Kanellakis,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于概念锚定和高斯上下文分布的视觉可通行性估计方法，显式建模语义不确定性，并在GOOSE数据集上验证了其与歧义源的相关性及安全导航优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航在非结构化环境中需要鲁棒场景理解，但视觉语言模型常受语义歧义影响，冲突预测可能导致危险故障，现有方法未显式处理这种不确定性。

Method: 利用概念锚定将开放词汇VLM预测映射到连续物理可通行性尺度，将模型响应建模为高斯上下文分布，从而导出稠密可通行性图和稠密不确定性图。

Result: 在真实GOOSE数据集上的实验表明，所提不确定性度量能有效关联视觉伪影和混合地形重叠等歧义来源，性能具有竞争力并提供了统计不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该方法通过统计不确定性显式应对语义歧义，提升了复杂户外环境中自主行为的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in unstructured environments requires robust scene understanding, yet Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often suffer from semantic ambiguity, where conflicting predictions can lead to dangerous failures. To address this, we present a novel pipeline for vision-based traversability estimation that explicitly models contextual uncertainty. Our approach utilizes Conceptual Anchoring to ground open-vocabulary VLM predictions onto a continuous physical traversability scale. By formulating the model's responses as a Gaussian Context Distribution (GCD), we derive both a dense traversability map and a dense uncertainty map based on the statistical properties of the distribution. Experimental validation on the real-world GOOSE dataset demonstrates that our proposed uncertainty metric effectively correlates with sources of ambiguity, such as visual artifacts and mixed terrain overlap. The method exhibits competitive performance while offering the distinct advantage of providing statistical uncertainty estimates to address semantic ambiguity, enabling safer and more reliable autonomous behavior in complex outdoor settings.

</details>


### [64] [TASG-Explore: Traversability-Aware Sector-Guided Exploration for Ground Robot on Uneven Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08512)
*Shaocong Wang,Shiliang Shao,Ting Wang,Guangjie Han,Lianqing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TASG-Explore框架，通过分层可通行性分析与扇区引导规划，在崎岖地形中实现高效、完整且安全的自主探索，显著提升处理效率和覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面机器人在不平坦地形自主探索需权衡效率、覆盖完整性与安全性：精细地形推理提升局部可靠性但拖慢大规模探索，粗略区域引导扩展快但易遗漏狭窄通道与不规则边界。

Method: 采用可变体素地面拟合与自适应8位障碍编码进行分层可通行性分析；将代价地图分割为扇区，增量更新扇区簇并提取地形耦合前沿点，维护含未知拓扑假设的动态拓扑路图；扇区引导规划器选择区域目标并插入局部视点生成高效探索路径。

Result: 在洞穴、森林、崎岖山丘等环境中对比六个SOTA规划器，综合性能最优；可通行性分析处理速度提升6.3倍且保持高精度，探索效率提升51%，崎岖山丘场景覆盖率最高提升2.95倍；大规模真实实验验证实用性。

Conclusion: TASG-Explore有效融合精细地形分析与粗粒度区域引导，兼顾探索效率与安全性，适用于复杂崎岖地形的自主探索任务。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration on uneven terrain requires ground robots to balance exploration efficiency, coverage completeness, and terrain safety. Detailed tsrrain reasoning improves local reliability but can slow large-scale exploration, whereas coarse region guidance expands quickly in open areas but can miss narrow passages and irregular traversable boundaries. To address this challenge, this paper presents TASG-Explore, a traversability-aware sector-guided exploration framework for ground robots. The framework first performs hierarchical traversability analysis using variable-voxel ground fitting and adaptive 8-bit obstacle encoding. It then splitting cost map into sectors, incrementally updates sector clusters, extracts terrain-coupled frontier viewpoints, and maintains a dynamic topological roadmap with unknown topological hypotheses. Finally, a sector-guided planner selects region targets and inserts local viewpoints to generate efficient exploration routes. Benchmark experiments in diverse challenging environments, including caves, forests, and rugged hills, show that TASG-Explore achieves the best overall performance among six representative state-of-the-art planners. The proposed traversability analysis improves processing efficiency by 6.3 times while maintaining high accuracy, and the exploration planner improves exploration efficiency by 51% and increases coverage by up to 2.95 times in rugged hill scene. Large-scale real-world experiments further demonstrate the practical value of the proposed method.

</details>


### [65] [PGMT: Perceptive General Motion Tracking for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08511)
*Hongyi Li,Li Peizhuo,Yucheng Tao,Ze Wang,Fangzhou Xu,Jinyi Chen,Yanyan Yuan,Dapeng Jia,Yongbin Jin,Mingfeng Fan,Guillaume Sartoretti,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PGMT，一种感知型通用运动跟踪流程，使类人机器人能在复杂地形上执行全身运动。通过运动条件化地形感知和地形感知的跟踪松弛，在Unitree G1上零样本部署，实现了高达37厘米障碍物地形的鲁棒适应与全身运动，支持遥操作和摔倒恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人运动跟踪器可复现多样全身运动，但在复杂地形上性能下降，因为与地形无关的参考动作在物理上不可行。需要让机器人感知地形并自适应调整动作。

Method: PGMT分两阶段：先学习通用跟踪与恢复先验；再通过运动条件化地形快照（motion-conditioned terrain glimpses）整合地形感知，选择性编码与当前动作相关的地形区域。利用地形感知的跟踪松弛（terrain-aware tracking relaxation）允许偏离参考但不失动作意图。

Result: 在Unitree G1上零样本部署，在含高达37厘米障碍物的真实地形上展现鲁棒地形自适应运动与全身动作执行，支持遥操作、动态运动跟踪和摔倒恢复。

Conclusion: PGMT将通用类人运动跟踪从平地扩展到复杂地形，为地形自适应运动、多样全身行为和复杂环境遥操作提供统一策略。

Abstract: Humanoid motion trackers can reproduce diverse whole-body motions, but their performance degrades on complex terrain where terrain-agnostic references become physically infeasible. We present PGMT, a Perceptive General Motion Tracking pipeline for humanoid robots that learns terrain adaptation from independently selected motion references and terrains. PGMT first learns a general tracking and recovery prior, then incorporates terrain perception through motion-conditioned terrain glimpses that selectively encode regions relevant to the current motion. Terrain-aware tracking relaxation allows necessary deviations from the reference while preserving its motion intent. Zero-shot deployment on a Unitree G1 demonstrates robust terrain-adaptive locomotion and whole-body motion execution over real-world terrain with obstacles up to 37 cm high, while supporting teleoperation, dynamic motion tracking, and fall recovery. PGMT extends general humanoid motion tracking beyond flat ground, providing a unified policy for terrain-adaptive locomotion, diverse whole-body behaviors, and teleoperation in complex environments.

</details>


### [66] [AURORA: Active Uncertainty-Driven Re-Orientation for In-Hand Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08493)
*Feiyu Zhao,Yuetong Li,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AURORA是一个主动3D重建框架，通过Ray-GPIS估计候选视线方向的重建不确定性，并结合不确定性-新颖性目标选择下一最佳视角，再以轴条件手内旋转策略执行重定向，从而闭环地提升物体重建质量与信息获取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手抓取物体会造成严重视觉遮挡，现有手内操作策略多采用预定义或开环重定向，未显式针对未被充分观察的区域进行探索，导致重建不完整。

Method: AURORA采用主动重建与重定向闭环框架：核心为Ray-GPIS，沿候选视线估计方向性重建不确定性，并通过不确定性-新颖性目标选出下一最佳视角；该视角由轴条件手内旋转策略实现。RGB-D观测通过无CAD的6D姿态跟踪和轻量级几何重建增量融合。

Result: 实验表明AURORA在重建质量和信息采集效率上优于非主动旋转策略；Ray-GPIS在重建性能、动作排序质量和规划效率上超过主动视角规划基线。消融实验验证了方法对遮挡和姿态误差的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AURORA通过显式目标驱动的主动重定向有效提升了物体重建质量与效率，Ray-GPIS作为视角规划器具有显著优势，且方法在实际遮挡和姿态扰动下表现稳健。

Abstract: Observing objects grasped by a robot hand is challenging due to severe visual occlusions. Although in-hand manipulation can expose hidden surfaces, existing approaches often rely on predefined or open-loop reorientation strategies that do not explicitly target under-observed regions. We propose AURORA, an active 3D reconstruction framework that closes the loop between online object-centric reconstruction and in-hand reorientation. At its core, Ray-GPIS estimates direction-wise reconstruction uncertainty along candidate viewing rays and selects next-best-view targets using an uncertainty--novelty objective, which are realized through an axis-conditioned in-hand rotation policy. The resulting RGB-D observations are fused incrementally using CAD-free 6D pose tracking and lightweight geometric reconstruction. Experiments demonstrate that AURORA improves reconstruction quality and information-acquisition efficiency over non-active rotation strategies, while Ray-GPIS also outperforms active view-planning baselines in reconstruction performance, action-ranking quality, and planning efficiency. Targeted ablations further validate its robustness to hand occlusion and pose errors. The project webpage is available at this https URL

</details>


### [67] [Multi-bounce Drum Roll with Optimized Active Tricks to Leverage Soft Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08490)
*Naoto Yamanaka,Takanori Jin,Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种软体机器人鼓手，通过“敲击-拉动”（TP）和“微脉冲”（MP）两种技巧，结合贝叶斯优化调控软体弹性，实现了高频、均匀且高效的鼓滚奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速鼓滚需要“多次反弹”技巧，要求鼓槌在一次挥击内多次反弹。机器人复现时，身体弹性是关键，但击打过程中的精细运动对发挥弹性潜力也至关重要。

Method: 设计了两种技巧：TP（击打后增加拉动动作以增加反弹次数）和MP（击打过程中注入小幅振荡以保持音量）。由于软体非线性复杂性，采用贝叶斯优化以数据驱动方式高效调整参数，分别优化反弹次数和音量保持目标。

Result: 软体TP实现了最高反弹次数（每击12.25次）且间隔均匀；软体MP抑制了音量衰减，声学效率比刚性MP高6.8倍。

Conclusion: 所提技巧结合弹性与优化，可使机器人实现出色的鼓滚演奏。

Abstract: This paper presents a soft robotic drummer for accurate and efficient drum rolls. High-frequency drum rolls require the "multi-bounce technique," where a drumstick bounces multiple times with a single stroke. In robotic reproduction of this technique, the body's elasticity is key, while the fine motion during the stroke is also crucial for maximizing the potential of that elasticity. Therefore, we design two tricks: i) Tap-Pull (TP) trick to increase the number of rebounds by adding a pulling motion after impact; and ii) Micro-Pulse (MP) trick to keep the drumming volume by injecting small oscillations during the stroke. Due to the nonlinear complexity of soft embodiment, both tricks are efficiently tuned using Bayesian optimization in a data-driven manner for accomplishing the respective objectives quantified. We evaluated the optimized behaviors with soft and rigid end-effectors. As a result, the soft TP achieved the highest bounce count (12.25 per stroke) with uniform intervals. The soft MP suppressed the volume decay, yielding 6.8-times higher acoustic efficiency compared to the rigid MP. These results indicate that the proposed tricks with the combination of elasticity and optimization can make robots play excellent drum rolls.

</details>


### [68] [Safe Task Planning with Long-Term Graph Memory for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08444)
*Siyuan Li,Taiyan Lang,Aoqi Yan,Jia Yu,Feifan Liu,Yihan Du,Yu Zheng,Xun Wang,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SafeMem 框架，通过构建和维护环境的长时语义图记忆，结合 LLM 风险预测器与保守性调制重规划循环，提升具身智能体在部分可观测条件下零样本任务规划的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM/VLM 驱动的任务规划器缺乏物理风险意识，尤其在部分可观测环境中，危险可能位于视野之外，导致生成不安全的高层动作。

Method: SafeMem 基于第一人称观测增量构建环境对象及其关系的语义图记忆；LLM 风险预测器利用该图记忆评估候选动作，并在检测到危险时触发带有解释的保守性调制重规划循环。

Result: 在 IS-Bench 基准和真实机器人平台上，SafeMem 相比最先进的 VLM 驱动任务规划器显著提高了安全成功率。

Conclusion: SafeMem 通过长期语义图记忆与风险感知重规划机制，有效弥补了现有规划器在物理风险意识上的不足，表明该方法能提升开放动态环境下具身智能体的安全任务规划能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) have significantly advanced zero-shot task planning for embodied agents. However, most LLM- and VLM-driven methods struggle to generate safe high-level actions due to a lack of physical risk awareness, particularly under partial observability, where hazards lie outside the immediate field of view. To address this challenge, we propose a novel safe task-planning framework, SafeMem, which constructs and maintains a long-term semantic graph memory of the open and dynamic environment. Based on egocentric observations, the proposed framework incrementally accumulates knowledge about surrounding objects and their relationships with a graph. Then, an LLM-based risk predictor evaluates candidate actions using the graph memory, triggering a conservatism-modulated replanning loop with explanations for detected hazards. Extensive experiments on the IS-Bench benchmark and a real-world robot platform demonstrate that the SafeMem framework substantially improves safe success rates compared to state-of-the-art VLM-driven task planners. Video results are available on our webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [69] [Coverage Path Planning for Redundant Manipulators using Generalized Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08409)
*Raksi Kopo,Kostas J. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将经典生成树覆盖（STC）扩展到冗余机械臂的联合生成树覆盖（JSTC）方法，包含离线与在线两个版本，分别解决静态全覆盖路径规划与动态环境下的快速规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冗余机械臂进行表面覆盖时，每个表面点可能存在多个逆运动学（IK）解，配置选择显著影响运动质量，现有方法难以高效处理这种任务冗余性。

Method: 离线JSTC：对每个网格单元采样多个IK解，将问题建模为广义最小生成树（GMST），为每个单元选择一个配置并追踪生成树，得到无重复覆盖路径。在线JSTC：通过增量扩展与回溯生成树，结合可行性和代价评估，支持动态网格更新。

Result: 仿真结果表明，离线JSTC相比其他方法减少了计算时间、重新配置次数和关节运动量；在线JSTC在动态场景中实现了快速的逐步规划。

Conclusion: JSTC方法有效解决了冗余机械臂的表面覆盖问题，兼顾了离线规划效率与动态场景适应性，为任务冗余机械臂的覆盖路径规划提供了新思路。

Abstract: Surface coverage with task-redundant manipulators is challenging because each surface point may admit multiple inverse kinematics (IK) solutions, and configuration choices strongly affect motion quality. This paper extends the classical Spanning Tree Coverage (STC) method to redundant manipulators through offline and online Joint Spanning Tree Coverage (JSTC) algorithms. Offline JSTC samples multiple Inverse Kinematics (IK) solutions per grid cell and formulates the problem as a Generalized Minimum Spanning Tree (GMST), selecting one configuration per cell and tracing the resulting tree to obtain a non-revisiting coverage path. Online JSTC incrementally expands and backtracks a spanning tree with feasibility and cost evaluation while handling dynamic grid updates. Simulation results show that offline JSTC reduces computation time, reconfigurations, and joint motion compared to other methods, while online JSTC achieves fast per-step planning in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [70] [Localized Visual Feature Aggregation via Focus Pooling for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08408)
*Ruiyu Wang,Zheyu Zhuang,Danica Kragic,Florian T. Pokorny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FocusPool，一种基于机器人本体感觉上下文选择性聚合CNN中间特征的注意力池化模块，直接利用编码器中的局部控制相关视觉信息，无需输入裁剪，显著提升策略成功率并大幅减少训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过输入预处理（如裁剪）聚焦空间局部控制线索以提高数据效率，但未充分探索是否可直接从CNN编码特征中提取这类局部特征；常用池化方法无法有效聚合中间层保留的局部控制上下文。

Method: FocusPool是注意力池化模块，根据当前机器人本体感觉上下文选择性地聚合CNN中间层视觉特征，生成捕获任务进展且控制相关的局部信息表示，直接用于策略学习。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，相比池化方法和显式局部聚焦方法，FocusPool将策略成功率分别提高36.2%和41.2%，且仅需训练编码器参数的5.8%。

Conclusion: FocusPool证明中间CNN特征可直接暴露控制相关的局部上下文，通过本体感觉引导的注意力池化可实现高效、数据高效的视觉运动策略学习。

Abstract: Focusing on spatially localized, control-relevant visual cues has been shown to improve data efficiency in visuomotor policies by reducing the need to model task-irrelevant visual variation. Existing methods often impose this focus through input preprocessing, such as cropping control- or object-centric regions in RGB images or point-clouds. However, it remains underexplored whether such localized features can be exposed directly from commonly used convolutional neural network (CNN) encoded features. In this paper, we show that intermediate CNN features preserve localized visual context for control, but existing pooling methods fail to aggregate it effectively. We introduce FocusPool, an attention pooling module that selectively aggregates intermediate visual features according to their relevance to the robot's current proprioceptive context. The resulting pooled representation captures task-progressive, control-relevant local information and is used directly for policy learning. Across simulation and real-world experiments, FocusPool improves policy success rates over pooling and explicit local focus methods by 36.2% and 41.2%, with training only 5.8% of encoder parameters.

</details>


### [71] [RoboCousin: Build Your Own Simulation Playground for Robust Bimanual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08339)
*Jingxuan Zhu,Jingyi Li,LiangLiang Chen,Zhiyuan Jing,Jidong Zhang,Hongming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboCousin是一个基于仿真的数据生成平台，可将用户提供的观测图像自动转换为可用于双臂操作的可复用资产、场景和专家轨迹，并生成多样化“数字孪生”以扩展训练数据规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作策略需要大量多样化的训练数据，但物理机器人演示收集昂贵且难扩展；现有仿真管线局限于封闭资产库和预设场景，新增物体或环境需大量人工重建和标注。

Method: RoboCousin基于RoboTwin 2.0构建，从物体图像自动生成具备视觉/碰撞几何、语义/物理元数据及抓取接触候选的仿真资产；构造保留任务相关功能与空间关系的数字变体（对象、背景、布局、语言指令）；支持桌面级和房间级场景构建，并采用碰撞感知的基座控制以支持非固定工作空间的交互。

Result: 发布RoboCousin-OBD数据集（3000+标注物体实例、50个背景环境），生成覆盖50个任务的超100万条专家轨迹。仿真与真机实验表明：自动生成交互标注与人工标注质量相当；生成资产能有效提供sim-to-real监督；桌面数字变体能提升策略从单重建场景到新场景的迁移性能。

Conclusion: RoboCousin为扩展合成双臂操作数据的规模与覆盖面提供了实用路径，有望降低对物理演示的依赖并提升策略泛化能力。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation policies require large and diverse training datasets, yet collecting demonstrations on physical robots is expensive and difficult to scale. Simulation can generate data efficiently, but existing pipelines typically operate within closed asset libraries and predefined scenes: adding a newly observed object or environment still requires substantial effort to reconstruct geometry, specify physical and semantic properties, annotate interactions, and integrate the result into executable tasks. We present RoboCousin, an extensible simulation-based data-generation platform that turns user-provided observations into reusable assets, scenes, and expert trajectories for bimanual manipulation. Built on RoboTwin~2.0, RoboCousin converts object images into simulation-ready assets with visual and collision geometry, semantic and physical metadata, and automatically generated grasp-contact candidates. It further constructs digital cousins that vary compatible objects, backgrounds, layouts, and language instructions while preserving task-relevant affordances and spatial relations. The same asset system supports tabletop and room-level scene construction, with collision-aware base control for interaction beyond a fixed workspace. We release RoboCousin-OBD, containing more than 3,000 annotated object instances and 50 background environments, and use RoboCousin to generate over one million expert trajectories across 50 tasks. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that the automatically generated interaction annotations are comparable to curated annotations, generated assets provide effective sim-to-real supervision, and tabletop cousins can improve transfer beyond training on a single reconstructed scene. RoboCousin therefore provides a practical path for expanding both the scale and coverage of synthetic bimanual manipulation data.

</details>


### [72] [A Multi-Modal Perception Pipeline for Object Detection and Tracking in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08338)
*Davide Malvezzi,Michele Pestarino,Vittoria Cavicchioli,Valentina La Gamba,Silvia Severi,Fabio Bagni,Luca Bartoli,Massimiliano Bosi,Francesco Gatti,Micaela Verucchi,Ayoub Raji,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向自动驾驶赛车场景的多模态晚期融合感知流程，结合相机、LiDAR和RADAR的独立检测结果，并通过显式补偿检测延迟及嵌入车辆动力学与赛道布局先验的多目标跟踪框架，实现对周围车辆的鲁棒状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知在恶劣条件（如能见度受限、传感器噪声和故障）下仍具挑战，尤其自动驾驶赛车车速高、振动强、安全余量小，现有方法在动态极端场景中鲁棒性不足，需利用所有车载传感器并解决检测延迟与运动先验融合问题。

Method: 采用多模态晚期融合（late-fusion）感知流程：先由相机、LiDAR和RADAR各自生成独立检测，再通过专门的多目标跟踪框架融合；跟踪过程显式补偿检测延迟，并在模型中嵌入车辆动力学与赛道布局的先验知识，以输出及时且鲁棒的周围车辆状态估计。

Result: 在真实世界多种关键场景（涵盖极具挑战的边角案例，类似城市驾驶）上的实验验证了该流程的有效性，表明其能够支持安全且自适应的规划决策。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态晚期融合跟踪流程在自动驾驶赛车领域表现良好，甚至在类似城市驾驶的困难场景下也能提供可靠的感知结果，适合作为安全与自适应规划的基础。

Abstract: Object detection and tracking are fundamental components of perception systems for autonomous driving. Achieving robust performance under adverse conditions such as limited visibility, sensor noise, and failures remains an open challenge, particularly in autonomous racing, where vehicles operate at very high speeds, experience strong vibrations, and interact under small safety margins. This paper presents a multi-modal late-fusion perception pipeline for object detection and tracking in the autonomous racing domain. The proposed system extends previous work by exploiting all onboard sensors through a late-fusion approach and a dedicated multi-object tracking framework. Independent detections from cameras, LiDARs, and RADARs are combined to provide timely and robust state estimates of surrounding vehicles. The tracking method explicitly compensates for detection delays and embeds in its model prior knowledge of vehicle dynamics and track layout. Experimental evaluation on real-world data across diverse critical scenarios, representative of challenging edge cases also in urban driving, confirms the effectiveness of the proposed pipeline and its suitability to support safe and adaptive planning decisions.

</details>


### [73] [EvoNav-Bench: Benchmarking Lifelong Navigation in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08292)
*Xilin Wang,Guoxi Zhang,Hongming Xu,Zhuofan Zhang,Tianxu Wang,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 EvoNav-Bench，一个在动态变化环境中评估终身导航（LN）智能体的基准。该基准在子任务之间引入环境修改，使得先前的经验有用但不完全可靠。实验表明，现有基于场景表示复用的 LN 方法在此设置下表现脆弱，而简单的启发式策略（如 Frontier-Update、Fail-then-Update、Stage-Reset）能够提供可控的适应分析并缓解环境变化带来的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身导航（LN）方法通常假设环境静止，但真实场景中人类活动会导致环境变化。当环境变化后，智能体融合过时的旧观测和新观测会造成失败，而现有基准无法暴露这一缺陷。因此需要一个新的基准来评估 LN 智能体在环境演化下的表现。

Method: 基于 ProcTHOR 框架，EvoNav-Bench 扩展了 GOAT-Bench 风格的 LN 任务，在导航子任务之间加入环境修改（如物体移动、布局变化），使先验经验部分失效但仍有参考价值。该基准支持受控评估。作者在基准上测试了三种利用场景表示的最新方法，并对比了三种简单启发式策略：Frontier-Update（更新前沿边界）、Fail-then-Update（失败后再更新）、Stage-Reset（阶段重置）。

Result: 实验显示，现有方法在环境演化下表现脆弱，性能显著下降；而三种启发式策略能够主动适应场景变化，提供了分析智能体如何应对环境变化的控制组，并减轻了环境演化带来的负面影响。

Conclusion: EvoNav-Bench 揭示了现有 LN 方法在演化环境中的不足，并表明在场景变化后及时更新或重置记忆的重要性。该基准为未来研究更鲁棒的终身导航智能体提供了测试平台和基线策略。

Abstract: Lifelong navigation (LN) requires an embodied agent to solve a sequence of navigation subtasks in the same environment. Since solving each subtask from scratch incurs redundant exploration, an LN agent must consolidate experience from earlier stages and reuse it in later stages, often through persistent scene representations such as scene graphs or visual snapshots. However, existing approaches typically assume a stationary environment, whereas in real-world LN settings, human activities can cause the environment to evolve. With the stationary assumption violated, existing methods may fuse outdated prior observations with new observations, yet current benchmarks cannot reveal this failure mode. In this paper, we present EvoNav-Bench, which extends the GOAT-Bench style LN formulation in the context of evolving environments. Built on the ProcTHOR framework, EvoNav-Bench introduces environment modifications between navigation tasks, making prior experience useful but not fully reliable. This design enables controlled evaluation of how environment evolution affects LN agents that reuse prior scene observations. Using EvoNav-Bench, we benchmark three recent methods that build and reuse scene representations for navigation. We also compare three simple heuristic strategies for handling environment evolution: Frontier-Update, Fail-then-Update, and Stage-Reset. Our results show that existing methods are brittle under environment evolution, while the heuristic strategies enable a controlled analysis of how agents can adapt to scene changes and mitigate their impact.

</details>


### [74] [Seeing is Not Believing: Breaking the Physical-to-Digital Trust Boundary in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08280)
*Leming Shen,Shikai Geng,Yuanqing Zheng,Chris Xiaoxuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了ROS 2中的一个严重漏洞：攻击者可通过修改单个环境变量，利用预挂接的钩子隐蔽拦截和注入遥测与控制信号，从而在欺骗下游验证器的同时劫持机器人执行危险任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作中的任务交接依赖下游验证器进行远程证明，而远程证明通过检查传感器遥测来确保机器人行为匹配任务。但研究质疑这些遥测数据的可信性，并发现通常不可信。

Method: 作者发现ROS 2中的漏洞：修改一个环境变量即可执行预构建的钩子，在发布前拦截并注入遥测和控制信号。攻击还能利用第三方Docker容器和辅助工具的广泛依赖，分发嵌入恶意钩子的受损包，从而简化攻击实施。

Result: 在物理Franka Emika机械臂上运行Secure ROS 2时，攻击可实时注入伪造遥测，抖动仅约3毫秒，保持时间同步和硬件完整性，并针对基于AI的检测器实现87%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该攻击展示了ROS 2遥测信任的严重缺陷，已负责任地向ROS 2开发团队披露，并提供演示视频。

Abstract: In multi-robot collaboration, task handovers rely on downstream verifiers performing remote attestation, which inspects sensor telemetry to ensure a robot's physical behavior strictly matches its assigned task. But can this telemetry be trusted? We show that it often cannot. In this paper, we uncover a severe vulnerability in Robot Operating System (ROS) 2: by modifying a single environment variable, an adversary can execute a pre-built hook to covertly intercept and inject both telemetry and control signals before they are published. Consequently, adversaries can hijack a robot to perform dangerous tasks while spoofing downstream verifiers with synthesized fake telemetry. Worse still, by exploiting the widespread reliance on third-party Docker containers and auxiliary tools, attackers can distribute compromised packages embedded with these malicious hooks to launch such attacks easily. On a physical Franka Emika robotic arm running Secure ROS 2, our attack injects fabricated telemetry in real time with only around 3 ms of jitter, preserving temporal synchronization and hardware integrity while achieving an 87% success rate even against an AI-based detector. We have responsibly disclosed these findings to the ROS 2 development team. We prepared a demo video available at this https URL .

</details>
