### [75] [CALIPER: Clean Scenes Cannot Rank Physical Inference in Pretrained Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08250)
*Aman Mehta,Riya Baviskar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CALIPER直接测试评估视觉编码器的物理推理能力；固定干净场景下标准评估无法区分编码器是否真正推断物理，重采样场景后性能差异显现，线性探针不可靠，基准能否排序模型需经验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉编码器常作为操作世界模型的感知前端，但现有物理能力评估（扰动基准、线性探针）几乎都在干净固定相机场景中进行，无法区分真正推断物理的编码器和仅依赖像素位移等表面线索的编码器。

Method: CALIPER流程：未知质量和摩擦的物体被以已知速度撞击两次，第三次展示至接触瞬间，用冻结特征的线性读出预测滑动距离；通过交换另一物体的校准片段来验证是否真正使用物理证据。在2000个模拟片段、八种表示（V-JEPA 2、随机初始ViT、原始像素等）上比较固定场景与重采样相机/光照/杂乱场景，并加入线性探针和基于目标距离选择推速的任务。

Result: 校准片段使R^2提升+0.50，交换校准片段则消除该增益；固定干净场景下所有表示都接近真实模拟状态上限（差距≤0.02 R^2），而重采样场景后表示间R^2散布达0.50。推速选择任务中V-JEPA 2误差4mm，随机ViT误差20mm（与忽略物体相当）。线性探针受帧聚合方式影响超过预训练影响，擦除被探针的质量方向代价在一个场景为0、另一场景为0.35 R^2。

Conclusion: CALIPER能有效区分编码器的物理推理能力；基准能否对模型排序是经验属性，线性探针不能作为物理感知的可靠证据，应通过三类检查（校准增益、交换消除、场景重采样）来验证基准有效性。

Abstract: How far a pushed object slides depends on its mass and friction, which no single image reveals. Pretrained visual encoders are increasingly used as the perception front end of world models for manipulation, and their physical competence is assessed with perturbation benchmarks and linear probes, almost always in a clean, fixed-camera scene. We show that these assessments cannot distinguish an encoder that infers physics from one that does not. CALIPER (calibrate, then predict) is a direct test: an object of unknown mass and friction is struck twice at known speeds, a third strike is shown only up to the moment of contact, and a linear readout on frozen features must predict how far the object slides. Swapping in another object's calibration clips checks that the evidence is actually used. Across 2,000 simulated episodes and eight representations, from V-JEPA 2 to a randomly initialised ViT and raw pixels, calibration adds +0.50 R^2 and the swap removes it. Yet in the clean scene every representation lands within 0.02 R^2 of the ceiling set by true simulator state, because a fixed camera exposes the object's displacement directly in pixel coordinates. Resampling camera, lighting, and clutter for every clip spreads the same representations across 0.50 R^2; when the readout chooses a push speed for a goal distance, V-JEPA 2 misses by 4 mm and the random ViT by 20 mm, no better than ignoring the object. Linear probes track none of this: a change in frame aggregation moves a probe more than pretraining does, and erasing the probed mass direction from the same representation costs nothing in one scene and 0.35 R^2 in the other. Whether a benchmark can rank models is an empirical property, and we give three checks that establish it.

</details>


### [76] [3DWay: Generalizing Robot Manipulation via 3D Consistent Waypoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08224)
*Ziqin Huang,Yingyue Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Ruida Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Gu Wang,Xingyu Liu,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从多视角图像预测3D一致路标点（3DWay），通过将3D预测重构为多视角一致的2D路标点生成与几何三角化，实现显式3D运动规范，同时保留预训练VLM的强先验，提升机器人的空间接地与操作泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹表征能紧凑表达运动线索，但现有方法多在2D图像空间预测轨迹，存在固有的3D模糊性；即使结合深度也难确定自由空间路标点。为解决可靠的3D推理问题，需要直接预测3D一致的路标点。

Method: 3DWay：从多视角图像输入，先预测多视角一致的2D路标点，再利用几何三角化恢复3D路标点，从而把3D路标点预测转化为生成问题，并利用预训练VLM的强先验；预测结果可指导现有VLA模型或直接执行简单任务。

Result: 大量实验表明3DWay显著改善3D空间接地和视觉语言推理，展现强力的机器人操作泛化潜力。

Conclusion: 3DWay通过显式3D运动规范有效弥合操作策略与大尺度VLM之间的模态鸿沟，为通用机器人操作提供可行范式，代码将开源。

Abstract: Intermediate representations are key to bridging the modality gap between generalizable manipulation policies and large-scale pretrained vision-language models (VLMs). Among these, trajectory-based representations compactly represent motion-relevant cues, yet most existing approaches predict trajectories in 2D image space, resulting in intrinsic 3D ambiguity. Moreover, using 2D trajectories with depth still leaves the free-space waypoints ambiguous, limiting reliable 3D reasoning. To address this, we propose predicting 3D consistent waypoints (3DWay) from multi-view images. By reformulating 3D waypoints prediction as generating multi-view consistent 2D waypoints followed by geometric triangulation, we enable explicit 3D motion specification while preserving the strong priors of pretrained VLMs. The predicted waypoints can guide existing VLA models for better generalization or be directly executed on simple tasks. Extensive experiments show that 3DWay substantially improves 3D spatial grounding and vision-language reasoning, demonstrating strong potential for generalizable robot manipulation. Codes will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [77] [Bridging Language and Physics: Automated Design of Continuum Robots with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08220)
*Jingyi Chen,Mohan Zhang,Laura Yao,Yingtai Ni,Jianmin Ji,Jie Peng,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多层级AID-SR框架，通过模拟物理状态反馈和迭代细化，使大型语言模型能设计出物理可行且功能有效的肌腱驱动连续体机器人，在仿真和真实实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型用于机器人设计虽具潜力，但在复杂物理交互下因语言推理与物理结果之间存在差距，导致生成设计物理有效性低。

Method: AID-SR框架包含多层级闭环：将模拟器观察到的物理状态转化为结构化反馈给LLM设计者，并结合语义批评、人类反馈和迭代细化，以提高设计物理可行性与功能意义。

Result: 在14个任务（覆盖到达、抓取、移动和操作）中达到96.2%的模拟可行性通过率；结合通用强化学习训练，26.7%的机器人能成功完成任务；并将其中三个设计制作成实体，在真实世界中成功完成对应任务。

Conclusion: 仿真与真实实验表明，AID-SR打破LLM用于连续体机器人自动化设计的障碍，证明该方法能生成物理可行且功能有效的机器人。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently emerged as a promising tool for automating robot design from high-level specifications, yet they remain ineffective for robots operating under complex physical interactions. This limitation stems from the gap between language-based reasoning and the physical consequences of embodiment, often resulting in designs with low physical validity. In this work, we propose a multi-layered framework, AID-SR, that establishes a closed loop by translating simulator-observed physical states into structured feedback for the LLM designer. Combined with semantic critique, human feedback, and iterative refinement, the framework promotes the generation of physically feasible and functionally meaningful robot designs. We evaluate our approach on tendon-driven continuum robots across a benchmark of 14 tasks spanning reaching, grasping, locomotion, and manipulation. The proposed framework achieves 96.2% rate for passing the simulation feasibility check and by applying a common reinforcement learning training, 26.7% robots can successfully fulfill the corresponding task. We then fabricate three designed robots of AID-SR that successfully complete the task in real-world. These extensive experiments across simulation and real-world environments demonstrate and break the wall of utilizing the LLMs for automated design of continuum robots. The source code and experimental resources are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [78] [TacClip: a clip-on sensor measures dynamic contact forces without covering the fingerpads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08214)
*Yuqian Ye,Hao Li,Jingxi Xu,Haojun Feng,Seongheon Hong,Mark R. Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacClip是一种最小化穿戴的指尖传感器，利用光纤布拉格光栅记录指尖形变，与视觉/手套追踪兼容，保留触觉，力误差<0.5N，支持水下使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部追踪系统无法捕捉动态接触信息，且覆盖指尖会影响自然触觉，需要一种不遮挡指尖且能记录接触力/振动的可穿戴方案。

Method: 将FBG嵌入小型塑料夹并佩戴于指甲上，可选聚酰亚胺条补充视觉追踪；通过指尖形变测量力和振动，无电子元件故可水下使用。

Result: 按压测试中0–8N范围内典型误差低于0.5N；布料处理和胶带边缘查找测试成功捕获滑动期间的振动和动态事件。

Conclusion: TacClip为指尖动态交互提供轻量、防水、保留触觉的测量手段，适合与手部追踪结合。

Abstract: TacClip is a minimally encumbering wearable device for recording fingertip deformation caused by contact forces and vibrations. It can be combined with vision- or glove-based hand tracking systems that leave the fingertips uncovered and provides a measure of dynamic contact interactions, while leaving the finger pads exposed so that the user retains natural sensitivity to texture, friction, temperature, and fine surface features. The signal is produced by a Fiber Bragg Grating (FBG) embedded on a small plastic clip mounted over the fingernail. Optionally, for use with vision-based tracking, additional FBGs on polyimide strips can complement camera-based pose estimation. In finger pressing tests, TacClip estimates the force magnitude with typical errors below $0.5~\mathrm{N}$ over a $0$--$8~\mathrm{N}$ range. In tests of cloth handling and tape edge finding, we show that it captures the vibrations and dynamic events generated during exploratory sliding. With no electronics, TacClip can also be used submerged in water, while preserving bare finger contact.

</details>


### [79] [Monkey See, Can Monkey Do? A Benchmark for Evaluating Robot Skill Learning by Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08209)
*Weiwei Gu,Anmol Gupta,Anant Sah,Ryan Varghese,Lalitha Shreya Vanam,Prabhath Adireddi,Peter Karkus,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboReel，一个统一的基准，用于从人类视频中评估机器人学习策略（LfO）的模型，包含 10 个操作任务、4 个测试套件和 7 种以上 SOTA 算法，发现长时程和低容错任务仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LfO 是机器人学习的重要途径，但现有方法在假设、硬件和环境设置上差异大，难以比较和评估进展。

Method: 构建 RoboReel 基准，捆绑真实人类演示视频、模拟机器人轨迹和评估环境；设计四个测试套件，评测视觉干扰鲁棒性和长时程任务能力，并对比多类 LfO 模型和表征选择。

Result: 在 10 个任务上评估 7 种以上算法（包括基于 VLA 的变体），表明长时程和低容错任务对现有模型仍具挑战。

Conclusion: RoboReel 为 LfO 研究提供了统一评估框架，揭示当前模型的局限性，为未来发展提供方向。

Abstract: Learning from Observation (LfO) is a fundamental robotic capability that replicates how humans and animals socially learn from each other. Beyond its biological parallels, this modality provides a practical solution for data scaling in sample-inefficient and data-starved domains like robotics. Recent work has demonstrated promising results in learning manipulation skills from human videos, yet progress in this area remains difficult to assess. Existing methods vary widely in assumptions, hardware choices, and environment setups making it difficult to draw meaningful comparisons and identify advances in the field. To address these challenges, we introduce RoboReel: a unified benchmark for evaluating models that learn policies from human videos. RoboReel consists of bundled real-world human demonstration videos, simulated robot trajectories, and evaluation environments on ten manipulation tasks. We develop four test suites to evaluate the models' performance on multiple axes, including the robustness to visual distractors and the ability to complete long-horizon tasks. Our benchmark covers learning-from-observation models from different categories, and studies the effectiveness of multiple representation choices in our benchmark evaluation that covers over seven state-of-the-art algorithms (including our VLA based variants) in the field of LfO. Finally, we present an analysis of the different types of algorithms showing that long-horizon tasks and tasks with low tolerances are still challenging for current models. Webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [80] [Dual-Layer Semantic-Spatial Belief Mapping for Aerial Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08164)
*Jianqiang Xiao,Xiang Deng,Yuexuan Sun,Yanjin Wu,Wenbiao Yan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AeroBelief，一种用于空中目标导航的双层语义-空间信念建图框架，将瞬时的VLM观测转化为持久空间引导，在UAV-ON基准上取得最优SR/OSR/SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中目标导航中VLM的帧级输出往往噪声大、稀疏且空间上不稳定，难以直接用于持续导航，需要将其转化为持久、可靠的空间指引。

Method: 构建双层信念图：直觉层累积场景级语义线索用于探索，证据层保存经保守资格筛选的目标级观测用于接近与确认；通过证据门控融合生成空间信念热点，并引入目标条件视觉推理+保守证据筛选提升观测可靠性；同时用基于四叉树覆盖的无人机中心偏航对齐区域引导，通过时间承诺稳定探索方向，区域评分与语义信念解耦以避免重复低效搜索。

Result: 在UAV-ON基准上，AeroBelief的SR、OSR、SPL分别达21.61%、35.57%、10.62，均为对比方法中最好。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持持久语义-空间信念、保守证据筛选和时间稳定的区域引导对空中目标导航的有效性。

Abstract: Aerial Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) requires an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) to locate a described target in an unknown outdoor environment using onboard visual observations. Vision-language models (VLMs) can interpret open-ended target descriptions and visual observations, but their frame-level outputs are often noisy, sparse, and spatially transient. We propose AeroBelief, a dual-layer semantic-spatial belief mapping framework that transforms transient VLM observations into persistent spatial guidance. It separates broad contextual plausibility from target-specific evidence: an intuition layer accumulates scene-level semantic cues for exploration, while an evidence layer preserves qualified target-specific observations for approach and confirmation. Evidence-gated fusion combines the two layers into spatial belief hotspots. We further introduce object-conditioned visual reasoning with conservative evidence qualification to improve observation reliability before spatial accumulation. In parallel, egocentric regional guidance converts quadtree coverage into UAV-centered, yaw-aligned directional proposals and stabilizes them through temporal commitment. Its regional scoring is independent of semantic belief values, maintaining exploration pressure and reducing repeated low-gain search. Experiments on the UAV-ON benchmark show that AeroBelief achieves the best reported overall SR, OSR, and SPL among the compared methods, reaching 21.61%, 35.57%, and 10.62, respectively. These results support the effectiveness of persistent semantic-spatial belief, conservative evidence qualification, and temporally stable regional guidance for aerial ObjectNav.

</details>


### [81] [OmniNav: Robust Long-Horizon Target Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08159)
*Yujie Tang,Meiling Wang,Jinhao Jiang,Sibo Zuo,Yinan Deng,Xinyu Zhang,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniNav 将长时程目标导航建模为对分解的任务状态后验的持续推理，联合估计场景有效性、目标信念和交互可行性，并通过可更新的 3D 场景记忆、基于证据的贝叶斯信念修正以及考虑操作可达性与碰撞的端点选择，显著提升语义导航与实例导航成功率及真实拾放任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程目标导航需要同时维持有效场景记忆、在部分可观测下修正目标信念、选择可交互的导航端点，但这三者的状态仅条件有效：场景记忆会因物体移动或消失而过时，失败搜索应更新目标位置信念，几何上便利的端点可能无法操作。现有方法未联合处理这些耦合的不确定性。

Method: OmniNav 提出持续推断框架：1) 增量构建可更新的 3D 物体场景记忆，防止陈旧证据传播；2) 引入证据感知的贝叶斯信念修正机制，从语义上下文推导依赖区域的先验，将失败搜索作为负证据并更新后验以指导前沿选择；3) 在导航端点选择中纳入操作可达性与碰撞约束，并通过分层闭环恢复传播执行反馈。

Result: 在语义 ObjectNav 和细粒度实例导航基准上达到最高成功率；对目标位置移动具有鲁棒性；在真实世界拾放任务中，相较开环基线成功率从 53.3% 提升至 71.7%。

Conclusion: OmniNav 通过联合建模场景、信念与交互可行性的不确定性，实现更稳健的长时程导航与操作，验证了持续推理方法的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon target navigation requires a robot to sustain task execution across evolving observations, decisions, and physical interactions. This requires three coupled capabilities: maintaining valid scene memory, revising target beliefs under partial observability, and selecting interaction-feasible navigation endpoints. However, the state underlying each capability is only conditionally valid: scene representations become stale when objects move or disappear, unsuccessful searches alter beliefs over target locations, and geometrically convenient endpoints may still be infeasible for manipulation. To address these challenges, we present OmniNav, which formulates long-horizon navigation as continual inference over a factorized task state posterior coupling scene validity, target belief, and interaction feasibility. For representation, OmniNav incrementally constructs an updatable 3D object scene memory, preventing stale scene evidence from propagating to subsequent decisions. For exploration, it introduces an evidence-aware Bayesian belief-revision mechanism that derives dependency-aware region priors from semantic context, incorporates unsuccessful searches as negative evidence, and updates them for posterior-guided frontier selection. For interaction, OmniNav incorporates manipulation reachability and collision constraints into navigation-endpoint selection and propagates execution feedback through hierarchical closed-loop recovery. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniNav achieves the highest success rates among the compared methods on semantic ObjectNav and fine-grained instance navigation benchmarks, remains robust to target relocation, and improves real-world pick-and-place success from 53.3% to 71.7% over an adapted open-loop baseline. The project page of OmniNav is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [82] [DISEIL: Demonstration Distillation for Sample-Efficient Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08123)
*Suyog Khanal,Arun Kumar A V,Santu Rana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DISEIL方法，在交互式模仿学习中不仅决定何时打断学习者，还主动决定纠正哪个失败以及示范从哪里开始，通过将失败分组并利用视觉-语言模型生成示范请求，显著提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式模仿学习只解决何时打断策略的问题，而‘纠正哪个失败’和‘示范从哪里开始’仍由触发打断的偶然情节决定，缺乏目的性，导致专家示范利用效率低。

Method: DISEIL在每个失败情节中标记策略首次不可靠的步骤，用几何描述符表示该时刻，并将失败聚类为反复出现的失败模式；然后使用视觉-语言模型和语言模型读取选中的模式，生成下一次示范的请求；任务约束存储检查该请求是否可执行，然后才花费专家时间。所有模型均不产生机器人动作。

Result: 在5个模拟任务（状态和图像观测）的10种设置中，仅改变专家被请求的内容就获得了最高的平均保留成功率，其中1个设置平局；在最少的示范预算下优势最大。

Conclusion: DISEIL表明主动决定示范内容和起始位置能显著提升模仿学习性能；当前局限为单轮练习、模拟环境和大多脚本化专家，未来目标是让学习者跟踪示范覆盖范围，并按人类教师所需努力比例请求缺失行为。

Abstract: A robot that can be taught a new task from a handful of demonstrations has to work out for itself what it still cannot do, and then ask for exactly that. Interactive imitation learning takes a step in that direction by letting a policy practice on its own and calling an expert when it goes wrong. Existing methods decide when to interrupt the learner. A further 2 decisions are left to whichever episode happened to trigger the interruption: which failure to correct, and where the demonstration should start. This paper is a first attempt at making both of them deliberately. DISEIL (Demonstration dIstillation for Sample-Efficient Imitation Learning) marks each failed episode at the step where the policy first becomes unreliable, represents that moment with a geometric descriptor, and groups the failures into recurring failure modes. A vision-language model and a language model read the selected mode and write a request for the next demonstration, and a store of task constraints checks that the request can be carried out before any expert time is spent. No model produces a robot action. Across 5 simulated tasks under state and image observations, changing only what the expert is asked for gives the highest mean held-out success rate in all 10 settings, with a tie in 1, and the margin is widest at the smallest budget we tested. The scope is narrow: a single round of practice at a time, in simulation, with experts that are mostly scripted. The longer-term aim is a learner that also tracks what its demonstration set already covers, and that asks a human teacher for the missing behavior in proportion to the effort each request costs them.

</details>


### [83] [mjorbit: A Simulation Framework for Space Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08010)
*John Z. Zhang,Joris Verhagen,Fausto Vega,Patrick McKeen,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于模拟带接触的多体空间机器人通用框架，基于MuJoCo构建，支持CPU和GPU后端，并展示了其在模型预测控制和强化学习中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务、组装和制造需要高效、大规模的机器人仿真，但现有机器人仿真框架缺乏对轨道动力学和空间环境的支持，需要一种将轨道传播与机器人仿真耦合的通用方法。

Method: 作者先对轨道传播与现有机器人仿真框架的耦合方法进行了经验性权衡研究，然后开发了mjorbit框架，它基于MuJoCo引擎添加了航天器动力学、执行器和传感器，提供低延迟C++ CPU后端和高吞吐量GPU后端，并通过简单Python API访问。

Result: 通过多个真实在轨案例研究，展示了mjorbit在模型预测控制和强化学习中的实际应用，代码已开源。

Conclusion: mjorbit提供了一个通用、灵活且高性能的仿真工具，适用于空间机器人接触仿真，促进了在轨服务等应用的研究与开发。

Abstract: This paper presents a general framework for simulating multi-body space robots with contact. We bring efficient, large-scale robot simulation to in-space servicing, assembly, and manufacturing applications. First, we perform an empirical trade study of methods for coupling orbit propagation with existing robotics simulation frameworks. Next, we present mjorbit, a general, flexible, and performant framework built on the MuJoCo engine widely used in robotics, to which we add key spacecraft dynamics, actuators, and sensors. We provide a low-latency C++ CPU backend and a high-throughput GPU backend behind a simple Python API. We demonstrate mjorbit by solving several realistic on-orbit case studies with both model-predictive control and reinforcement learning. Open-source code and examples are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [84] [SPOT: Spatial Perception-Oriented Long-Horizon Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07933)
*Lixing Fang,Ziyan Xiong,Sunli Chen,Zhiyang Dou,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPOT是一种面向空间感知的VR遥操作系统，通过扩展操作者的感知视野（结合机器人搭载的双眼鱼眼相机、宽视野立体显示、视角解耦自由观看和视觉稳定），解耦视觉探索与机器人运动，从而提高长时程人形机器人数据采集的效率、准确性和恢复速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量演示数据是训练通用人形机器人的核心瓶颈，而现有遥操作系统在长时程操作中受限于操作者的感知视野：窄视场遗漏周边事件、机器人相机在运动时不稳定、头部视角控制与机器人运动耦合干扰观察。

Method: SPOT系统结合机器人搭载的双眼鱼眼相机、宽视野立体显示、视角解耦自由观看和视觉稳定，提供宽广、稳定且可主动查看的机器人中心视角。与传统接口不同，它通过虚拟半球渲染立体观察，使自然头部旋转改变观察方向而不直接控制机器人。

Result: 在感知关键的多种任务（掉落恢复、周边取物、大范围双手操作、精细对准、动态交互）上，SPOT显著提升了数据采集效率、准确性和恢复速度，展示了在用户友好和可扩展的长时程人形数据采集中的有效性。

Conclusion: SPOT通过解耦视觉探索与运动控制来扩展感知视野，为长时程人形遥操作数据采集提供了一种有效且用户友好的方案，有助于缓解高质量演示数据短缺问题。

Abstract: High-quality demonstration data is becoming a central bottleneck for training general-purpose humanoid robots. While recent humanoid teleoperation systems have made substantial progress in retargeting human motion to robot motion, long-horizon loco-manipulation requires another capability: operators must maintain task-relevant spatial awareness over time, e.g., object locations, surrounding environments, the robot's pose. We call the extent of this awareness the operator's perceptual horizon. However, existing methods often shorten this: narrow views miss peripheral events, robot-mounted cameras become unstable during locomotion, and coupled head-view control makes looking around interfere with robot motion. We present SPOT, a Spatial Perception-Oriented VR Teleoperation system for collecting long-horizon humanoid demonstration data by providing extended perceptual horizon. SPOT combines a robot-mounted binocular fisheye camera, a wide-field stereoscopic display, viewpoint-decoupled free-looking, and visual stabilization to provide a robot-centric view that is wide, stable, and actively inspectable. Unlike conventional egocentric interfaces, SPOT decouples visual exploration from robot actuation: the egocentric stereo observation is rendered on a virtual hemisphere around the operator, so natural head rotations change where the operator looks within the wide-field view rather than commanding the robot head, camera, or torso. We evaluate SPOT on perception-critical humanoid data-collection tasks spanning drop recovery, peripheral retrieval, large-workspace bimanual manipulation, fine alignment, and dynamic interaction. SPOT improves efficiency, accuracy, and recovery speed, demonstrating its effectiveness for user-friendly and scalable long-horizon humanoid data collection.

</details>


### [85] [A Multimodal Label Forecasting Method for Aperiodic Visuo-Motor Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07930)
*Borui He,Garrett E Katz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对无周期性的时间序列预测任务（人体运动中的跌倒预测），提出两个新基准数据集（仿真与真实硬件），并设计了一种利用内源/外生变量且严格保证i.i.d.采样的新型深度架构，在真实和仿真数据上分别取得12.73%和10.40%以上的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测（TSF）基准大多假设时间序列具有近似周期性，而现实中的许多任务（如基于第一视角和本体感觉预测人体跌倒）并不满足该假设。当前Transformer和MLP方法在这种非周期性场景下表现不佳，缺乏对应的基准和专用方法。

Method: 提出一种新的深度学习架构，同时利用内源变量（如本体感觉）和外生变量（如第一视角视觉），并设计训练过程严格强制执行训练样本的i.i.d.采样，以应对非周期性轨迹。

Result: 在多个实验条件下，所提方法相比现有最佳方法取得统计显著的改进：真实数据上提升≥12.73%，仿真数据上提升≥10.40%。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了周期性假设在真实TSF任务中的局限性，提供了新的非周期基准数据集，并证明了所提架构和训练策略在该类问题上的有效性，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Deep learning models have been increasingly applied to Time Series Forecasting (TSF) in recent years. Transformer-based and MLP-based models have both been used effectively on many real-world TSF regression benchmarks, and there is ongoing debate as to which family of methods is best. While these benchmarks have drawn much attention, it is also worth noting that many current datasets and methods assume approximate periodicity in the time series. In this work, we focus on a new TSF task without periodicity: anticipating falls during humanoid locomotion, on the basis of egocentric vision and proprioception. When the locomotion trajectories are sufficiently diverse, periodicity is violated. We contribute two new benchmark datasets (one from simulation, one from real hardware), showing that periodicity is violated and recent deep TSF methods struggle on these benchmarks. We also propose a novel deep learning architecture that exploits both endogenous and exogenous variables and a training process that rigorously enforces i.i.d sampling of training examples. Our results show statistically significant improvement over prior art in multiple experimental conditions, by 12.73% or more on the real data and 10.40% or more on the simulation data. Code and datasets will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [86] [Conditional Timed Partial Orders: An Expressive and Interpretable Framework for Robot Task Specification and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07905)
*Sebastian Escobar,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出条件偏序（cTPO），扩展了带时间偏序（TPO）的表达能力，支持更丰富的相对时间约束和基于环境条件的条件事件激活，并证明其规划问题仍可归约为MILP，但规模更大。为应对计算困难，提出一种分解算法，将cTPO划分为子TPO序列，证明其完备性并保持最优性，实验显示最高可达四个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TPO虽然为机器人任务提供可解释的规范框架并支持MILP规划，但表达能力有限，仅能描述简单时间约束下的偏序事件。需要更丰富的表达来处理实际任务中的相对时间约束和条件触发，同时避免因表达增强导致的MILP规模爆炸。

Method: 引入cTPO以扩展TPO，添加条件事件激活和更丰富的相对时间约束；将cTPO规划问题形式化为MILP；针对大型MILP，提出分解算法，将cTPO分解为若干子TPO并顺序求解小规模MILP，证明分解完备且保留最优性。

Result: 实验表明cTPO作为任务规范框架有效，分解算法相比整体MILP可实现高达四个数量级的加速，同时保持规划质量和可解释性。

Conclusion: cTPO显著提升了任务规范表达能力，分解算法有效缓解了计算复杂度问题，使复杂任务的规划既高效又保持可解释性，为机器人任务规范提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Timed Partial Orders (TPOs), originally proposed for workflows, provide an interpretable framework for robot task specification with planning algorithms based on mixed-integer linear programming (MILP). However, TPOs are limited in expressivity, capturing only partial-order events with simple timing constraints. In this paper, we introduce Conditional TPOs (cTPOs), which extend TPOs with richer relative-timing constraints and conditional event activations based on environmental conditions. We show that planning for cTPOs also reduces to an MILP problem; however, the added expressivity results in significantly larger MILPs that can become computationally intractable. To address this challenge, we propose a decomposition algorithm that partitions a cTPO into smaller sub-TPOs, yielding a sequence of smaller MILP problems. We prove that this decomposition is complete and preserves plan optimality while improving the interpretability of complex tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of cTPOs as a task specification framework and the efficiency of our decomposition approach, achieving up to four orders of magnitude speedup over the monolithic MILP.

</details>


### [87] [M3-Tele: A Unified Multimodal Teleoperational Framework for Compliant Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07859)
*Hengxiang Chen,Shenwen Deng,Yujian Ma,Gan Ma,Qiang Li,Nutan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的 multimodal teleoperation 框架 M3-Tele，用于柔顺全身移动操作，可同步采集视觉、触觉、力与本体感觉数据，并显著提升接触丰富型任务的遥操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作与数据采集框架常忽略多模态感知与全身协调操作的联合考虑，导致演示采集效率和质量受限，影响下游策略学习效果。

Method: 设计 M3-Tele 框架，整合全身协调与物理柔顺调节，在任务执行中同步捕获对齐的视觉、触觉、力和本体感觉观测，并配备专用控制器以提升接触稳定性。

Result: 力跟踪误差从 4.132 N 降至 0.346 N，接触丢失从每试 2.46 次降至 0.02 次，触觉变形误差降低 65%；用户研究验证了系统可靠性与可用性；Diffusion Policy 实验表明触觉与力联合感知的价值。

Conclusion: M3-Tele 显著改善接触丰富型遥操作性能，并为移动操作中的多模态数据采集与策略学习提供有效支撑。

Abstract: Executing contact-rich tasks efficiently requires the seamless integration of whole-body coordination and physical compliance regulation. However, existing teleoperation and data-collection frameworks often overlook the joint consideration of multimodal perception and coordinated whole-body operation. This limitation can reduce the efficiency and quality of demonstration collection, thereby affecting the effectiveness of downstream policy learning. In this work, we present \textbf{M3-Tele}: A Unified \underline{M}ultimodal \underline{Tele}operational Framework for Compliant Whole-Body \underline{M}obile \underline{M}anipulation, enabling stable physical interaction and capturing aligned visual, tactile, force, and proprioceptive observations during task execution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves contact-rich teleoperation performance. The proposed controller reduces the force tracking error from 4.132~N to 0.346~N, the contact loss from 2.46 to 0.02 events per trial and the tactile deformation error by 65\%. User studies across four mobile manipulation tasks also verify the reliability and usability of the proposed system. Furthermore, Diffusion Policy experiments highlight the value of joint tactile and force sensing.

</details>


### [88] [Scene Graph-Driven Haptic Feedback for Safety Enhancement in Robotic Ophthalmic Surgery via Physically Simulated iOCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07857)
*Danial Arbabi,Korab Hoxha,Angelo Henriques,Mirza Imamovic,M. Ali Nasseri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于场景图（SG）的触觉反馈系统，用于机器人视网膜下注射任务。该系统通过分析物理模拟的术中OCT图像构建实时手术场景图，并利用规则引擎生成状态依赖的触觉反馈，以弥合手术机器人操作中的感觉鸿沟。用户研究显示，该方法将针头对准误差降低14%，系统可用性评分提高8%，且不增加操作时间，并促使医生采用更安全的“先对准后接近”策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人眼科手术虽然精度高，但将外科医生与器械解耦，导致触觉反馈缺失，形成“感觉鸿沟”，影响手术安全性和效果。需要一种实时、情境感知的触觉反馈机制来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 利用物理模拟的术中OCT（iOCT）视频流构建实时手术场景图（SG），作为手术场景的语义抽象层；再由确定性规则引擎根据SG状态生成触觉反馈，作用于机器人操控设备。系统在仿真头模和定制手术机器人上进行了用户研究（N=16）。

Result: SG驱动的触觉反馈将针头对齐误差降低14%（p=0.044），系统可用性量表（SUS）得分提高8%（p=0.015），任务完成时间无显著差异。针刺轨迹分析显示，用户出现更安全的“先对齐后接近”策略，即负强化触觉促使医生在接近视网膜目标前先微调工具轨迹。

Conclusion: 场景图可作为机器人显微手术中实时、安全增强的情境感知触觉反馈的直接计算基础，有效提升手术精度和用户体验。

Abstract: Robotic ophthalmic surgery offers high precision but introduces a "sensory gap" by decoupling the surgeon from their instrument, resulting in a loss of tactile feedback. This paper presents a novel haptic feedback system for subretinal injection tasks leveraging Scene Graphs (SG). The system bridges the sensory gap by analyzing a physically simulated intraoperative Optical Coherence Tomography (iOCT) feed to construct a real-time surgical SG. The SG serves as a semantic abstraction layer for the surgical scene, which is then utilized by a deterministic, rule-based engine to generate state-dependent haptic feedback on a robotic input device. The system was evaluated in a user study (N=16) using an anthropomorphic head phantom and a custom-built surgical robot. Results demonstrate that the SG-driven haptic feedback improved surgical precision, reducing needle alignment error by 14% (p = 0.044) and improving System Usability Scale (SUS) scores by 8% (p = 0.015), while maintaining comparable task completion times. A needle trajectory analysis revealed the emergence of a safer "Align-then-Approach" strategy, in which our haptic negative reinforcement prompted users to fine-tune the tool's trajectory before approaching the retinal target. This work suggests that SGs can effectively serve as the direct computational foundation for real-time, safety-enhancing context-aware haptic feedback in robotic microsurgery.

</details>


### [89] [OpenWAM: An Open, Modular Exploration Towards Systematic World-Action Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07398)
*Yuran Wang,Siqiao Huang,Mingleyang Li,Chenhao Zhang,Jiaqi Liang,Weiyang Jin,Yue Chen,Xuemin Chi,Donghao Zhou,Qize Yu,Yu-Kai Wang,Yuhan Rui,Shenzhe Yao,Zhen Yuan,Zhenhao Shen,Kefei Zhu,Zijie Zhu,Ning Gao,Xiaowei Chi,Guanqi He,Shanghang Zhang,Hao Dong,Lin Shao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenWAM 是一个开放研究栈，将世界-动作预训练分解为可控实验，通过模块化基础设施和受控实验提炼出三条设计原则，并构建了在仿真和真实世界基准上表现优异的 OpenWAM-α 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）采用单体式设计，生成骨干、视觉表征、架构、信息流、推理过程和训练数据紧密耦合，导致无法弄清哪些设计选择至关重要及其原因。

Method: 提出 OpenWAM-Infra 将设计空间分解为可组合模块，并配套统一训练、推理、部署和评估；通过 OpenWAM-Study 进行受控实验，考察继承什么、世界与动作学习如何交互、协同如何扩展，进而总结原则；最后依据原则构建 OpenWAM-α，在约 6,400 小时第一人称人类与机器人数据上联合训练。

Result: OpenWAM-α 在八个仿真基准和真实机器人实验中，涵盖单臂、双臂及灵巧手等多种具身，均持续取得优异性能，从仿真到物理世界保持顶尖水平。

Conclusion: OpenWAM 提供了完整的开放栈（基础设施、评估协议、预训练模型和数据配方），有助于未来研究，并验证了模块化设计和三条原则的有效性。

Abstract: World-Action Models inherit world knowledge from video-generative priors, and channel it into executable control signals through embodied experience. Existing systems, however, are monolithic: the generative backbone, visual representation, architecture, information flow, inference procedure, and training data are tightly coupled, obscuring which design choices matter and why. We introduce OpenWAM, an open research stack that turns world-action pretraining into a controlled experimental program. OpenWAM-Infra factorizes the WAM design space into composable modules with unified training, inference, deployment, and evaluation. On this substrate, OpenWAM-Study examines three questions through controlled experiments: what to inherit, how world and action learning interact, and how their synergy scales; and distills three principles: upstream knowledge transfers through a sufficiently capable generative backbone and a compact, information-rich latent space; world-action synergy requires dedicated action capacity, explicit world-to-action information flow, and synchronized joint denoising; and embodied pretraining principally improves out-of-domain generalization, with one-stage co-training over egocentric and robot data integrating world coverage and action grounding. Composing these principles, we build OpenWAM-{\alpha}, an open WAM pretrained on roughly 6,400 hours of egocentric human and robot data and evaluated across simulation and real-world benchmarks. Across the eight simulation benchmarks and the real-robot experiments, which together span embodiments from single-arm and bimanual manipulation to dexterous hands, OpenWAM-{\alpha} delivers consistently excellent performance, sustaining its top-tier standing from simulation to the physical world. We release the full stack, including infrastructure, evaluation protocols, pretrained models, and data recipes, to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [90] [D3ARC: Time-Critical Distributed Disaster Detection for Asynchronous Cooperative Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07350)
*Nikolaos Koursioumpas,Lina Magoula,Nancy Alonistioti,Ramin Khalili*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出D3ARC，一个异步分布式分层框架，用于时间敏感且可靠的野火检测，通过多机器人协作和前瞻性评估提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化加剧自然灾害的严重性和不可预测性，传统野火监测受覆盖范围、成本和人员风险限制，需要自主且自适应的监测方案。

Method: D3ARC集成多个机器人代理，在不确定性下通过分布式感知、共享态势感知和协调行动合作；远程控制器异步决定每个机器人的运动，机器人自身感知环境并决定检测方式；引入前瞻性能力，在执行前评估候选策略，并集成安全导航、覆盖效率、合作与可靠性机制。

Result: 在逼真机器人仿真、消融研究和基线对比中，实现最高94%的任务成功率和89.4%的检测置信度。

Conclusion: D3ARC能有效提升野火检测的时间敏感性和可靠性，为自主监测自然灾害提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Climate change is increasing the severity and unpredictability of natural disasters. In time-critical crises such as wildfires, traditional monitoring practices remain limited by coverage, cost, and personnel risk, paving the way for autonomous and adaptive monitoring solutions. Within this context, this paper introduces D3ARC, an asynchronous distributed hierarchical framework for time-aware and reliable wildfire detection. D3ARC integrates multiple robotic agents that cooperate under uncertainty through distributed perception, shared situational awareness and coordinated actions. A remote controller asynchronously decides upon each robot's motion, while each robotic agent senses the environment and decides where and how to execute the wildfire detection. All robotic operations require time, and as time progresses, wildfires continue to spread, reducing the opportunity for early intervention. As such, all agents share a common objective: to detect a wildfire with a certain performance threshold as fast as possible and within a time limit. D3ARC integrates mechanisms for safe navigation, coverage efficiency, cooperation and reliability. It introduces a forward-looking capability that allows agents to anticipate the future by evaluating candidate strategies before execution. The framework is evaluated through realistic robotics simulations, ablation studies, and baseline comparisons, achieving an overall mission success up to 94% with 89.4% detection confidence.

</details>


### [91] [PV-WM: A Heterogeneous Micro-Macro World Model for Articulated Pedestrian-Vehicle Co-Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07328)
*Haozhuang Chi,Jingsong Liang,Ziying Song,Lei Yang,Shihao Li,Haoruo Zhang,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PV-WM，一种基于历史轨迹的单一网络世界模型，统一预测行人的根运动、15关节姿态和车辆状态，在异构未来状态预测中优于模块化专家系统，并显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人-车辆联合预测涉及异构物理尺度：行人需同时考虑根运动和关节运动，而车辆可视为刚性体。现有道路代理预测器通常忽略行人姿态，姿态预测器又不覆盖车辆未来轨迹，缺乏统一建模。

Method: PV-WM通过循环推进同步异构状态，包含行人根运动、15关节姿态及学习到的车辆状态；从行人块和车辆块生成下一循环边界，利用预测中心和朝向并保持观测尺寸重建车辆框，每次转换后重新计算人-车几何关系。

Result: 相比单次完整状态预测器，循环执行使Root ADE降低12.7%，MPJPE降低14.8%；在824个Waymo场景中，相比模块化专家，Root ADE降低5.2%，MPJPE降低7.6%，人-车距离误差降低11.9%，定向框最近接近误差降低5.8%，同时参数减少57.1%，FLOPs降低96.5%，p95延迟降低25.5%。

Conclusion: PV-WM证明单一网络可统一异构未来状态，保留行人和车辆各自的动力学特性，并在精度和效率上全面超越现有方法。

Abstract: Local pedestrian-vehicle forecasting spans heterogeneous physical scales: pedestrians combine root locomotion with articulated motion, whereas vehicles are rigid bodies described by kinematic state and oriented extent. Existing road-agent forecasters typically omit pedestrian articulation, while pose forecasters leave vehicle futures outside the learned rollout. We introduce PV-WM, a history-only world model over structured post-perception tracks. It recurrently advances pedestrian root motion, 15-joint articulation, and learned vehicle states within a synchronized heterogeneous state. The generated pedestrian and vehicle chunks supply the next recurrent boundary; vehicle boxes are reconstructed from predicted center and heading with observed extent, and P-V geometry is recomputed after every transition. Relative to a matched one-shot complete-state predictor, recurrent execution reduces Root ADE by 12.7% and MPJPE by 14.8%. Feedback interventions show that later predictions depend on the content, temporal order, and pedestrian identity of generated articulation. Across 824 aligned Waymo contexts, with 797 providing valid future vehicle support, PV-WM reduces Root ADE by 5.2%, MPJPE by 7.6%, P-V distance error by 11.9%, and oriented-box closest-approach error by 5.8% relative to a validation-selected Modular Specialist. The single-network model uses 57.1% fewer parameters, 96.5% lower average FLOPs per local scene, and 25.5% lower measured p95 latency. PV-WM unifies this heterogeneous future state while preserving type-specific pedestrian and vehicle dynamics.

</details>


### [92] [How Long Until Your Robot Ignores You? A Safety Benchmark for LLM Orchestrators in Human-Humanoid Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07288)
*Aulon Bajrami,Mohamed Elshamouty,Werner Kraus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向LLM编排器在人机协作中安全性的基准测试环境，基于MCP架构和ISO 10218-2:2025标准，定义了五类安全不变量、四级合规分类及三层评估流程。Layer-1结果显示模型家族决定安全底线，上下文管理对失败模式有分离效应，且Gemini展现出独特的比例合规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于通过自然语言接口编排机器人行为，但目前缺乏评估其在人机协作中作为安全意识决策者可靠性的基准。与确定性安全系统不同，LLM编排器表现出从过度合规到完全违规的合规谱系，亟需系统性的评测方法。

Method: 构建了首个基于Model Context Protocol(MCP)架构的安全基准环境，以ISO 10218-2:2025防护措施为安全不变量基础。定义了五个可测试的安全不变量、四级合规分类（正确合规、过度合规、合规不足、完全违规），并设计了三层评估流程（文本提示、模拟传感器-执行器循环、物理验证）。在Layer-1中，对三个云后端（Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-4o-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash）和一个本地开源模型qwen3:8b进行了40次100轮对话测试，并设置了全上下文和滑动窗口预算两种条件。

Result: Layer-1结果显示：(1)模型家族决定安全底线，Claude和Gemini接近零违规，而GPT-4o-mini每会话最多违规13次；(2)上下文管理将两种失败轴分离，使每个云后端的平均行为问题减少42-57%，但GPT-4o-mini的违规次数几乎翻倍（从3.8增至7.2）；(3)比例合规行为（将移动速度限制在规则允许的最大值而非拒绝）仅在Gemini中一致出现。初步模拟层重现了模型排序及GPT-4o-mini失败模式反转现象。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了LLM编排器在人机协作中的安全行为差异，强调模型选择对安全底线的重要性，并展示了上下文管理对失败模式的复杂影响（在部分模型上降低行为问题却增加违规）。比例合规的出现表明不同模型具有不同的合规策略。研究为未来LLM安全编排器评估和设计提供了基础框架，后续模拟和物理验证将进一步完善结论。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed to orchestrate robot behavior through natural-language interfaces, yet no benchmark exists to evaluate their reliability as safety-aware decision makers in human-humanoid collaboration. Unlike deterministic safety systems that enforce binary allow/deny decisions, LLM-based orchestrators exhibit a compliance spectrum ranging from overcompliance (refusing safe actions) to full safety violations. This paper introduces the first safety benchmarking environment for LLM orchestrators in human-humanoid collaboration, built on a Model Context Protocol (MCP)-based architecture with safety invariants grounded in ISO 10218-2:2025 protective measures. The benchmark defines five testable safety invariants, a four-level compliance taxonomy (correct compliance, overcompliance, undercompliance, full violation), and a three-layer evaluation pipeline (text prompting, simulated sensor-actuator loops, and physical validation on a Unitree G1 EDU humanoid). We report Layer-1 results: three cloud backends (Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-4o-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash) and a local open-weights baseline (qwen3:8b) across 40 100-turn sessions under full-context and sliding-window budget conditions, while the simulation and physical layers remain ongoing. We find that (1) model family determines the safety floor, as Claude and Gemini remain at or near zero violations while GPT-4o-mini commits up to 13 per session, (2) context management dissociates two failure axes, reducing mean behavioral issues by 42-57% for every cloud backend while nearly doubling GPT-4o-mini's violations (3.8 to 7.2 per session), and (3) proportional compliance, clamping movement speed to the rule-specified maximum rather than refusing, emerges consistently only in Gemini; the preliminary simulation layer reproduces the model ranking and the GPT-4o-mini failure-mode inversion.

</details>


### [93] [LightSplat: Real-Time High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian SLAM with Loop Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07274)
*Junze Bao,Ye Gao,Yiming Huang,Xiaolong Yu,Chen Dong,Qing Gao,Wei Wang,Jinhu Lü*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LightSplat 是一个基于混合表示的 RGB-D SLAM 框架，通过局部稀疏特征跟踪和双线程后端构建密集高斯子图，并利用特征加速的 3DGS 配准实现在线闭环，以高效、鲁棒的方式实现高保真 3DGS 地图的在线重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS SLAM 系统在操作性能和地图适应性上存在严重局限，无法满足实际部署的严格要求。

Method: LightSplat 采用混合表示：局部稀疏特征用于快速鲁棒跟踪；双线程后端逐步构建密集高斯子图；通过特征加速的 3DGS 配准实现在线闭环，并利用位姿图优化提升全局一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集和真实机器人平台上，该方法重建质量接近最先进水平，能适应实际相机运动，平均帧率保持在 8 FPS。

Conclusion: LightSplat 为在实际环境中部署高保真 3DGS 提供了高效且鲁棒的基础。

Abstract: SLAM systems based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have recently demonstrated promising reconstruction accuracy for dense 3D scene representations. However, current 3DGS systems struggle to meet the strict demands of real-world deployments due to severe limitations in operational performance and map adaptability. To this end, we propose LightSplat, a hybrid-representation RGB-D SLAM framework. It synergizes local sparse features for robust and fast tracking with a dual-thread backend that progressively constructs dense Gaussian submaps. Crucially, we enable online loop closure through feature-accelerated 3DGS registration, refining overall map consistency through pose graph optimization. Ultimately, LightSplat achieves the online reconstruction of high-fidelity Gaussian map. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets and real-world robotic platform demonstrate that our method achieves near state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and the capability to accommodate practical camera motions, maintaining an average framerate of 8 FPS. Overall, LightSplat provides an efficient and robust foundation for deploying high-fidelity 3DGS in real-world environments.

</details>


### [94] [Singularity-Free Guiding Vector Fields on SO(3) with Designer-Specified Progression Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07269)
*Jesus Bautista,Hector Garcia de Marina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在SO(3)上无奇异的导引向量场（SF-GVF），用于姿态路径跟踪，通过将增强状态方法与李群几何结合，得到闭式几何制导律，并可将路径推进速度作为设计规格显式指定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欧几里得空间中的无奇异导引向量场无法直接推广到SO(3)上的姿态路径跟踪，且欧几里得条件v=0会排除具有最小速度约束的平台；而SO(3)上对应的ω=0条件在物理上通常可接受，使得推进行为成为可利用的设计自由度。此外，需要避免每步优化并处理拓扑障碍。

Method: 将欧几里得SF-GVF构造提升到SO(3)，结合增强状态方法与李群内蕴几何，在SO(3)的稠密开子集（除去测度为零的对映点集）上定义封闭形式的几何制导律；将沿路径的推进速度建模为设计者指定的函数ν(ξ)，并在双不变黎曼度量下建立统一的结构性结果。

Result: 所得控制输入直接位于so(3)（机体角速度），无需每步优化。仿真在自交路径上验证了常速和点收敛两种推进行为下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架在SO(3)上实现了无奇异姿态路径跟踪，将推进行为从隐式自由度提升为显式设计规格，并利用SO(3)特有的拓扑与几何结构，为具有主动姿态控制的平台提供了新的设计自由度。

Abstract: This paper develops a singularity-free guiding vector field (SF-GVF) for path following on the special orthogonal group SO(3). First, we lift the Euclidean SF-GVF construction to SO(3), integrating the augmented-state approach with the intrinsic Lie-group geometry and obtaining a closed-form geometric guidance law whose integral curves converge to a designer-specified attitude path. The field is defined on a dense open subset of SO(3), excluding only the measure-zero antipodal set - a manifestation of the topological obstruction to continuous global stabilization on SO(3). The construction requires no per-step optimization and produces a control input intrinsically in so(3) as body angular rates. Second, we formalize the progression behavior along the path as a designer-supplied function \nu(\xi), promoting the parametric speed from an implicitly resolved degree of freedom to a first-class design specification. In contrast to the Euclidean condition v = 0, which excludes vehicles with minimum-speed constraints, the corresponding condition \omega = 0 on SO(3) is physically admissible for most platforms with active attitude control, making the progression behavior a design freedom structurally available on SO(3) but absent in the Euclidean setting. The framework's structural results are established under a bi-invariant Riemannian metric and hold uniformly across choices of path, progression, and Lyapunov gain. The framework is illustrated in simulation on self-intersecting paths under both constant and point-convergence progression behaviors.

</details>


### [95] [Generalizable 6D Pose Estimation of Textureless Objects with Planar-based Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07231)
*Jie Lu,Hengtan Zhang,Li Gong,Pengpeng Wang,Xianjia Yu,Jinxiang Deng,Tomi Westerlund,Zhongxue Gan,Lirong Zheng,Zhuo Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PG-Pose，一种结合平面高斯泼溅重建与几何驱动优化的无CAD模型6D姿态估计方法，在无纹理物体上达到94.2% ADD(S)@0.1d精度，并成功应用于工业机器人抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无纹理物体缺乏外观特征，且现有通用方法因底层表示几何约束不足而性能受限，导致无CAD模型的6D姿态估计仍具挑战。

Method: 离线阶段从多视角RGB图像提取三种表示，利用PGS重建3D高斯并渲染深度图反投影生成3D点云；在线阶段通过2D-3D匹配估计初始姿态，再用PGS-Refiner迭代优化。

Result: 在OnePose-LowTexture数据集上平均精度为94.2% ADD(S)@0.1d，较最先进的GS方法提升2.1%；在双轴工业机器人上完成未知物体的抓取任务。

Conclusion: PG-Pose通过几何感知的表示与优化有效提升无纹理物体姿态估计精度，并验证了其在工业抓取中的实用性。

Abstract: Estimating the 6D pose of textureless objects without prior CAD models remains a critical challenge due to the lack of appearance features. While recent generalizable approaches alleviate the dependence on object-specific models, their performance on low-texture objects is often limited by insufficient geometric constraints in the underlying representations. In this work, we propose PG-Pose, a geometry-aware framework combining Planar-based Gaussian Splatting (PGS) reconstruction and Geometry-driven pose optimization. In the offline representation extraction stage, three distinct representations of the object are extracted from multi-view reference RGB images with known poses. PG-Pose reconstructs a 3D Gaussian representation and renders high-fidelity depth maps to generate 3D point clouds through back projection. In the online pose inference stage, the initial pose of the input image is estimated by 2D-3D correspondence matching between the input image and the reconstructed 3D point clouds, followed by a PGS-Refiner for iterative pose optimization. Evaluations on the OnePose-LowTexture datasets, PG-Pose achieves an average accuracy of 94.2% ADD(S)@0.1d, with a 2.1% improvement average accuracy compared with the state-of-the-art (SOTA) GS-based approach. To further demonstrate the effectiveness of PG-Pose for industrial robots in grasping tasks, we deploy it on a dual-arm industrial robot and successfully realize the grasping task on an unseen object.

</details>


### [96] [Phase-and-First-Arrival VLM Feedback for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning in Surgical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07211)
*Wanli Liuchen,Fangyuan Wang,Bin Li,Anqing Duan,Yunhui Liu,Peng Zhou,David Navarro-Alarcon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于阶段与首次到达的反馈机制，通过每段轨迹一次VLM查询识别最远视觉验证阶段及其首次到达时间，在稀疏奖励操纵任务中保留部分进展并实现时间信用分配，显著优于CLIP奖励并在真实硬件上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏结果反馈限制了机器人从失败操作中学习，多阶段手术尝试中的有效动作片段无法被利用。传统终止奖励将所有失败视为同一结果，标量VLM评分也无法说明进展价值及发生时间。

Method: 引入阶段与首次到达反馈：对每个记录的episode仅用一次VLM查询，识别视觉上验证的最远任务阶段及该阶段首次到达的时刻，使学习器能重用部分行为并精确定位时间信用。构建了SurgPhaseBench基准，包含刚体与可变形任务的阶段结构，并在仿真和硬件上评估。

Result: 在五个仿真任务中，方法平均成功率达75.2%，高于使用相同视觉输入基于CLIP奖励的52.1%；仅改变反馈表示时优势依然存在。在硬件上，该方法实现自主积木拾取与滑动恢复。

Conclusion: 轨迹级视觉监督能在稀疏奖励控制中保留部分进展并提供所需的时间信用，有效提升复杂操纵学习性能。

Abstract: Sparse outcome feedback limits what robots can learn from unsuccessful attempts at complex manipulation. Failed multi-stage surgical attempts can contain grasps, lifts, or transfers worth reusing. In sparse-reward reinforcement learning, terminal rewards collapse such attempts to the same outcome, while scalar vision-language model (VLM) ratings reveal neither what progress merits credit nor when it occurred. We introduce phase-and-first-arrival feedback: one VLM query per recorded episode identifies the furthest visually verified task phase and when that phase is first reached, allowing the learner to reuse partial behavior and localize credit. We instantiate it in SurgPhaseBench, a phase-structured suite spanning rigid and deformable tasks, and evaluate it in simulation and hardware. Across five simulated tasks, our method reaches 75.2% mean success, compared with 52.1% for a reward based on Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) using the same visual input; the advantage persists when only the feedback representation changes. On hardware, the same record supports autonomous block picking and slip recovery. Together, these results show that trajectory-level visual supervision can preserve partial progress while providing the temporal credit needed for sparse-reward control.

</details>


### [97] [State-of-the-Art in Learning-by-Demonstration with Passive Observation for Industrial Assembly Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07165)
*David Koetter,Oliver Petrovic,Christian Brecher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对工业装配中的被动学习示范（LbD）进行系统文献综述，重点分析一次性示范方法下的感知架构与泛化能力，发现向以对象为中心的感知转变，使学习到的动作基元能以最小训练迁移到新产品变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高混合、低批量制造需要灵活的机器人编程方式，学习示范（LbD）通过捕获专家技能实现直观编程，但现有综述缺乏对被动LbD在工业装配中感知架构和泛化机制的系统分析，尤其是一次性示范的局限性。

Method: 系统文献综述，聚焦被动LbD和一次性示范方法，评估系统如何利用单个演示适应新装配任务，并分析感知架构和演示泛化的研究趋势。

Result: 研究发现领域正转向以物体为中心的感知（object-centric perception），使得学习到的基元能够以最少训练迁移至新装配变体。

Conclusion: 一次性被动LbD在工业装配中具有可行性，以对象为中心的感知是提升泛化能力的关键方向，可减少新任务所需的重新训练成本。

Abstract: Learning-by-Demonstration (LbD) enables intuitive robot programming by capturing expert skills, which is crucial for agility in high-mix, low- volume manufacturing. This systematic literature review analyzes passive LbD for industrial assembly processes, focusing on the perception architecture and the generalization of the perceived demonstration. We specifically investigate one-shot approaches where only a single demonstration is required. The review evaluates how systems adapt to new assemblies using this limited data. We identify a shift towards object-centric perception, allowing learned primitives to be transferred to new product variants with minimal training.

</details>


### [98] [EquiGQNet: Fast Grasp Quality Evaluation via Shared Equivariant Point Cloud Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07145)
*Sungwon Seo,Jaeseog Won,Jiyou Shin,Youngjin Seo,Hyunjun Kim,Seokmin Yoon,Tuan Luong,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EquiGQNet，一种结合早期融合与晚期融合优点的6自由度抓取质量评估器，通过SO(3)等变编码和中间层动作融合实现高效且准确的抓取规划，显著提升速度与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有早期融合方法虽能捕获抓取候选的局部几何，但需重复编码场景；晚期融合方法共享场景表示但丢失了抓取相关的局部几何。两者均无法兼顾效率与准确性。

Method: EquiGQNet 采用双管齐下：抓取方向使用 SO(3)-等变编码（编码一次-旋转）替代早期融合的旋转重编码，从共享场景编码中提取抓取对齐特征；抓取平移通过中间层动作融合（MAF）在全局聚合前注入抓取位置，保留每个候选的局部几何。

Result: 在仿真中，EquiGQNet 在复杂形状和有限可抓取区域物体上性能与早期融合相当，显著优于晚期融合，并将 CEM 规划时间从 3.31 秒降至 0.48 秒（6.9 倍加速）；真实场景整理任务中实现 95.2% 成功率与 230 次/小时，优于早期融合（153）和晚期融合（170）。

Conclusion: EquiGQNet 有效融合早期与晚期融合优势，在保持高抓取成功率的同时大幅提升规划效率，适用于真实世界杂乱场景中的机器人抓取。

Abstract: Planning six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) grasps for unseen objects in cluttered tabletop scenes from a single-view depth image requires accurate and efficient evaluation of diverse grasp candidates. Existing early-fusion methods capture local object geometry relative to each grasp candidate but repeatedly encode the scene, whereas late-fusion methods reuse a shared scene representation but may lose this grasp-relative local geometry. We propose EquiGQNet, an efficient 6-DoF grasp quality evaluator that combines the strengths of both approaches. For grasp orientation, EquiGQNet replaces the early-fusion operation of rotating and re-encoding the point cloud for each grasp candidate with an SO(3)-equivariant encode-once-then-rotate scheme, yielding grasp-aligned geometric features from a shared scene encoding. For grasp translation, Mid-level Action Fusion (MAF) injects the grasp position into intermediate features before global aggregation, retaining local geometry relative to each candidate. We evaluate EquiGQNet in two grasp planning pipelines: Cross-Entropy Method (CEM)-based continuous grasp search and candidate ranking with a pretrained generative planner. In simulation, EquiGQNet achieves grasping performance comparable to the early-fusion baseline and substantially outperforms late fusion on objects with complex geometry and limited graspable regions, while reducing CEM planning time from 3.31s to 0.48s, a 6.9x speedup over early fusion. In real-world household-object decluttering, EquiGQNet achieves a 95.2% grasp success rate and 230 picks per hour, versus 153 and 170 for early- and late-fusion baselines. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Task Success: Stage-Wise Reliability of World Model Planning under Sensing Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07126)
*Geonmyeong Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 世界模型规划中，感知扰动在表征、预测、规划偏好和物理结果各阶段的影响并不一致，阶段式诊断可定位其衰减或持续之处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传感器输入在影响规划决策前需经过编码器和预测器，仅靠最终任务成功率无法揭示感知扰动在流水线中的衰减或持续位置，因此需要系统追踪扰动在各阶段的影响。

Method: 对世界模型规划器施加10种视觉和时间退化，并在相同的50个任务上通过配对评估，追踪这些退化对表征、未来预测、规划者偏好和物理结果的影响；同时用另一个操作任务和不同世界模型进行二次评估。

Result: 退化影响的相对大小在不同阶段间并不一致：较大的表征偏移可能在下游减弱，而较小的初始偏移可能持续到最终结果，内部响应排序与物理结果排序不直接吻合。时间退化也呈现不同模式：即使观察历史的整体变化相似，响应也因损坏信息的位置和规划者的实际暴露程度而大不相同；二次评估中也观察到这种非均匀的阶段式响应。

Conclusion: 阶段式诊断能够识别感知扰动在何处衰减或持续，有助于优先安排后续的模型验证和传感缓解措施。

Abstract: In world model planning, sensing inputs pass through an encoder and predictor before affecting planner decisions, so final task success alone cannot reveal where sensing disturbances attenuate or persist in the pipeline. We apply 10 visual and temporal sensing degradations to a world model planner and track their effects across representation, future prediction, planner preference, and physical outcome using paired evaluation on the same 50 tasks. The relative impact of degradations was not preserved across stages: large representation shifts could attenuate downstream, while smaller initial shifts could persist to the outcome, and internal-response ordering did not directly match physical-outcome ordering. Temporal degradations also showed distinct patterns: even with similar overall changes in observation history, responses differed substantially with the location of corrupted information and the planner's actual exposure. This non-uniform stage-wise response was also observed in secondary evaluations with another manipulation task and a different world model. Stage-wise diagnosis can therefore identify where sensing disturbances attenuate or persist and help prioritize subsequent model verification and sensing mitigation.

</details>


### [100] [MAC-I$^2$: Learned Metrics-Aware Covariance for Robust Visual-Inertial Fusion in Initialization and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07116)
*Xiang Fei,Yuheng Qiu,Can Xu,Yutian Chen,Ruogu Li,Xingxing Zuo,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAC-I²，通过为视觉和惯性模态学习度量感知的协方差，实现鲁棒的视觉-惯性融合，显著提升初始化与校准的精度和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-惯性融合方法使用预定义的不确定性，无法反映各模态在局部环境中的实际可靠性，在光照变化、动态物体、无纹理区域等挑战场景下性能不佳。

Method: 视觉侧将学习的特征匹配不确定性传播到位姿协方差；惯性侧设计带可学习初始协方差的IMU模型，并提出专用微调策略在留出训练子集上实现度量感知协方差，使预测协方差反映真实噪声量级。

Result: 在EuRoC和VBR数据集上验证：EuRoC上初始化成功率99.9%，重力误差和速度误差比最强基线降低约60%和42%；在挑战性VBR序列上保持80%成功率，而VINS-Mono等基线低于10%。

Conclusion: MAC-I²通过度量感知协方差让视觉和惯性按各自实际可靠性参与融合，显著优于现有方法，为可靠的VI系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Visual-Inertial (VI) fusion is fundamental to accurate and robust state estimation, where camera and IMU measurements are combined according to their respective uncertainties. Existing methods, however, fuse the two modalities with predefined uncertainties, regardless of how reliable each is in the local context, and thus often struggle under challenging environments involving illumination changes, dynamic objects, and textureless regions. In this paper, we present MAC-I$^2$, which achieves robust VI fusion through learned metric-aware covariance for both modalities, so that vision and IMU compete on their own merits rather than relying on predefined uncertainties. Here, metrics-aware means that each predicted covariance faithfully reflects the actual magnitude of the corresponding measurement noise. On the visual side, we propagate learned feature-matching uncertainties into pose covariances for the fusion. On the inertial side, motivated by the observation that integration error accumulates sharply at the early stage and grows slowly afterward, we design a learned IMU model with a learnable initial covariance, and propose a dedicated fine-tuning strategy on a held-out training subset to enable the metrics-aware covariance on unseen sequences. As a showcase, we build a VI initialization and calibration system, since accurate and robust initialization and calibration are the prerequisite for any reliable VI system. Experiments on EuRoC, and VBR show that MAC-I$^2$ substantially outperforms existing methods: it achieves a 99.9% initialization success rate on EuRoC, reducing gravity and velocity errors by about 60% and 42% over the strongest baseline, and maintains 80% success rate on challenging VBR sequences where baseline methods such as VINS-Mono drop below 10%.

</details>


### [101] [From LLM-Generated Specifications to Learned Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07111)
*Merve Atasever,Keyan Azbijari,Cagan Bakirci,Alfredo Reina Corona,Tolga Izdas,Richard Yang,Erdem Biyik,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用大语言模型自动生成参数化信号时序逻辑（PSTL）规范，用于四足机器人运动策略的强化学习训练。在MuJoCo XLA（MJX）环境中，将LLM生成的PSTL模板实例化为奖励函数，并采用PPO训练策略，实验表明该方法在高速命令跟踪和步态匹配上显著优于Text2Reward等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人运动策略通常依赖手工设计的奖励函数，但手工设计成本高、可解释性差，且难以预知局部奖励对全局行为的影响。STL等正式规范可提升可解释性，但编写STL仍需要大量领域专业知识。为填补这一空白，研究探讨LLM能否自动生成PSTL规范，从而降低对人工专家经验的依赖。

Method: 首先，给定自然语言运动目标和受限规范语法，使用GPT-5.5和Qwen 3.6独立生成STL模板，涵盖命令跟踪、安全和步态结构。然后，利用专家轨迹对生成模板的参数进行实例化，并保留与专家行为一致的规范。接着，将最终的规范转换为光滑的有限历史奖励函数，并结合PPO算法在MuJoCo XLA（MJX）中训练四足机器人运动策略。研究评估了步态感知（gait-aware）和步态无关（gait-agnostic）两种设置，并分别与手工奖励、Text2Reward风格的LLM奖励代码以及专家切换oracle进行对比。

Result: 在步态感知设置下，由Qwen 3.6生成的规范在0.3–2.1 m/s的所有测试速度上实现了100%的存活率和命令成功率，并在高速区域正确匹配目标步态；而Text2Reward在速度≥1.9 m/s时两个指标均为0%。步态无关设置中接触模式可从任务目标自然涌现。总体而言，LLM生成的PSTL奖励在多种速度下均表现出较强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，LLM能够结合语法约束与专家轨迹来生成高质量的PSTL奖励规范，不仅减少了人工设计奖励的工作量，还提升了奖励的可解释性和策略性能。尤其在高速运动任务中，该方法显著优于Text2Reward等替代方案，为四足机器人自动化策略学习提供了一种有效且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Quadruped robot locomotion policies are often trained using reinforcement learning, which in turn relies heavily on hand-crafted reward functions. Designing reward functions requires substantial manual engineering, and it is often unclear which local rewards will induce the desired global behavior. Shaped rewards from formal specifications in languages like Signal Temporal Logic (STL) can make rewards more interpretable, but writing STL specifications itself still requires domain expertise. We study whether large language models (LLMs) can fill this gap by generating Parametric Signal Temporal Logic (PSTL) specifications that are subsequently used for policy learning. Given a natural language locomotion objective and a constrained specification grammar, GPT-5.5 and Qwen 3.6 independently propose STL templates for command tracking, safety, and gait structure. We instantiate the parameters of the generated PSTL templates using expert trajectories and retain only specifications that are consistent with demonstrated expert behavior. The resulting specifications are then transformed into smooth, finite-history reward functions and used to train a quadruped locomotion policy with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in MuJoCo XLA (MJX). We evaluate both \emph{gait-aware} and \emph{gait-agnostic} settings. The former specifies walking-trot, trot, and bound regimes, while the latter allows contact patterns to emerge from the task objective. We compare against hand-engineered rewards, Text2Reward-style LLM-generated reward code, and an expert-switching oracle. Gait-aware Qwen 3.6 specifications achieved 100\% survival and command success across all tested speeds (0.3--2.1 m/s) and matched the target gait at high speeds, whereas Text2Reward achieved 0\% for both metrics at $\geq 1.9$ m/s. Videos: this https URL

</details>


### [102] [Eventually Optimal and Scalable Multi-Agent Planning for Block Cave Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07109)
*Christopher Leet,Paolo Forte,Uwe Köckemann,Henrik Andreasson,Sven Koenig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并形式化Block Cave Mining (BCM)问题，旨在最大化矿石吞吐量同时满足放矿比例约束；开发了SAMM（最优任意时间求解器）及其高效变体SAMMS，实验表明SAMMS在较大规模场景中接近最优吞吐量且扩展性良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下采矿自动化能提升安全、效率和可持续性，但动态矿井环境中协调自动驾驶车队在优化和运动规划上存在挑战，需要有效解决运输规划问题。

Method: 将BCM问题建模为混合整数线性规划，提出SAMM，联合集成任务分配、调度和路径规划；为提升可扩展性，提出SAMMS，通过将问题分解为较短的规划子周期，以牺牲最优性换取效率。

Result: 在现实工业矿山场景的实验评估中，SAMMS实现了接近最优的吞吐量，并能有效扩展到更大的车队和矿山布局。

Conclusion: SAMMS在效率和可扩展性上表现优越，适合实际大规模应用，而SAMM提供最优性保障，为自动化矿山运输规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automation in underground mining has the potential to significantly enhance safety, operational efficiency, and sustainability. However, effectively coordinating fleets of autonomous vehicles in dynamic mine environments introduces substantial challenges in both optimization and motion planning. To address these challenges, we introduce and formalize the \emph{Block Cave Mining (BCM)} problem, which focuses on computing a transport plan that maximizes ore throughput while satisfying draw ratio constraints. To solve this problem, we propose SAMM, an eventually optimal anytime solver that jointly integrates task assignment, scheduling, and path planning via a mixed-integer linear programming formulation. To improve scalability, we also introduce SAMMS, a variant of SAMM that trades optimality guarantees for efficiency by decomposing the problem into shorter planning subcycles. Experimental evaluations using realistic industrial mine scenarios demonstrate that SAMMS achieves near-optimal throughput and scales effectively to larger fleets and mine layouts.

</details>


### [103] [RoboDreamer: Anticipatory Humanoid Locomotion with Predictive State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07096)
*Zhe Li,Yangyang Wei,Xichen Yuan,Zhenzhe Zhang,Weihao Yuan,Shanghang Zhang,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboDreamer，通过两阶段教师-学生蒸馏和随机时间掩码结合Mamba骨干，实现人形机器人在不完美感知下的鲁棒运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形运动控制需要在感知不完美的条件下保持稳定，并利用时间上下文实现一致运动，而现有方法对历史信息的利用和掩码处理不足。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：先基于干净观测训练教师策略，再通过随机连续时间掩码蒸馏学生策略，使其从历史推断缺失当前信息；推理时复用掩码接口进行隐式闭环动作细化和可选多步分块，并使用Mamba作为时间骨干。

Result: 在IsaacLab、MuJoCo和Unitree G1上的实验显示，观测掩码下能实现鲁棒运动跟踪并成功部署；消融表明掩码/蒸馏提供主要增益，Mamba进一步改善跟踪且保持实时延迟。

Conclusion: RoboDreamer结合时间掩码蒸馏与Mamba结构，显著增强掩码下的鲁棒性，并能在真实机器人上实时运行，验证了其实际应用价值。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion requires control policies that remain stable under imperfect sensing while exploiting temporal context for consistent motion. We present RoboDreamer, a two-stage teacher--student framework that combines next-observation consistency with randomized continuous temporal masking. A teacher is first trained on clean observations, and a student is then distilled under masked recent observations, encouraging the policy to infer missing current information from history. At inference, the same masking interface is reused for implicit closed-loop action refinement and optional multi-step action chunking. Mamba is used as the temporal backbone, while matched ablations show that masking/distillation provides a substantial part of the gain and Mamba contributes additional tracking improvements with real-time latency. Experiments in IsaacLab, MuJoCo, and on a Unitree G1 demonstrate robust motion tracking under observation masking and successful real-world deployment.

</details>


### [104] [Human-Aware Target Tracking and Navigation: Fusing Kinematic State Estimation with Structural Map Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07091)
*Sagar Gupta,Don Gideon,Seng W. Loke,Kevin Lee,Bijo Sebastian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端自主导航栈，利用地图推理在目标遮挡时预测可行轨迹，实现鲁棒的人员跟随，在真实密集环境中遮挡长达7秒时重获取成功率达71.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人执行人员跟随任务时，经常因动态环境中的暂时遮挡和传感器跟踪丢失而失效，需要一种能在跟踪丢失期间维持安全、可预测跟随的策略。

Method: 系统融合深度学习视觉跟踪与2D LiDAR点聚类的多模态感知，结合轮式里程计和IMU进行连续状态估计。当跟踪丢失时，激活基于拓扑地图的恢复框架，通过图搜索沿结构定义的行走道（遵守左侧交通规则）传播目标最后已知轨迹，生成离散可行未来轨迹，推理可能的结构变化（如直行或转弯），并将预测直接输入局部避障规划器。

Result: 在真实密集多人的环境中评估，系统在长达7秒的重大遮挡事件中达到71.4%的目标重获取成功率。

Conclusion: 利用地图信息的空间推理方案能有效应对遮挡造成的跟踪丢失，使机器人安全、可预测地继续跟随直至视觉重新捕获目标，增强了系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots performing person-following tasks often suffer from temporary occlusions and sensor track loss in dynamic environments. This research presents an end-to-end autonomous navigation stack that addresses target occlusion through map-informed spatial reasoning. The proposed system features a multi-modal perception pipeline, fusing deep learning-based visual tracking with 2-dimensional LiDAR point clustering to maintain high-fidelity tracking of a tagged person. A continuous state estimator integrates this perception data with wheel odometry and IMU sensors for stable localization. When the active track is lost due to occlusion, the system activates a map-based recovery framework. Leveraging a predefined topological map, the system executes a graph-based search to propagate the target's last known trajectory along structurally defined walking lanes, adhering to left-hand regional conventions. By generating a discrete set of feasible future trajectories, the robot reasons about potential structural trajectory changes, such as continuing a heading or turning at an intersection. This map-informed prediction is fed directly to the local obstacle avoidance planner, enabling the robot to continue following its target safely and predictably until the person is visually reacquired. Real-world evaluations in dense multi-person environments demonstrate the system's robustness, achieving a 71.4\% target reacquisition success rate during major occlusion events lasting up to 7 seconds.

</details>


### [105] [Large Discrete Policy: Advancing Explicit Behavior Modeling with Stochastic Iterative Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07049)
*Zhenxin Li,Nadine Chang,Xinglong Sun,Jingde Chen,Wenhao Yao,Zi Wang,Maying Shen,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu,Shiyi Lan,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出大规模离散策略（LDiP），通过从大规模物理合理候选动作中迭代评分与剪枝，以离散方式实现连续生成模型的表达能力，在自动驾驶和机器人操作中超越或匹配连续策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续生成模型作为行为策略虽然表达力强，但迭代去噪过程难以解释且易产生不合理动作；而离散策略因缺乏有效评分机制通常被视为表达能力不足。

Method: LDiP 构建一个大规模离散动作词汇表，仅包含物理上合理的候选；不扰动动作，而是通过带随机性的迭代评分在分数空间中逐步细化排名并剪枝候选，在保留明确决策过程的同时实现细粒度探索和排序。

Result: 在端到端规划、闭环驾驶、机器人操作和视觉-语言-动作等多种场景下，LDiP 在自动驾驶任务中一致优于强离散和连续基线，在机器人操作中超越或匹配连续生成策略。

Conclusion: 当离散策略配备有效的评分机制后，可以提供一种具有表达力、合理性和可解释性的行为建模替代方案。

Abstract: Behavior policies are often formulated as continuous generative models, whose iterative denoising processes are expressive but difficult to interpret and prone to producing implausible actions. We propose the Large Discrete Policy (LDiP), a fully discrete behavior modeling framework that selects actions from a large vocabulary of physically plausible candidates. Rather than perturbing actions, LDiP improves expressivity through stochastic iterative scoring: it progressively re-scores and prunes candidates with score-space stochasticity, enabling fine-grained ranking and exploration among plausible actions while preserving an explicit decision process. Across end-to-end planning, closed-loop driving, robotic manipulation, and vision-language-action settings, LDiP consistently outperforms strong discrete and continuous baselines in autonomous driving, and exceeds or matches continuous generative policies in robotic manipulation. These results show that discrete policies, when equipped with effective scoring mechanisms, offer an expressive, plausible, and interpretable alternative for behavior modeling. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [106] [MEMOBench: A Process Level Memory Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07047)
*Haiyang Sun,Haoxiao Wang,Junming Chen,Weicheng Fang,Zihao Su,Jingkun Yi,Wenyou Yi,Hao Chen,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MEMOBench基准，用于机器人操作任务中记忆的细粒度过程级评估，包含30个历史依赖任务、4200个可执行检查点，并定义存储、更新、压缩三类记忆指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略评估多依赖当前观测决定动作，而机器人操作常需处理不可见信息；现有记忆基准仅用最终任务成功判定，混淆了记忆遗忘与操作失败，缺乏对记忆过程本身的评测。

Method: 构建MEMOBench，包含30个历史依赖任务、84个模板、1500条专家演示和4200个可执行检查点；每个检查点将粗到细语言与模拟器谓词配对，标注记忆操作（存储、更新、压缩），并据此定义三个速率指标；还利用检查点语言设计语义、对比和逐帧记忆对齐目标来辅助训练。

Result: 最强记忆模块基线平均成功率仅31.9%，且高存储率常伴随低更新和压缩率；基于检查点语言的辅助训练目标带来小幅提升，但效果有限。

Conclusion: MEMOBench提供了一套诊断性评估工具和训练监督信号，揭示了当前VLA策略在记忆操作上的严重短板，为记忆增强的机器人策略研究提供了新基准和方向。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires acting on information that is no longer visible, yet Vision-Language-Action policies are usually evaluated when the current observation largely determines the next action. Existing robotic memory benchmarks expose this gap, but they still rely mainly on final task success and therefore conflate forgetting with manipulation failure. We present \textbf{MEMOBench}, a benchmark for process level memory evaluation in robotic manipulation. MEMOBench includes 30 history dependent tasks, 1{,}500 expert demonstrations, and 4{,}200 executable checkpoint instances from 84 templates. Each checkpoint pairs coarse to fine language with a simulator predicate and labels one memory operation: Storage, Update, or Compression. These annotations define Memory Storage Rate, Memory Update Rate, and Memory Compression Rate, which measure memory fidelity alongside task success. Across standard and memory augmented VLA policies, the strongest memory module baseline reaches only 31.9\% average success rate, and high storage often coexists with weak update and compression. Checkpoint language also supervises semantic, contrastive, and framewise memory alignment objectives, yielding modest gains across different memory operations. MEMOBench provides a diagnostic evaluation suite and training supervision for memory grounded robotic policies. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [107] [WM-Craftnet: World Synesthesia Model for Generalizable and Robust Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07002)
*Jie Yin,Zeyuan Zhao,Xiaojing Tan,Yang Liu,Chiyu Wang,Xinyang Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WM-Craftnet，一种基于世界模型条件的灵巧手操作框架，利用多模态潜动态预测作为策略上下文，通过干净深度重建和触觉信号提升鲁棒性，并支持跨物体迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉和视觉触觉强化学习方法在受控环境下表现良好，但在位姿偏移、力扰动和物体变化下鲁棒性下降，且部分观测和噪声影响隐式状态推断。

Method: WM-Craftnet学习紧凑的动作条件潜动力学模型（World Synesthesia Model, WSM），由本体感觉、深度、触觉和动作监督，使用多模态重建和奖励预测。WSM不用于想象或策略优化，而是作为非对称Actor-Critic策略的循环任务上下文。训练中利用噪声深度输入重建干净深度，提供去噪几何状态；并在九种z轴物体上预训练WSM，作为49物体下游策略学习的先验。

Result: 消融实验表明预测式世界建模、干净深度监督和触觉接触线索均影响所学状态；预训练WSM提升多物体旋转性能，并在未见物体、扰动恢复和sim-to-real迁移中展现定性和定量优势。

Conclusion: 通过世界模型条件化和干净深度重建，可显著提升灵巧手操作策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性，且预训练世界模型可复用至大规模下游任务。

Abstract: Generalizable and robust dexterous in-hand manipulation requires a policy to infer object pose, geometry, contact, and potential slip from partial and noisy observations. Although recent tactile and visuotactile RL methods achieve strong in-hand rotation in controlled settings, their robustness often degrades under pose shifts, force disturbances, and object variation. We propose WM-Craftnet, a world-model-conditioned framework that learns compact action-conditioned latent dynamics from proprioception, depth, tactile sensing, and actions, supervised by multimodal reconstruction and reward prediction. Rather than using the world model for latent imagination or policy optimization, WM-Craftnet uses the learned World Synesthesia Model (WSM) as recurrent task context for an asymmetric actor--critic policy. Importantly, WSM is trained to reconstruct clean depth targets from noisy depth inputs, providing a denoised geometric state for real-robot deployment. Ablations over recurrent baselines, auxiliary heads, tactile masking, and WSM modality heads show that predictive world modeling, clean-depth supervision, and tactile contact cues all shape the learned state. A WSM pretrained on nine \(z\)-axis objects serves as a reusable prior for \(49\)-object downstream policy learning. This context improves multi-object rotation, with quantitative and qualitative evidence for unseen-object, perturbation-recovery, and sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [108] [Mind the Phase: Effective Rank and Representation Health in Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06958)
*Felipe Tommaselli,Thiago H. Segreto,Juliano D. Negri,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过策略雅可比矩阵的有效秩来研究腿式机器人强化学习策略的表示结构，发现网络架构（层归一化与残差连接）会在摆动相和支撑相之间分配不同的有效秩维度，并据此提出一种简单方法，将这种表示特征转化为更平滑、更可靠的仿真到现实迁移，在真实Spot上实现了约3倍的关节抖动降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在腿式机器人强化学习中，PPO的非平稳性使浅层网络成为事实标准，但习得策略的表示结构缺乏理解，也没有训练时信号来预测硬件上的行为表现。现有全局有效秩分析掩盖了步态相位相关的架构差异，需要更细粒度的表征工具。

Method: 实证研究策略雅可比矩阵的有效秩，并创新性地将秩条件化于步态相位，以揭示全局平均会掩盖的架构结构。发现标准架构（层归一化、残差连接）相比普通MLP在摆动相多分配约两个有效秩维度。基于此提出一个简单配方，将这些表示特征转化为更平滑、更可靠的sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 所提方法在仿真和物理Spot上均实现了约3倍的关节抖动降低，表明表示健康可作为训练时透镜来追踪sim-to-real平滑性。

Conclusion: 策略雅可比矩阵的相位条件化有效秩是一种有效的训练时指标，能反映架构差异并预测硬件平滑性，利用这一表示签名可显著提升真实世界部署的可靠性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become the leading paradigm in legged locomotion, enabling complex behaviors from backflips to parkour through massively parallel simulation. Under PPO's non-stationarity, shallow networks remain the de facto architecture, supported by carefully staged curricula and environments, yet the representations these policies learn stay poorly understood, leaving no training-time signal of how they will behave on hardware. In this work, we empirically study locomotion policies through the effective rank of the policy Jacobian and show that conditioning rank on the gait phase exposes architectural structure that global rank averages away. In particular, we find that standard architectural choices, namely layer normalization and residual connections, allocate roughly two more dimensions of effective rank to swing than to stance, which is fully absent in vanilla MLPs. Building on this, we propose a simple recipe that turns these representational signatures into smoother, more reliable sim-to-real transfer. In practice, this results in roughly 3x lower joint jitter that holds from simulation onto a physical Spot, suggesting that representation health is an effective training-time lens to track sim-to-real smoothness.

</details>


### [109] [Distributed Dexterous Manipulation with Spatially Conditioned Multi-Agent Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06930)
*Sarvesh Patil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于空间条件多智能体Transformer（MAT）的框架，用于高效学习分布式灵巧操作（DDM）系统的鲁棒控制策略。该系统由64个软体delta机器人以8x8网格排列构成。通过自适应层归一化、空间对比嵌入和基于MAT的行为克隆结合SAC微调，实现了长时程平面操作任务，并在真实世界中以约65%更少的机器人达到约1.5厘米的平均误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式灵巧操作（DDM）由于高动作空间冗余、机器人间协作以及动态物体-机器人交互，带来了显著的控制挑战。现有方法难以高效学习鲁棒策略，且需在任务性能与机器人使用数量间取得平衡，以减少磨损和维护。

Method: 提出基于空间条件多智能体Transformer（MAT）的框架，包含三个核心贡献：(i) 带自适应层归一化的MAT以提高计算效率；(ii) 空间对比嵌入，将Transformer嵌入与机器人的空间配置相结合；(iii) 基于MAT的行为克隆方法，并使用Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）进行微调。此外，提出一种动作选择公式，用于分析任务性能与所用机器人数量之间的权衡。

Result: 实验表明，MAT通过堆叠的注意力块迭代地细化动作，空间条件有利于学习DDM策略。在仿真和真实世界中，对多种几何形状的物体实现了长时程平面操作任务。在真实世界中，该方法在沿不同轨迹操作物体时，平均误差约1.5厘米，同时使用约65%更少的机器人，并通过减少机器人间碰撞降低了磨损。

Conclusion: 空间条件多智能体Transformer为分布式灵巧操作提供了一种高效且鲁棒的控制策略学习方法，能够在减少机器人使用的条件下保持任务性能，从而降低维护成本并延长系统寿命。

Abstract: Distributed Dexterous Manipulation (DDM) is a novel paradigm that presents significant control challenges due to high action-space redundancy, inter-robot cooperation, and dynamic object-robot interactions. This paper introduces a framework based on spatially conditioned Multi-Agent Transformers (MATs) to efficiently learn robust control policies for a DDM system grounded in an array of 64 soft delta robots arranged in an 8x8 grid. Our three core contributions are: (i) an MAT with adaptive layer norm for compute efficiency, (ii) spatial contrastive embeddings to ground transformer embeddings in the spatial configuration of the robots, and (iii) an MAT-based behavior cloning method fine-tuned using Soft Actor Critic. We also propose an action selection formulation to analyze the trade-off between task performance and the number of robots utilized. Our experiments show that MATs iteratively refine their actions through the stacked attention blocks. This further informs the benefit of spatial conditioning in transformers to learn DDM policies. We demonstrate long-horizon planar manipulation tasks with objects of various geometries in simulation and real-world. Finally, we show how action selection mitigates robot maintenance by reducing wear and tear due to inter-robot collisions while maintaining the ability to manipulate objects along various trajectories in the real-world, achieving an average error of ~1.5 cm, while using ~65% fewer robots.

</details>


### [110] [DriftParking: Trajectory Modeling via Drifting Field for End-to-End Automated Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06923)
*Ziyan Wang,Dong Li,Weibo Wang,Yinyin Lu,Jiayu Xie,Jiamao Gu,Dongpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DriftParking，一种基于漂移场范式的一步式轨迹生成框架，通过在端点残差空间中构造条件吸引与自适应排斥，实现高精度、可执行的端到端泊车轨迹生成，并在实车闭环中达到 97% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端泊车方法难以同时满足推理效率、轨迹质量和精确端点对齐；而传统模仿目标对专家机动几何的结构化偏差监督有限，导致在高度受限空间中终点误差容限低的问题。

Method: 重建漂移场范式：将分布级吸引替换为指向配对专家轨迹的条件一对一吸引；引入以专家为中心的构造性排斥；在接近收敛时自适应衰减排斥。将轨迹生成形式化为端点残差空间，分解为起始到目标基线与可学习残差，使端点对齐成为监督目标的结构约束，同时为排斥监督提供结构化空间。

Result: 在所有评估指标上达到最优性能；跨多种泊车场景的闭环实车实验显示 97% 泊车成功率，展现很强的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: DriftParking 通过漂移场重构与端点残差表示，有效解决端到端泊车中精度与质量兼顾的难题，为高精度条件轨迹生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automated parking requires generating complete and executable trajectories in highly constrained spaces with low tolerance for goal pose error. Existing end-to-end parking methods struggle to jointly achieve inference efficiency, trajectory quality, and precise endpoint alignment, while conventional imitation objectives provide limited supervision on structured deviations from expert maneuver geometry. We propose DriftParking, a one-step trajectory generation framework that reconstructs the drifting-field paradigm for high-precision conditional trajectory generation.
