Specifically, we replace distribution-level attraction with conditional one-to-one attraction toward the paired expert trajectory, introduce expert-centered constructive repulsion, and adaptively attenuate repulsion near convergence. We further formulate trajectory generation in an endpoint-residual space by decomposing each trajectory into a start-to-goal baseline and a learnable residual, turning endpoint alignment into a representation-level structural constraint on the supervision target while providing a structured space for repulsive supervision. DriftParking achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics. Closed-loop on-vehicle experiments across diverse parking scenarios further show a 97% parking success rate, demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [111] [Distributed Secure Learning Control for Large-scale Multirobots under Stealthy Actuator Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06896)
*Xinglong Zhang,Qingwen Ma,Cong Li,Hui Yin,Changxin Zhang,Yueying Wang,Wei Pan,Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向大规模多机器人系统的分布式安全学习控制（DSLC）框架，结合强化学习与分布式模型预测控制，通过博弈论架构在线学习攻防策略，以抵御隐蔽执行器攻击，并具备可扩展性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统分布式学习控制虽具灵活性，但缺乏性能保证；集成RL的DMPC有潜力，但在恶意隐蔽网络攻击下安全性不足，因为策略生成依赖邻居信息交换，易被快速影响。

Method: 构建分布式安全学习控制框架，采用基于微分博弈的DMPC，利用分布式攻击者-演员-评论家架构在线学习最优防御与攻击策略，生成解析闭环形式而非数值开环控制序列。

Result: 通过仿真与多轮式机器人真实实验验证，该方法在不同控制任务中有效且可扩展，防御策略可泛化至不同规模及攻击概率的MRS。

Conclusion: DSLC实现了主动攻防平衡，在保持学习控制灵活性的同时提供可证明的性能安全，适用于多种协调场景下的恶意隐蔽执行器攻击防护。

Abstract: Distributed learning control for multirobot systems (MRS) offers significant flexibility in presence of uncertainties but lacks provable performance guarantees. A promising direction involves integrating reinforcement learning (RL) into distributed model predictive control (DMPC), leveraging the strengths of RL in nonlinear policy design and the receding-horizon replanning capabilities of DMPC. However, ensuring secure control within such a learning framework under malicious cyber attacks, particularly stealthy ones, remains a critical challenge, because the distributed policies generation depends on information exchange among neighbors, where compromised agents can rapidly influence the behavior of others through the communication network. This article proposes a distributed secure learning control (DSLC) framework for large-scale MRS under malicious, stealthy actuator attacks. Our framework offers two key features: (i) a unified approach that enables secure learning control across various coordination scenarios and (ii) a game-theoretic distributed learning-based predictive control strategy that learns how to balance the attacker and defender through a differential-game based DMPC framework. Specifically, DSLC employs a distributed attacker-actor-critic architecture to learn the optimal defense and attack policies online within each prediction interval. Unlike numerical optimization-based controllers that calculate open-loop control sequences, our method simultaneously generates adversarial attack policies and corresponding defense policies in analytical closed-loop form. The defense policies could be directly generalized to MRS with varying scales and diverse actuator attack probabilities. The effectiveness and scalability of DSLC are validated through comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments in multiple wheeled robots via various control tasks.

</details>


### [112] [Predictive-Coding-Based Autonomous Regulation of Internally Generated and Externally Coupled Processing in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06888)
*Henrique Oyama,Hiroki Sawada,Jun Tani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于预测编码的在线调节机制，通过积累的重建误差选择元先验，以动态平衡内部预测与外部感觉证据在物理人机交互中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预测编码将适应性行为描述为内部预测与外部感觉证据的动态平衡，但具身认知系统如何在持续交互中在线调节这种平衡仍不清楚。

Method: 扩展预测编码启发的变分递归神经网络（PV-RNN），引入元先验控制后验推断受学习先验动力学的约束程度，并设计在线机制，利用最近交互历史积累的重建误差在预定义元先验区间间切换。

Result: 在三个物理人机交互任务中，较低元先验增加后验-先验散度并减少重建误差；重建历史驱动的区间选择还降低了前瞻预测误差和机器人侧物理冲突。任务3表明能容纳近期感官观测，同时后续人类运动偏离模型先验生成的未来轨迹。

Conclusion: 积累的重建不匹配可作为内源性信号，调节后续推断依赖内部动力学与持续感觉输入的程度，支持具身交互中的在线平衡调控。

Abstract: Predictive coding characterizes adaptive behavior as a dynamic balance between internally generated predictions and external sensory evidence, yet how an embodied cognitive system can regulate this balance online during ongoing interaction remains poorly understood. This study proposes a predictive-coding-based mechanism for regulating internally generated and externally coupled processing during physical human--robot interaction. The framework employs a predictive-coding-inspired variational recurrent neural network (PV-RNN), in which a meta-prior controls the degree to which posterior inference is constrained by learned prior dynamics. We extend this architecture with an online mechanism that uses reconstruction error accumulated over recent interaction history to select between predefined meta-prior regimes. The mechanism was evaluated across three physical human--robot interaction tasks involving fixed structured, changing structured, and less-constrained interaction. Across all tasks, lower meta-prior values produced the expected increase in posterior--prior divergence and reduction in reconstruction error. More importantly, reconstruction-history-driven regime selection was also associated with reduced prospective prediction error and robot-side physical interaction conflict, demonstrating consequences beyond the retrospective reconstruction objective itself. Task~3 further showed that recent sensory observations can be successfully accommodated while subsequent human motion still departs from the model's prior-generated future trajectory. Overall, these findings show that accumulated reconstruction mismatch can provide an endogenous signal for regulating how strongly subsequent inference relies on learned internal dynamics relative to ongoing sensory input during embodied interaction.

</details>


### [113] [ContextFlow: In-Context Flow Matching for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06852)
*Jian Ding,Xianjie Dai,Roei Herzig,Nussair Hroub,Jinjie Mai,Dengxin Dai,Bernard Ghanem,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContextFlow 是一种将条件流匹配模型用于上下文模仿学习的方法，通过连续动作分布生成和感知器式多模态压缩器，在 LIBERO 基准上将未见任务配置的平均成功率比 ICRT 提高 35 个百分点，并无需微调即可匹配任务特定微调 VLA 模型 π0 的性能，在真实机器人上也展现出对新配置的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归上下文模仿方法将连续动作离散化，并通过 next-token 预测加剧早期误差累积，限制了对未见任务配置的泛化。虽然流匹配策略已被用于连续机器人控制并有助于缓解复合误差，但在流匹配框架内进行上下文模仿学习仍未被充分探索。

Method: 引入 ContextFlow，一种条件流匹配模型，学习连续动作分布以进行上下文模仿学习。它基于演示和观察条件化流式动作预测，实现从带噪动作分布中鲁棒生成。为更好编码多模态上下文演示，采用感知器风格的多模态上下文压缩器，将视觉、本体感觉和动作序列蒸馏为紧凑的任务相关潜在表征。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，ContextFlow 在未见任务配置上的平均成功率比 ICRT 高 35 个百分点，且无需任何微调即可匹配任务特定微调 VLA 模型 π0 的性能。在真实机器人上，它泛化到单臂和双臂任务的新配置，在一种新的开笔帽配置上达到 40% 成功率。

Conclusion: ContextFlow 有效结合了流匹配与上下文学习，通过连续动作生成和高效上下文压缩，显著提升了机器人操作在未见配置上的泛化能力，并证明了无需任务特定微调即可达到强基线水平。

Abstract: Although highly effective in vision and language domains, applying in-context learning to robotics remains challenging. Existing autoregressive in-context imitation methods discretize continuous actions and exacerbate the accumulation of early prediction errors through next-token prediction, limiting their generalization on unseen task configurations. Meanwhile, flow-matching policies have been explored for continuous robot control and can help mitigate compounding errors; however, in-context imitation learning within a flow-matching framework remains underexplored. To address these limitations, we introduce ContextFlow, a conditional flow-matching model that learns continuous action distributions for in-context imitation learning. ContextFlow conditions flow-based action prediction on demonstrations and observations, enabling robust generation from noisy action distributions. To better encode multimodal in-context demonstrations, we adapt perceiver-style multimodal context compressors that distill visual, proprioceptive, and action sequences into compact, task-relevant latent representations. On LIBERO, ContextFlow outperforms ICRT by 35 percentage points in average success rate on unseen task configurations, while matching the performance of the task-specific fine-tuned VLA model $\pi_0$ without any fine-tuning on unseen tasks. On real robots, it generalizes to unseen configurations of both single-arm and bimanual tasks, achieving 40% success on a new pen-uncapping configuration. Project Page: this https URL .

</details>


### [114] [Diagnosing and Dynamically Filtering Occupancy World Models for Active Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06820)
*Jiahui Zhang,Gongbo Liang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: This paper investigates how occupancy prediction errors in active mapping affect downstream planning performance, finding that correcting false positives or negatives alone does not consistently improve coverage, and that ground truth occupancy helps coverage efficiency more than endpoint coverage. A dynamic filtering strategy is proposed to suppress unsupported occupancy predictions and redirect viewpoints toward reachable surfaces.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Active mapping systems use pretrained occupancy networks to predict unobserved geometry, which influences both exploration choices and feasible motion. However, the impact of occupancy errors on planning performance is not well understood. The paper aims to diagnose these effects by isolating the occupancy representation.

Method: The study fixes the planner and varies only the occupancy representation: no completion, learned occupancy, learned occupancy with false positives removed by an oracle, false negatives restored by an oracle, and ground truth occupancy. Experiments compare final coverage and coverage efficiency. Based on findings, a dynamic filtering strategy is introduced that preserves predictions in unexplored space but suppresses repeatedly unsupported occupancy using online observations.

Result: Correcting false positives or negatives alone does not consistently improve final coverage. Ground truth occupancy provides much larger improvement in coverage efficiency than in endpoint coverage. Preliminary examples show the dynamic filtering strategy can redirect viewpoint selection toward reachable surfaces that would otherwise remain unobserved.

Conclusion: There is a gap between occupancy accuracy and downstream planning performance. Even with an accurate geometric world model, planning and reachability remain important bottlenecks. The proposed dynamic filtering strategy offers a promising direction to mitigate the negative effects of occupancy errors.

Abstract: Active mapping requires a robot to select camera viewpoints that efficiently reconstruct an unknown 3D scene. To reason about unobserved regions, recent systems use pretrained occupancy networks as world models that complete missing geometry. The predicted structure contributes to expected coverage gain and constrains feasible robot motion. Consequently, occupancy errors can change both what the robot chooses to explore and where it is able to move. We diagnose these effects by holding the planner fixed and varying only the occupancy representation provided to it. We consider planning without completion, with learned occupancy, with false positives removed by a ground truth oracle, with false negatives restored by an oracle, and with ground truth occupancy. Our experiments show that correcting false positives or false negatives alone does not consistently improve final coverage. This finding reveals a gap between occupancy accuracy and downstream planning performance. Ground truth occupancy provides a much larger improvement in coverage efficiency than in endpoint coverage, suggesting that planning and reachability remain important bottlenecks even when the geometric world model is accurate. Based on these findings, we introduce a dynamic filtering strategy that preserves predictions in unexplored space while suppressing repeatedly unsupported occupancy using online observations. Preliminary examples show that this strategy can redirect viewpoint selection toward reachable surfaces that would otherwise remain unobserved.

</details>


### [115] [RoboSense: Leveraging Robotaxi Fleets as Drive-by Sensors for Urban Traffic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06813)
*Yilin Wang,Yiheng Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将交通监控任务显式纳入目标的动态Robotaxi路由框架，通过MILP优化时空覆盖与出行时间，在SUMO仿真中验证了可同时提升监控性能和平均车速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robotaxi车队可作为移动传感器进行网络级交通监控，但现有路由优化未显式考虑监控任务。

Method: 构建基于单元的网络表示、单元级监控指标和混合整数线性规划（MILP），联合最小化时变旅行时间并最大化监控覆盖。

Result: 在5×5城市网格中，不同市场渗透率（2%,5%,10%）和权重组合下，加入时空覆盖目标可有效提升监控性能，且适当权重下监控性能与平均车速同时改善。

Conclusion: 网络监控与出行效率可形成双赢，激励Robotaxi运营商参与交通数据采集。

Abstract: Urban traffic monitoring plays a critical role in safety analysis, congestion management, and incident response. The growing deployment of robotaxis creates a new opportunity for network-level traffic monitoring. Although robotaxis are primarily designed to serve passengers, they can also be leveraged as drive-by sensors to collect traffic data. Compared to conventional infrastructure sensors or probe vehicles, a fleet of robotaxis forms a cooperative perception environment, which can collectively gather spatially and temporally continuous traffic information. This paper proposes a novel dynamic robotaxi routing framework that explicitly incorporates traffic monitoring tasks as an objective. The framework introduces: (1) a cell-based network representation that aligns with sensing capabilities of robotaxis; (2) a cell-level monitoring metric to quantify spatiotemporal robotaxi coverage; and (3) a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation that jointly minimizes time-dependent travel time and maximizes traffic monitoring performance. A 5 by 5 urban grid network is built in SUMO to evaluate the framework under three robotaxi market penetration rates (2%, 5%, and 10%) with a range of objective weight combinations. Results show that incorporating spatiotemporal network coverage in the objective function can effectively improve the traffic monitoring performance. Interestingly, with appropriate weights between the two objectives, monitoring performance and robotaxi average speed can be improved simultaneously. This suggests better network monitoring leads to more accurate traffic state prediction and improved mobility. This win-win situation could incentivize robotaxi operators to contribute their vehicles as drive-by sensors for traffic monitoring.

</details>


### [116] [OCTN: Neural OCT Representations for Robot-Guided Precision Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06810)
*Ravi Prakash,Ryan P. McNabb,Patrick J. Codd,Shan Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCTN是一种基于隐式神经表示的框架，可将OCT体扫描转换为连续、可微分的组织强度场，支持快速体积查询、机器人激光手术导航和稀疏采集下的密集重建，在保持高保真度的同时显著提升速度并减少采集时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT虽适于术中引导，但原生体数据离散采样、各向异性且不利于几何推理和机器人集成，需要一种连续、可微且空间准确的表示方法。

Method: 采用两阶段混合训练策略，结合体素监督与切片间插值，保留B扫描保真度并改善稀疏区域连续性；将学习到的组织表示存储在GPU上以支持快速空间查询；通过隐式表面发现和基于Newton/SGD的优化实现表面约束路径规划；并用于从稀疏B扫描重建密集体积。

Result: GPU查询速度比传统CPU处理快43倍；稀疏采集下减少4倍采集时间且保留临床结构；在Duke TissueOCT和公共数据集上PSNR>30dB，训练时间<10s，表面Chamfer距离误差小于10μm。

Conclusion: OCTN展示了隐式神经表示在OCT引导手术中的多功能性，兼顾速度与保真度，数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography (OCT) offers compact, contactless, micron-scale imaging suitable for intraoperative guidance, but native OCT volumes are discretely sampled, anisotropic, and currently inefficient for downstream geometric reasoning and robot integration. We present OCTN (pronounced "octane"), an implicit neural representation framework that converts volumetric OCT scans into a continuous, differentiable, and spatially faithful tissue-intensity field. OCTN uses a two-stage hybrid training strategy that combines supervision from acquired voxels with inter-slice interpolations, preserving B-scan fidelity while improving continuity in sparsely sampled regions. For versatility, we first show that OCTN enables fast volumetric reasoning by storing the learned tissue representation natively on the GPU, supporting intensity-based spatial queries with up to 43x speedup over conventional CPU processing. We then demonstrate OCTN-enabled OCT-guided robotic laser surgery where the continuous tissue representation supports implicit surface discovery and surface-constrained path planning via multiple optimization strategies, including Newton- and SGD-based optimization. Next, OCTN enables reconstruction of dense volumetric structure from sparsely acquired B-scans, while reducing acquisition time by 4x and preserving clinically relevant structures. Across the newly generated Duke TissueOCT dataset and public OCT datasets, OCTN achieves robust, high-fidelity reconstruction with PSNR > 30 dB and training time < 10 s, while preserving surface consistency within 10 $\mu$m Chamfer distance relative to baseline reconstruction. The TissueOCT dataset and code are publicly available at this http URL

</details>


### [117] [SkillX: Unified Multi-Skill Policy Learning for Humanoid Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06718)
*Zhangchen Ye,Enxuan Ruan,Yifei Bao,Runhan Huang,Jiankun Yang,Jiakang Jin,Yixiao Huo,Pengyuan Wang,Yinan Han,Huaxing Huang,Wenhao Cui,Yiming Li,Xiaoyu Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SkillX，一个用于人形足球的统一强化学习框架，通过单一条件策略学习并组合多种原子技能，实现带球、停球和射门等动作的长期技能切换，并在仿真和真实Noetix E1人形机器人上验证了鲁棒性和Sim-to-Real部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形体育方法依赖任务特定的多阶段流水线，难以在单个可部署策略中联合学习和组合多种物体交互技能。

Method: SkillX整合三种核心设计：技能特定的对抗式运动先验、技能特定的评论家，以及物体感知的时间编码器，通过单一命令条件策略执行原子技能并在其间切换。

Result: 在仿真和真实Noetix E1人形机器人上展示了鲁棒的多技能执行、长时程技能组合以及成功的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: SkillX为复杂人形足球技能的统一学习与组合提供了一种有效方案，并验证了其在实际硬件上的可部署性。

Abstract: Humanoid soccer is a challenging testbed for dynamic whole-body control, requiring robots to coordinate balance, locomotion, object interaction, and skill switching over long horizons. Existing humanoid sports methods often rely on task-specific multi-stage pipelines, making it difficult to jointly learn and compose multiple object-interactive skills within a single deployable policy. To address this, we present SkillX, a unified reinforcement learning framework that learns and composes multiple atomic soccer skills through a single command-conditioned policy. SkillX integrates three core designs: skill-specific adversarial motion priors, skill-specific critics, and an object-aware temporal encoder, enabling the robot to execute atomic skills and transition among them such as dribbling, trapping, and shooting. Experiments in simulation and on a real Noetix E1 humanoid demonstrate robust multi-skill execution, long-horizon skill composition, and successful sim-to-real deployment.

</details>


### [118] [CAVEAT: Recurrent Multimodal Diffusion Planning for Mapless Aerial Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06623)
*Steven Visch,Nicolò Botteghi,Antonio Franchi,Barbara Bazzana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CAVEAT，一种基于扩散策略的无人机自主探索方法，利用循环内部状态融合多模态传感器数据，无需全局地图即可生成航点序列，并在仿真和真实环境中验证了部分探索与视觉伺服能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否在不维护全局地图的条件下，仅凭机载多模态观测和固定维循环内部状态生成无人机探索航点序列，以降低对地图构建的依赖并提升部署效率。

Method: CAVEAT采用扩散策略，条件变量为循环内部状态（由LiDAR、视觉和位姿特征融合更新），训练数据来自基于地图的FUELv2专家轨迹。推理时采用滚动部分热启动连续预测，并利用临时局部符号距离场提供障碍物规避启发式指导。

Result: 仿真实验评估了两种推理机制并与专家FUELv2对比；在Flyability Elios 3上的概念验证实验展示了在未见过的室内环境中的部分探索，以及通过单独训练的策略实现目标导向的视觉伺服。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无全局地图的循环扩散策略能够实现部分探索行为，但性能与基于地图的专家存在差距；同时验证了该框架可扩展至真实平台并支持视觉伺服任务，为轻量级自主探索提供了新思路。

Abstract: Can exploratory UAV waypoint sequences be generated from multimodal onboard observations and a fixed-dimensional recurrent internal state without maintaining a persistent global map in the deployed policy? We investigate this question through CAVEAT, a diffusion policy conditioned on a recurrent internal state updated from fused LiDAR, visual, and pose features and trained from trajectories generated by the map-based FUELv2 expert. Rolling inference partially warm-starts consecutive predictions, while a temporary local signed distance field provides heuristic obstacle guidance. Simulation results evaluate both inference mechanisms and compare CAVEAT with its demonstration-generating expert. Proof-of-concept experiments on a Flyability Elios 3 demonstrate partial exploration of a previously unseen indoor environment and target-directed visual servoing using a separately trained policy.

</details>


### [119] [MemCorr-DP: Counterfactual Correspondence Conditioning for a Diffusion Policy Guided by a Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06615)
*Tan Su,Haoxiang Yang,Ruxin Wang,Binghui Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MemCorr-DP，利用显式3D参考关系作为条件，在物体位置和相机视角联合变化下显著提升扩散策略的闭环成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆的视觉运动策略在训练分布附近准确，但当物体位置与相机视角同时变化时会失效；参考轨迹包含可迁移的几何信息，但策略需要将该几何与当前场景对齐并在去噪中保持敏感。

Method: MemCorr-DP是一种扩散策略，将冻结的RoMa v2匹配提升为当前场景与参考轨迹之间的显式3D关系；使用反事实配对目标令相反行为共享相同物理状态与噪声动作，同时保留参考特定的去噪目标；并通过混合条件微调使策略从真实几何适应到实际对应误差。

Result: 在物体位置超出训练支持且相机视角改变±15°的最强联合偏移下，MemCorr-DP达到96.67%的闭环成功率，而具有相同动作架构的视觉Transformer仅为88.00%；消融与参考干预实验表明行为受所选参考影响，且完整关系集优于仅未来运动或质心几何。

Conclusion: 在空间与视角复合变化的任务中，显式3D参考关系是稳健的条件接口，为策略提供了可泛化的几何指导。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned visuomotor policies can remain accurate near their training distribution yet fail when object position and camera viewpoint change together. A successful reference trajectory contains the geometry needed to transfer the same interaction, but the policy must align that geometry with the current scene and remain sensitive to it during denoising. To address these challenges, we present MemCorr-DP, a diffusion policy that lifts frozen RoMa v2 matches into explicit 3D relations between the current scene and the reference trajectory. A counterfactual paired objective assigns opposite behaviors the same physical state and noisy action while retaining reference-specific denoising targets. Mixed-condition fine-tuning then adapts the policy from ground-truth geometry to measured correspondence errors. Our strongest evaluation places the Door in the outermost position bands beyond the training support and changes the query camera by $\pm15^\circ$. Under this combined shift, MemCorr-DP achieves 96.67% closed-loop success, compared with 88.00% for a visual Transformer with the same action architecture. Objective ablations and reference interventions show that behavior responds to the selected reference, while matched controls favor the complete relation set over future motion or centroid geometry alone. These results support explicit 3D reference relations as a robust conditioning interface when spatial and viewpoint changes are compounded in the evaluated task.

</details>


### [120] [Unifying Physics-Based Humanoid Interaction with a Context-Conditioned Interaction Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06591)
*Jianan Li,Xiao Chen,Tien-Tsin Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CHIP，一个统一的物理仿真框架，从异构动作数据中学习可复用的类人交互技能，通过条件交互先验和分层训练支持复杂场景导航、操作及组合行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人控制方法往往针对运动或操作单一任务专门设计，依赖特定奖励工程，难以在多样化行为间泛化，缺乏统一的可扩展框架。

Method: CHIP采用三阶段训练：首先从异构数据学习物理运动模仿策略，然后蒸馏为上下文条件下的交互先验（在共享离散空间中编码行为结构），最后从先验初始化下游策略并通过先验正则化的在线强化学习进行适应。

Result: 在多样化类人交互任务上验证，支持场景感知运动、接触丰富的物体操作及组合行为（如环境感知物体搬运和长时程技能序列），并生成平滑过渡和物理合理动作。

Conclusion: 提出的框架统一了类人运动与操作技能学习，有效利用异构数据并提升跨任务的可复用性与适应性，为通用类人交互控制提供可行方案。

Abstract: Developing unified physics-based humanoid controllers that can navigate complex 3D scenes and manipulate objects remains a longstanding challenge. Existing approaches are often specialized for either locomotion or object-centric manipulation, or rely on task-specific reward engineering that does not scale well across diverse behaviors. We present CHIP, a unified, physics-grounded framework for learning reusable humanoid interaction skills from heterogeneous motion data. Central to our approach is a conditional interaction prior that models a context-dependent distribution over these skills within a shared discrete space. Our method is trained in three stages. We first learn physics-based motion-imitation policies that acquire grounded teacher behaviors from heterogeneous interaction data. We then distill these behaviors into a context-conditioned interaction prior that captures reusable motion structure across locomotion and manipulation. Finally, we initialize downstream task policies from the pretrained prior and adapt them through prior-regularized online RL post-training. Experiments on a diverse suite of humanoid interaction tasks show that our approach supports scene-aware locomotion, contact-rich object manipulation, and compositional behaviors such as environment-aware object transport and long-horizon skill sequencing, while producing smooth transitions and physically plausible motion.

</details>


### [121] [SHIFT: Surface-aware High-speed Integration For TSDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06547)
*Ayaan Choudhury,Lokender Tiwari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SHIFT，一种利用深度几何结构冗余的高效TSDF建图框架，通过压缩平面区域与冻结体素梯度，显著降低每帧更新成本与ESDF内存占用，同时保持建图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非投影距离场建图精度高但计算开销大，而传统TSDF集成方式每帧重复融合数百万深度像素，即使体素已收敛仍浪费计算资源，尤其在平面主导的环境中。

Method: SHIFT从3D深度几何中直接挖掘结构冗余，将平坦局部区域压缩为加权超射线，并冻结平面体素的梯度更新；同时采用紧凑的ESDF体素布局以减少波前内存储占用。

Result: 在多种RGB-D与LiDAR序列上，SHIFT将TSDF计算成本降低1.42至4.07倍，网格误差保持在毫米级，ESDF内存占用减少最高28%。

Conclusion: SHIFT在保证高精度建图的同时，显著提升TSDF集成效率并降低ESDF内存，适用于实时自主导航中的在线3D建图。

Abstract: Real-time 3D mapping is fundamental for autonomous robotic navigation, with Euclidean Signed Distance Fields (ESDFs) serving as the standard representation for online motion planning. While recent advancements in non- projective distance fields yield highly accurate maps, their computational overhead remains a severe bottleneck. Conventional integrators redundantly re-fuse millions of depth pixels every frame, even long after the corresponding voxels have converged, wasting significant computational resources in environments dominated by large planar surfaces. In this paper, we present SHIFT (Surface-aware High-speed Integration For TSDFs), an efficient mapping framework designed to reduce this per-frame update cost. By exploiting structural redundancy directly from 3D depth geometry, SHIFT compresses flat local regions into weighted super-rays and freezes flat-voxel gradients. A compact ESDF voxel layout further reduces the memory footprint of the remaining wavefront. Extensive evaluations across various RGB-D and LiDAR sequences show that SHIFT cuts TSDF cost by 1.42 to 4.07 times, while holding mesh error within millimeters, and reduces ESDF-layer memory by up to 28%

</details>


### [122] [Knowledge-Guided Hierarchical Policy Learning for High-Precision Cylindrical Assembly under Tight Tolerances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06522)
*Binbin Lian,Xinyu Liu,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合分层学习框架，用于170mm圆柱体组件的高精度装配（公差0.1mm），结合行为克隆与TD3算法，并通过上层启发式规则动态调整，实现高效、稳健的装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高精度（0.1mm公差）的圆柱体组件装配，并解决传统强化学习在真实世界中训练效率低、安全性差的问题，同时提升对初始条件和位姿误差的适应性。

Method: 采用双层混合框架：下层网络通过行为克隆整合专家经验，赋予机器人类人直觉，并引入TD3算法增强训练稳定性和鲁棒性；上层网络基于启发式规则动态调整下层决策。先在仿真模型中学习，再迁移到真实世界，确保安全高效训练。

Result: 奖励曲线在500个回合内收敛，学习效率高；对初始条件和位姿误差适应性强，极端条件下仍有满意成功率；在高斯噪声干扰下表现稳定。真实装配轨迹更平滑、波动更小。

Conclusion: 该混合分层学习方法能有效实现高精度圆柱体装配，兼具学习效率、鲁棒性和真实世界可迁移性，适用于高精度工业装配任务。

Abstract: A hybrid hierarchical learning framework is proposed to achieve high-precision assembly of 170mm cylindrical components with tolerance of 0.1mm. The lower-level network integrates expert experience through Behavior Cloning (BC), giving the robot human-like intuition, and incorporates the Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) algorithm to enhance training stability and robustness. The upper-level network dynamically adjusts the lower-level decisions based on heuristic rules, ensuring flexibility in operations. A simulated model is constructed to learn before transferring to real world. An efficient and safe training is allowed. Comparisons show that the reward curve converges within 500 episodes, indicating high learning efficiency. It also demonstrates better adaptability to initial conditions and pose errors, achieving satisfactory success rates even under extreme conditions. Moreover, the method exhibits good stability under Gaussian noise interference. In the real world, the assembly trajectory of the cylindrical segment shows smoother motion and less fluctuation.

</details>


### [123] [VLA-Corrector: Stage-Aware Observable State Understanding for Prompt-Based Closed-Loop Recovery of Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06508)
*Chang Song,Bin Qian,Yan Feng,Zhijie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种阶段感知的失败验证与提示恢复框架，无需更新参数或特权状态，即可对冻结的VLA策略进行闭环纠错。通过学习验证器联合估计操作进度和风险，生成阶段条件恢复提示，在LIBERO基准上显著提升闭环可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人操作中的VLA策略在运行时容易因动作噪声、物体位移或目标错位产生偏差，而最终任务成功与否无法提供足够信息来诊断和纠正这些失败，导致缺乏有效的闭环修正机制。

Method: 引入基于可观察历史的Learned Verifier，通过时间建模多视角视觉、本体感觉和已执行动作，联合估计操作进度和风险；将操作过程表示为语义进度阶段（接近、对齐、抓取、搬运、放置），并识别各阶段失败模式；检测到异常时，保留原始指令并生成阶段条件恢复提示，驱动同一个冻结VLA策略生成修正动作。

Result: 在LIBERO和LIBERO Plus上进行多轮评估，所提方法在多种扰动下显著提升闭环可靠性；在不使用特权物体/目标坐标的情况下，Learned Verifier的性能接近特权规则验证器。

Conclusion: 可观察的视觉-本体-动作历史足以推断潜在任务状态，为现有VLA策略提供实用的失败恢复能力，而无需特权信息或策略参数更新。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation with Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies remains vulnerable to execution-time deviations, as final task success provides little information for diagnosing and correcting failures caused by action noise, object displacement, or goal misalignment. We introduce a stage-aware failure verification and Prompt Recovery framework that enables closed-loop correction of a fixed VLA policy without parameter updates or privileged simulator states. The framework introduces an observable-history-based Learned Verifier that jointly estimates manipulation progress and execution risk by temporally modeling multi-view visual observations, proprioceptive states, and executed actions. To provide interpretable task understanding, we represent manipulation execution through semantic progress stages, including approach, alignment, grasp, transport, and placement, and identify stage-specific failure patterns. Upon detecting abnormal execution, the framework preserves the original instruction and generates a stage-conditioned recovery prompt, allowing the same frozen VLA policy to produce corrective actions. Extensive multi-round evaluations on LIBERO and LIBERO Plus demonstrate that the proposed approach substantially improves closed-loop reliability under diverse perturbations. Without access to privileged object or goal coordinates, the Learned Verifier achieves recovery performance close to that of the privileged rule-based verifier in the evaluated settings. These results show that observable visual-proprioceptive-action history is sufficient to infer latent task states and enable practical failure recovery for existing VLA policies.

</details>


### [124] [\textbf{PLATO}: \emph{Preintegration Learning from Accurate Trajectory Observations} for Neural Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06450)
*Haoying Li,Qihang Liu,Yifan Peng,Keyan Miao,Junfeng Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PLATO是一个基于似然的框架，利用精确轨迹观测联合学习IMU零偏动态（由神经常微分方程建模）和噪声协方差，通过稀疏优化与双伴随方案实现内存高效的惯性预积分学习，在EuRoC和水下机器人实验中验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯惯性预积分对IMU零偏和不确定性敏感，现有方法未能有效联合学习IMU零偏动态与噪声协方差，导致运动估计在挑战性环境下准确性受限。

Method: 提出PLATO框架，使用神经ODE建模IMU零偏动态，通过最大化似然函数联合优化零偏动态参数和陀螺仪/加速度计噪声协方差；利用负对数似然的稀疏结构，采用前向微分计算噪声参数梯度，并提出双伴随方案（离散不变误差伴随+连续时间伴随）耦合零偏动态和预积分展开，实现内存高效的优化。

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上验证了改进的性能，并在水下机器人实验中展示了在间歇性光照失败和视觉退化条件下的适用性。

Conclusion: PLATO通过数据驱动的零偏动态学习与噪声协方差估计，显著增强了惯性预积分的鲁棒性，适用于视觉退化等挑战性环境。

Abstract: Neural inertial odometry has demonstrated strong potential for motion estimation in challenging environments, yet inertial-only preintegration remains sensitive to IMU bias and uncertainty. To this end, this paper introduces \textbf{PLATO}:~\emph{Preintegration Learning from Accurate Trajectory Observations}, a likelihood-based framework that leverages accurate trajectory observations to jointly learn IMU bias dynamics modeled by a neural ordinary differential equation~(NODE) and gyroscope and accelerometer noise covariances. Optimization exploits the sparse structure of the negative log-likelihood, with IMU noise-parameter gradients computed by forward differentiation. A tailored double-adjoint scheme couples a discrete invariant-error adjoint with a continuous-time adjoint for the bias NODE, enabling memory-efficient likelihood optimization over the nested bias-dynamics and IMU-preintegration rollouts. Validation on EuRoC shows improved performance, and underwater robot experiments demonstrate applicability under intermittent lighting failures and visual degradation.

</details>


### [125] [Collision Snapshot Guided Time-Reversed Safety-Critical Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06433)
*Taehyung Kim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出COSTER框架，利用学习到的交通先验确定碰撞时间与地点，通过碰撞快照和时间反转生成安全关键场景，在合理性、多样性和数据效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键场景生成方法多通过简化对抗目标扰动已有智能体轨迹，限制场景合理性与多样性；插入新对抗车辆虽可缓解，但如何按场景确定插入时间和位置仍是挑战。

Method: COSTER首先在识别出的碰撞状态下插入与目标车辆接触的新车辆构建碰撞快照，然后利用条件变分自编码器从该快照执行时间反转推演，向后重构插入车辆在更早时间步的轨迹。

Result: COSTER在合理性、多样性和数据效率上优于现有方法；在Waymo开放运动数据集的安全关键场景中，用COSTER生成场景训练的智能体碰撞率降低31%，并提高了自车任务完成度。

Conclusion: COSTER通过引入交通先验和碰撞快照时间反转，实现了更合理、多样且高效的安全关键场景生成，可有效改善自动驾驶智能体的安全性能。

Abstract: The generation of safety-critical traffic scenarios is essential for training and evaluating autonomous vehicles. Prior approaches typically perturb the trajectories of existing agents in a traffic scenario using simplified adversarial objectives to induce safety-critical interactions, which can limit the plausibility and diversity of the generated scenarios. Although inserting new adversarial vehicles can alleviate this limitation, determining when and where to introduce them in a scenario-specific manner remains challenging. In this work, we introduce \underline{CO}llision \underline{S}napshot guided \underline{T}im\underline{E}-\underline{R}eversed safety-critical scenario generation (COSTER), a framework that leverages learned traffic priors to determine plausible collision times and locations. COSTER first constructs a collision snapshot by inserting a new vehicle in contact with the target vehicle at the identified collision state within a traffic scenario. Starting from this collision snapshot, a conditional variational autoencoder is used to perform a time-reversed rollout, reconstructing the trajectory of the inserted vehicle backward toward earlier timesteps. Experiments show that COSTER outperforms existing methods in plausibility, diversity, and data efficiency. Moreover, agents trained on COSTER-generated scenarios reduce collision rates by 31\% on safety-critical scenarios from the Waymo Open Motion Dataset while also improving ego task completion. The project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [126] [OVMAN: A Task and Benchmark for Open-Vocabulary Motion-Aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06424)
*Dibyendu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OVMAN任务，让智能体在两次访问之间应对场景变化并导航至由变化指定的目标（如物体被移动或曾经存在的位置）。发布了219个可解的两遍访问片段，并评估多个基线，结果显示即使简单双访问参考智能体也仅在45.2%的片段中成功，而神谕智能体可达99.5%，主要瓶颈在于开放词汇的实例接地而非几何或答案选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航基准仅在当前世界设定目标，而双访问基准要么评估重排要么评估回忆，无法表达“去被移动的椅子那里”或“去花瓶曾经所在的地方”等由变化定义的目标。

Method: 定义OVMAN任务：智能体巡视场景，经历脚本化变化后返回，并根据六种变化关系中的某种来导航至目标；其中两种关系需要到达物体离开后无剩余检测物的位置。发布219个两遍访问片段，均由神谕智能体三次验证可解；然后评估零样本目标导航器、开放词汇地图的过去时查询以及简单的两遍参考智能体，并进行错误分解。

Result: 零样本目标导航器在变化定义的目标上仅11.4%成功率，且在空置位置几乎失败（前位置0.000，移除0.025）；开放词汇地图对过去时查询的响应率约为其两遍读取方式的三分之一；简单的两遍参考智能体在导航上达到45.2%，而具身神谕智能体为99.5%。错误分解表明剩余困难主要来自开放词汇的实例接地，而非几何或已知位置后的答案选择。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了双访问变化目标导航中开放词汇实例接地是主要挑战，为后续研究提供了明确方向和可复现的评估基准。

Abstract: Homes change between a robot's visits. Navigation benchmarks pose their goals in the world the agent currently sees, and the two-visit benchmarks that exist score recall or rearrangement rather than navigation. None of them can express go to the chair that was moved or go to where the vase used to be. OVMAN is a task in which an agent tours a scene, returns after a scripted change, and must navigate to a goal specified by the change itself. Two of its six change relations answer with a place an object has left, where nothing remains to be detected. We release 219 two-visit episodes, each certified solvable by an oracle agent that completes it three times. Two released systems fail as predicted. A zero-shot object-goal navigator reaches the change-defined target in 11.4% of episodes and essentially fails at vacated locations, 0.000 on former and 0.025 on removed. A self-maintaining open-vocabulary map answers past-tense queries at about a third of the rate of the same maps read as two visits, even when grounding is supplied. A simple two-visit reference agent reaches 45.2% when navigating, against an embodied oracle of 99.5%. An error decomposition places the remaining difficulty in open-vocabulary instance grounding rather than in geometry or in selecting the answer once positions are known.

</details>


### [127] [LANTERN: A Closed-Loop Benchmark for VLM-Based Cooperative Driving with Temporally Grounded Warnings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06368)
*Yongshuo Liu,Xu Gao,Morui Zhu,Yongqi Zhu,Qi Chen,Deyuan Qu,Song Fu,Qing Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LANTERN，一个用于时间上接地协作性警告的闭环基准，通过匹配警告与无警告执行来隔离测量警告贡献，并引入安全门控指标CUS；微调VLM模型使CUS从34.6升至75.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将协作性警告的贡献与车载视觉混淆，无法在隔离条件下衡量警告的实际价值，因此需要一种能分离警告时间事件并配对执行的基准。

Method: LANTERN基准将警告起始、危险起始、警告终止和事后恢复分离，在匹配的警告/无警告执行下评估每个物理事件；涵盖6类安全场景，提供3272个训练序列（236309帧）和120对匹配路线用于闭环评估，并设置无危险控制以惩罚无条件刹车；提出Cooperative Unified Score（CUS），一种安全门控指标，联合奖励路线进展、预期、清障和恢复。

Result: 微调代表性VLM驾驶模型后，CUS从无警告时的34.6提升到有警告时的75.5，证明协作性警告的有效性及配对协议的分辨力。

Conclusion: LANTERN基准和CUS指标能有效评估协作性警告的独立贡献，资源将公开，有助于推动安全驾驶中警告系统的研究。

Abstract: We present LANTERN, a closed-loop benchmark for temporally grounded cooperative warnings. LANTERN separates warning onset, hazard onset, warning termination, and post-hazard recovery, and evaluates each physical event under matched warning and no-warning executions so that the warning's contribution is measured in isolation rather than confounded with onboard vision. The benchmark spans six safety-critical scenario families and provides 3,272 sequences with 236,309 frames for training, together with 120 matched route pairs for closed-loop evaluation. Each hazard route is evaluated under the warning and no-warning conditions, while its no-hazard control penalizes unconditional braking. We further introduce the Cooperative Unified Score (CUS), a safety-gated metric that jointly rewards route progress, anticipation, clearance, and recovery. Fine-tuning a representative VLM driving model raises CUS from 34.6 without warnings to 75.5 with them, demonstrating both the value of cooperative warnings and the discriminative power of the paired protocol. All resources will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [128] [OcclusionCBF: Backup Control Barrier Functions for Safe Navigation Among Hidden Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06342)
*Taekyung Kim,Hun Kuk Park,Renya Wada,Nikolay Atanasov,Shumon Koga,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OcclusionCBF安全滤波器，将备份控制屏障函数扩展至遮挡区域的可达占据预测，为隐藏在遮挡物后的动态障碍提供碰撞避免保证，并在仿真与硬件上验证其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在遮挡环境下导航时，可能进入某个状态，使得一旦动态障碍物突然可见，任何允许的控制输入都无法避免碰撞。现有反应式或遮挡感知预测方法缺乏安全性保证，需要一种能显式考虑潜在隐藏障碍物运动的安全滤波器。

Method: OcclusionCBF扩展了备份控制屏障函数（backup CBF），使用可达占据预测（reachable-occupancy）覆盖可能的隐藏动态障碍运动。通过将标称备份滚动与碰撞膨胀占据区域相交进行认证，并验证终端集合，产生仿射约束用于最小侵入的二次规划滤波。建立了递归可行性和对覆盖运动集合的碰撞避免保证。

Result: 在随机基准测试、MetaUrban仿真和硬件实验中，相比反应式和遮挡感知预测基线，任务成功率更高，且计算时间处于毫秒级。

Conclusion: OcclusionCBF能在遮挡环境下提供实时、可验证的安全滤波，有效提升任务成功率，是处理部分可观测动态障碍的一种可靠方案。

Abstract: Robots navigating under occlusion may enter states from which no admissible input can avoid a dynamic obstacle once it becomes visible. We present OcclusionCBF, a safety filter that extends backup control barrier functions to reachable-occupancy predictions for potentially hidden dynamic obstacles in occluded regions. The method certifies a prescribed backup rollout against collision-inflated occupancy and a verified terminal set, yielding affine constraints for minimally invasive quadratic-program filtering. We establish recursive feasibility of the resulting safety filter, and collision avoidance for every hidden-obstacle motion covered by the occupancy prediction. Randomized benchmarks, MetaUrban simulations, and hardware experiments demonstrate improved task success over reactive and occlusion-aware predictive baselines with millisecond-scale computation.

</details>


### [129] [Geometric Distributional Control: Learning Progress with Partial Structural Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06331)
*Tong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出几何分布控制（GDC），在部分已知模型与完全未知目标的中间场景中，将控制分解为可行性支架与学习进度信号，实现高效且保持可行性的实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时控制常处于预测优化与强化学习之间：前者需完整模型与目标，后者需长时程价值信号且训练困难。实际系统（如自动驾驶、仓储机器人、交通控制）仅部分已知几何/物理/规则，但任务进度方向未知，缺乏针对该中间状态的控制器设计。

Method: GDC将控制分解为可行性（已知几何、规则、约束构成的可执行支架）与进度（学习缺失的方向信号）。离线阶段利用短已知可行片段与弱符号进度证书拟合进度倾斜分布；在线阶段将学到的分数投影到支架上，以滚动时域方式选择动作。分数类似Bellman局部值梯度，不需全局递归或完整规划器。

Result: 证明了该分数沿数据诱导的软进度值下降。在结构化多层优化和SUMO路线进度驾驶任务中，相比仅已知求解器和学习基线，GDC效果更优，同时保持支架强制的可行性。

Conclusion: GDC为部分知识场景提供了有效且可行的控制方案，仅需轻量局部知识即可学习进度信号，兼顾模型驱动与学习的优势。

Abstract: Real-time control often sits between two limiting regimes. Predictive optimization and model-based control are powerful when dynamics, parameters, objectives, and online planning models are specified; reinforcement learning can relax this requirement, but must infer long-horizon value signals from sequential data and interaction, making training slow, high-variance, and hard to scale in large action spaces. This middle regime is common in systems including autonomous driving, warehouse robotics, traffic control, and delivery drones: partial geometry, physics, rules, or constraints are known, yet the local direction of task progress remains uncertain. Geometric Distributional Control (GDC) is designed for this partial-knowledge setting. It factorizes control into feasibility and progress: known geometry, rules, constraints, and response maps define an executable scaffold, while progress-weighted feasible data learns the missing directional signal on that scaffold. The learned score acts as a Bellman-like local value-gradient, selecting actions that make progress without requiring global Bellman recursion, a fully specified planner, or a black-box policy that absorbs both feasibility and preference. This knowledge can be lightweight and partial, such as simple dynamics, safety filters, local maps, constraint projectors, or lower-level response maps; it need not encode full dynamics or a long-horizon objective. Offline, GDC fits a progress-tilted distribution from short known-feasible snippets with weak signed progress certificates. Online, its score is projected through the scaffold and applied in receding-horizon feedback. We prove that this score descends a data-induced soft progress value and validate GDC on structured multilevel optimization and SUMO route-progress driving, where it improves over known-only solvers and learning baselines while preserving scaffold-enforced feasibility.

</details>


### [130] [IM-ENGINE: Image Editing for Embodied Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06279)
*Yian Wang,Junyi Cao,Xiaowen Qiu,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IM-ENGINE，一种以图像编辑为中间表示、结合生成语义先验与物理仿真器grounding的机器人数据生成管线，可生成兼具语义有效性和物理可执行性的操作监督，用于灵巧抓取与目标状态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作学习需要既有语义意义又物理可执行的监督，但现有数据来源难以兼得：人类演示具有意图但成本高且存在人机关节差异；仿真数据可扩展但常缺少功能性行为规范。

Method: IM-ENGINE利用渲染场景的已知几何、深度、分割和相机参数，通过图像编辑注入任务相关语义；借助仿真器先验和不变锚点物体恢复显式3D状态；在物理引擎中细化状态并转换为机器人可执行监督。在抓取中，生成图像空间的人类抓取，恢复手-物交互，重定向到机器人手并细化为物理验证抓取；在目标生成中，编辑场景图像为目标结果，恢复物体位姿，细化成物理有效且有语义的目标与轨迹。

Result: 该方法结合生成语义先验与仿真器grounding，能够产生可扩展的任务相关监督，用于灵巧抓取合成和目标状态生成，兼具语义相关性与物理有效性。

Conclusion: IM-ENGINE证明了以图像编辑为中间表示的仿真器grounded数据生成管线是一种有效途径，可弥合人类演示与仿真数据之间的鸿沟，为机器人学习提供大规模且语义物理兼具的监督信号。

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation requires supervision that is both semantically meaningful and physically executable, but current data pipelines often provide only one of these properties. Human demonstrations capture intent but are costly to collect and constrained by the human-robot embodiment gap, while simulation can scale data generation but often under-specifies functional behavior. We present IM-ENGINE, a simulator-grounded pipeline that uses image editing as an intermediate representation for embodied data generation. Given a rendered scene with known geometry, depth, segmentation, and camera parameters, IM-ENGINE edits the image to inject task-relevant semantics, recovers explicit 3D state using simulator priors and an unchanged anchor object, refines the state in physics, and converts it into robot-executable supervision. We instantiate the pipeline for dexterous grasp synthesis and goal-state generation. For grasping, IM-ENGINE generates a human grasp in image space, recovers the hand-object interaction, retargets it to a robot hand, and refines it into physically validated robot grasps. For goal generation, it edits a rendered scene into a desired outcome, recovers the target-object pose, and refines it into physically valid, semantically meaningful goals and trajectories. This combination of generative semantic priors and simulator grounding enables scalable task-relevant supervision for robot learning.

</details>


### [131] [Bundle Length Tradeoffs in Decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation Under Degraded Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06273)
*James Lott,Vahraz Honary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统研究了束长度B对多任务多机器人任务分配中MinSum与MinMax目标权衡的影响，并检验了理想通信参数在消息丢失下是否仍然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 束长度B在MRTA算法配置中通常被固定设置，但B对MinSum与MinMax之间的权衡影响未被系统刻画；同时，退化通信评估常沿用理想通信下的设置，尚不清楚这些标称调优在消息丢失下是否仍然适用。

Method: 针对ACBBA、PI和HIPC三种分配器，在300个配对的十目标协作访问场景中比较6种束长度，分别评估理想通信和25%伯努利丢包两种情况；并使用重复配对交叉拟合检验理想通信设置在丢包条件下的泛化性能。

Result: 理想通信下，增加B从1到12使MinSum成本降低19.0%（ACBBA）、23.0%（PI）和31.8%（HIPC），但MinMax成本上升45.6%、94.3%和67.6%；丢包下，ACBBA和PI的最低平均MinSum对应束长从B=12移至B=2，沿用理想通信设置导致MinSum惩罚14.4%和7.2%，MinMax成本增加30.0%和41.8%。HIPC保持较深的MinSum操作区域，三种分配器的MinMax设置在丢包下均稳定。

Conclusion: 束长度调优必须考虑通信条件：按理想通信选择的B在丢包下会导致MinSum明显恶化，尤其对ACBBA和PI；而HIPC和MinMax目标相对鲁棒，在退化通信下仍可保持良好表现。

Abstract: Bundle length B is commonly fixed when configuring multi-task multi-robot task allocation (MRTA) algorithms. MinSum and MinMax are known to favor different task distributions, but the role of B in this objective tradeoff has not been systematically characterized. Additionally, degraded-communication evaluations also often retain settings selected under ideal communication, leaving whether nominal bundle-length tuning transfers under message loss unresolved. We examine both questions for ACBBA, PI, and HIPC across six bundle lengths in 300 paired ten-target Collaborative Visit scenarios under ideal communication and 25% Bernoulli packet loss. Under ideal communication, increasing B from 1 to 12 reduces MinSum cost by 19.0%, 23.0%, and 31.8% for ACBBA, PI, and HIPC, respectively, while increasing MinMax cost by 45.6%, 94.3%, and 67.6%. Under packet loss, the lowest-mean MinSum setting shifts from B = 12 to B = 2 for ACBBA and PI. Repeated paired cross-fitting shows that retaining the ideal-network setting incurs held-out MinSum penalties of 14.4% and 7.2%, respectively, and increases MinMax cost by 30.0% and 41.8% relative to the loss-conditioned MinSum setting. HIPC retains a deep MinSum operating region, while the MinMax setting remains stable for all three allocators. Experiments at two additional target loads reproduce the ACBBA and PI MinSum shifts.

</details>


### [132] [GloVLA: Let Geometry Move and Local VLA Interact for Robust Object-Centric Manipulation in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06256)
*Truong Thanh Nguyen,Huy Hoang Nguyen,Ha Anh Nguyen,Binh Khanh Dinh,Ngo Anh Vien,Duy Nguyen Ho Minh,Minh Nhat Vu,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GloVLA 混合框架，将操作任务显式分解为几何传输控制与局部 VLA 策略，提升泛化性并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端 VLA 策略需同时处理长距离传输和短程接触丰富交互，导致效率低且对视觉变化、遮挡、干扰等敏感，易因状态分布偏移而失败。

Method: GloVLA 将目标中心操作分为两个互补阶段：几何传输控制器将末端执行器移动到交互中心交接区域，局部 VLA 策略仅处理短时交互。该框架模型无关，可集成不同 VLA 骨干，无需额外演示、不改变动作空间或成功谓词。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus Object 及新 LIBERO-Challenge 基准上提升成功率并大幅降低推理成本；全轨迹 GR00T N1.6 平均成功率降至 20.9%，GloVLA 保持 88.5%；物理 UR10e 上成功率从 35.6% 提升至 90.0%，平均推理时间减少一半以上。

Conclusion: GloVLA 通过解耦几何传输与局部交互，增强了 VLA 在非结构化环境中的鲁棒性与效率，验证了混合范式的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown promising generalization for language-conditioned robot manipulation, but deploying them in unstructured environments remains challenging. A single end-to-end VLA policy must simultaneously solve long-range transport of the end effector to task-relevant regions and short-horizon, contact-rich interaction upon arrival. This formulation is inefficient and brittle: small visual shifts, distractors, clutter, occlusions, or unfavorable initial gripper poses can push the policy outside the local state distribution in which it was trained, leading to task failure. We introduce GloVLA, a hybrid framework that explicitly separates object-centric manipulation into two complementary regimes: a geometric transport controller moves the end-effector into interaction-centric handoff regions, and local VLA policies handle only the short-horizon interaction phases. GloVLA is model-agnostic and can be integrated with different VLA backbones with no additional demonstrations and no changes to the action space or success predicate. Experiments on standard LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus Object tasks together with a newly introduced LIBERO-Challenge benchmark ettings with clutter, distractors,illumination changes, visual shifts, and obstruction show that GloVLA improves task success and substantially lowers VLA inference cost compared with full end-to-Challenge, full-trajectory GR00T N1.6execution degrades to 20.9% average success while GloVLA retains 88.5%; on a physical UR10e, overall success improves from 35.6% to 90.0% while mean inference time is more than halved. Videos and additional results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [133] [RefGuard: Identity-Aware Language-Guided Robot Manipulation via Joint Target-Anchor-Frame Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06221)
*Lan Wei,Kangyi Lu,Yongchen Wang,Chenmeng Bi,Qi Chen,Hanlin Niu,Yip Fun Yeung,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RefGuard，一种身份感知的接地框架，通过延迟对目标、锚点和参考帧的联合后验提交，解决机器人操作中因物体身份混淆导致的指令执行错误，在真实机械臂和程序化测试中显著降低身份切换率并提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在语言引导的机器人操作中依赖正确识别指令所指的物理对象。在杂乱场景中，重复物体、模糊锚点或依赖于帧的空间术语可能导致机器人在语义兼容但非预期的实例上执行几何上有效的动作，称为身份切换（identity switch）。现有方法在操作前过早承诺某一个隐变量，将残余歧义转化为不可逆的静默错误。

Method: RefGuard构建帧条件对象中心场景图（frame-conditioned object-centric scene graph），从RGB-D观测中分离帧无关几何和方向关系，维持目标、锚点、参考帧三者的联合后验分布，并通过决策策略选择执行、澄清、重新观察或中止，从而延迟承诺并处理歧义。

Result: 在真实UF850机械臂上，RefGuard在歧义压力试验中无身份切换，正确执行率90.0%，而微调VLA和LLM基线在相同试验中身份切换率达33–46%；在无歧义场景中保持93.3%成功率，在接地后场景变化中86.7%可恢复。在3200集程序化套件中，对于可解指令，正确执行率从56.6%提升至80.5%（相比先承诺锚点和帧的消融），同时推迟频率更低（19.5%对43.4%）。

Conclusion: RefGuard通过联合推理和显式管理接地不确定性，能够有效避免身份切换，兼顾歧义场景下的稳健性和清晰场景下的性能，且能适应动态变化，为VLA语言接地提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have substantially advanced language-guided robot manipulation, yet reliable execution still hinges on identifying which physical object an instruction refers to. In cluttered scenes containing repeated objects, ambiguous anchors, or frame-dependent spatial terms, a robot can execute a geometrically valid action on a semantically compatible but unintended instance; we call this failure an identity switch. The referent is jointly determined by three coupled latent variables: the target, the anchor, and the reference frame, so committing to any one of them before execution turns residual ambiguity into a silent and irreversible error. We propose RefGuard, an identity-aware grounding framework that delays commitment by maintaining a joint posterior over all three variables. RefGuard builds a frame-conditioned object-centric scene graph from RGB-D observations, separating frame-independent geometry from directional relations, and routes the posterior through a decision policy that executes, clarifies, reobserves, or aborts. On a real UF850 arm, RefGuard records no identity switch on any ambiguity-stress trial and executes correctly on 90.0% of them, whereas fine-tuned VLA and LLM (Large Language Model)-based baselines switch identity in 33-46% of the same trials, while retaining 93.3% success on unambiguous scenes and recovering from post-grounding scene changes in 86.7% of trials. On a 3200-episode procedural suite, it raises correct execution on solvable instructions from 56.6% to 80.5% over the ablation that commits to the anchor and frame before the target, while deferring less often (19.5% vs. 43.4%).

</details>


### [134] [Where Success Breaks: Failure-Boundary Learning for Robust Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06114)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zhijun Cao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将鲁棒视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略微调视为失败边界学习（Failure-Boundary Learning），并给出DLS方法：通过真实演示的少样本行为先验与仿真协同训练，在数字孪生滚动中大规模发现失败边界，利用语义进度定位生成进度感知信号，并以方向性边界塑形调整扩散去噪方向，从而提升真实机器人操作的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型经监督微调后存在结构性不对称：专家演示只表明成功行为所在区域，却无法提供策略失效边界的信息；因此鲁棒VLA适应不应仅是拟合演示，而应学习失败边界，即发现、定位并塑造“可恢复偏差”与“任务失败”之间的分界。

Method: DLS包含三个关键组件：1）使用少量真实演示构建“真实接地行为先验”，并加入仿真协同训练；2）通过在线策略数字孪生（digital twin）回放大规模发现失败边界，并利用特权模拟器状态进行“语义进度定位”，为每个回放分配进度感知信号，以捕捉边界被越过的位置；3）基于这些信号进行“方向性边界塑形”，在流（flow）动力学中增强产生成功的去噪方向、抑制导致失败的方向，避免使用动作似然或辅助评论器。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务中，DLS相比监督微调和在线强化学习基线，在随机初始状态和未见视觉条件下显著提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DLS验证了失败边界学习范式在VLA微调中的有效性，表明发现并塑形失败边界能够提升策略在分布偏移下的可靠性，为鲁棒VLA适应提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models adapted through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) inherit a structural asymmetry: expert demonstrations teach the policy where success behavior lies, but provide no signal about where it ceases to be reliable. We argue that robust VLA adaptation should therefore be viewed not as further demonstration fitting, but as **Failure-Boundary Learning**---the problem of *Discovering*, *Localizing*, and *Shaping* the boundary between recoverable deviations and task failure. To instantiate this view, we propose **DLS**: built on a **real-grounded behavioral prior** from few real demonstrations and simulated co-training, DLS *discovers* failure boundaries at scale through on-policy digital twin rollouts. Rather than reducing each rollout to a binary label, **semantic progress localization** uses privileged simulator states to assign progress-aware signals that capture *where* the failure boundary is crossed, not merely *whether*. These signals drive **directional boundary shaping** in the flow dynamics---reinforcing success-producing denoising directions and suppressing failure-producing ones, without action likelihoods or auxiliary critics. Across real-robot manipulation tasks, DLS improves robustness over SFT and online RL baselines, especially under randomized initial states and unseen visual conditions.

</details>


### [135] [SCoCaT: Success Conditioned Constrained Reinforcement Learning for Spacecraft Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06061)
*Aman Arora,Ricard Marsal I Castan,Matteo El-Hariry,Miguel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于终止的约束强化学习在终端导航任务中可能因安全约束激活而出现“可行性崩溃”（任务完成率低但约束满足）。通过给PPO等算法增加辅助价值批评者以提供稠密成功信号，可显著提升任务完成率而不牺牲安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方法虽比拉格朗日方法简单，但在目标靠近安全约束边界时，生存加权目标让智能体认为停留在目标区域外更有利，导致在安全关键部署中无法完成任务；需要理解并消除该失效模式。

Method: 形式化了可行性崩溃的机制；对标准RL算法（如PPO）做最小修改，额外引入一个辅助价值批评者，用于估计并注入每步的稠密成功信号，以抵消缩短有效时域带来的“停驻”诱因，使进入目标区域变得有利。

Result: 在6U-CubeSat（代表近距操作的质量与自由度范围）和实验室浮动平台（模拟零样本真机迁移）上验证，该方法在保持安全约束合规的同时大幅提高任务完成率，证明其具有跨平台通用性。

Conclusion: 可行性崩溃源于目标与安全域边界重叠时优化目标的短视；辅助价值批评者可有效恢复任务驱动的探索，是一种实用、即插即用的改进。未来的安全约束RL应当既考虑累积安全，也保留对任务完成目标的持续稠密监督。

Abstract: Termination-based constrained reinforcement learning is attractive for safety-critical robotic deployments: it avoids online optimization at inference, scales easily to many constraints via a single scalar per constraint, and is simpler to implement than commonly used Lagrangian methods. Instead of pricing violations through summed cost penalties, this approach makes violations structurally unprofitable by shortening the effective horizon for each violation. We identify a structural failure mode of this method class on terminal-navigation tasks: reaching a precise goal configuration while satisfying safety constraints that tighten along the final approach. When the goal sits inside the region close to where the constraints become active, the survival-weighted objective makes dwelling outside the goal region strictly preferable to entering, producing high constraint compliance with low task completion. We formalize this pathology and show that a minimal augmentation to off-the-shelf RL algorithms like PPO resolves this ``feasibility collapse''. We empirically demonstrate that adding a dense per-step success signal via an auxiliary value critic improves the task completion rate while maintaining safety-critical constraint compliance. Validation across two representative spacecraft platforms: a 6U-CubeSat spanning the mass and degree-of-freedom envelope of operational proximity operations, and a floating platform testbed for zero-shot sim-to-real transfer in our laboratory, supports the generality of these findings.

</details>


### [136] [FALCON-S: Fixed-wing ground-effect Aerodynamics Simulator and Flight Control Learning Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06046)
*Matteo El Hariry,Pedro Lima,Andrej Orsula,Matthieu Geist,Miguel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于固定翼飞行器近地飞行的模块化高保真仿真框架，支持6DoF物理、地面效应、执行器与传感器噪声，并兼容CPU/GPU并行，用于强化学习与最优控制的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真器多采用简化动力学或偏重悬停模型，缺乏对固定翼飞行器近地飞行时复杂气动效应（如地面效应）和真实物理的建模，难以支撑低空飞行控制策略的开发与基准测试。

Method: 构建包含完整6DoF刚体动力学、半经验地面效应气动模型、执行器动力学、传感器噪声及环境扰动的模块化仿真框架，基于Torch和NVIDIA Warp实现CPU/GPU后端，支持并行仿真，并提供统一接口以集成RL和传统控制算法，同时支持与X-Plane和JSBSim的交叉验证。

Result: 实现了高物理保真度与高吞吐量的仿真环境，可进行大规模强化学习训练和最优控制展开，并支持多种任务（如高度调节、轨迹跟踪）和多控制器评估，且能与其他飞行模拟器互操作。

Conclusion: 该仿真框架为固定翼近地飞行控制研究提供了逼真、高效且模块化的工具，有助于系统性分析和验证低空飞行策略。

Abstract: We present a modular, high-fidelity simulation framework for the development and benchmarking of flight control strategies in fixed-wing aerial robots operating near the ground. Unlike existing simulators that rely on simplified or hover-oriented dynamics, our framework models full 6DoF rigid-body physics, semi-empirical ground-effect aerodynamics, actuator dynamics, sensor noise, and environmental disturbances. This physical realism, combined with modular component design, enables systematic analysis of low-altitude flight behavior under realistic conditions. The simulator supports both CPU and GPU backends via Torch and NVIDIA Warp, enabling high-throughput parallel execution suitable for large-scale reinforcement learning training and optimal control rollouts. A unified interface accommodates a range of controllers (both RL and optical control algorithms) across tasks such as altitude regulation and trajectory tracking. Cross-validation with X-Plane and JSBSim is also supported to facilitate engineering integration and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [137] [How to Learn from What a Human Would Avoid? Intervention-Aware World Models with Real-World RL for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06009)
*Jiaju Yin,Zhenhui Zhang,Lixin Xu,Heng Zhang,Jun Shao,Yating Feng,Arash Ajoudani,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WHIRL框架，将人类二值干预转化为前向预测信号，通过干预感知的潜在世界模型预测未来状态下的人类接管概率，并作为风险塑形项约束策略，在多指灵巧手任务中提升了成功率并显著降低了操作员干预负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作在真实世界RL中因高维动作空间和硬件成本而困难。现有HIL RL将人类干预视为反应性修正，丢弃了操作员决定接管时蕴含的丰富安全信号。因此希望学习“人类会避免什么”，将干预信号转化为主动风险规避信号。

Method: 构建干预感知的潜在世界模型，包含四个预测头：动力学、奖励、终止以及新颖的逐状态干预概率头，用于预测未来状态被人类接管概率。该概率头为Actor提供风险塑形项，惩罚进入“易干预”区域，从而建模操作员内部安全阈值。

Result: 在16-DoF LEAP Hand上覆盖凸/不规则物体抓取、棱柱操作和长程多阶段任务，复杂抓取任务的成功率达96.7%，操作员干预负担（按步加权）最多降低84%。

Conclusion: 通过将人类直觉与预测性世界建模闭环，为真实世界训练复杂灵巧智能体提供了安全感知的实用方案，减少了操作员疲劳与硬件风险暴露。

Abstract: Multi-fingered dexterous manipulation remains a frontier for real-world reinforcement learning (RL) due to the high-dimensional action space and the prohibitive cost of hardware failures. While human-in-the-loop (HIL) RL allows operators to intervene before failures occur, current pipelines often treat these interventions as reactive corrections, discarding the rich safety signal inherent in the operator's decision to take control. In this paper, we ask: How can we learn from what a human would avoid? We present WHIRL, a safety-aware RL framework that transforms binary human interventions into forward-predictive signals for proactive risk avoidance. Our approach centers on an intervention-aware latent world model with four prediction heads: dynamics, reward, termination, and a novel per-state intervention-probability head that learns to predict the likelihood of a human takeover at future states. This head provides an actor-side risk-shaping term that discourages the policy from entering "intervention-prone" regions, modeling the operator's internal safety threshold. We evaluate our framework on a 16-DoF LEAP Hand across tasks spanning convex and irregular object grasping, prismatic manipulation, and long-horizon multi-stage tasks. Our results show that predictive risk-shaping enables the system to achieve a 96.7 percent success rate on complex grasping tasks while reducing the operator intervention burden by up to 84 percent in step-weighted terms. By closing the loop between human intuition and predictive world modeling, this work provides a practical safety-aware recipe for training complex dexterous agents in the real world while reducing operator fatigue and hardware-risk exposure.

</details>


### [138] [GLoRI: Closed-Loop Whole-Body Tracking with Global-Local Reference Interaction for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05994)
*Qingyao Xu,Sheng Yin,Zibo Zhou,Ya Zhang,Siheng Chen,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLoRI是一个闭环全身控制器，通过全局-局部交叉注意力网络融合全局参考与局部运动指导，在人形移动操作中实现高精度世界坐标系跟踪，并支持直接仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形移动操作需要世界坐标系下的全身运动跟踪，但局部参考缺乏绝对空间约束导致累积误差，现有全局方法未显式整合全局校正与局部运动指导，限制自主跟踪精度。

Method: 提出GLoRI，其核心GLoRI-Net采用Global-Local Cross Attention (GLCA)将全局目标与位姿差异特征融合进局部关键点特征，保留运动结构的同时校正世界坐标位置，实现闭环控制。

Result: 在保留的HuMoTo动作上达到100%完成率和6.44cm的g-MPJPE，在Isaac Gym到MuJoCo直接迁移中保持鲁棒性；在真实Unitree G1上无需微调即可用单一策略应对多样未见物体，超越依赖遥操作或单物体交互的先前系统。

Conclusion: GLoRI通过结构化全局反馈与局部指导的显式集成，显著提升自主移动操作的精度与泛化能力，证明跨仿真环境迁移和真实世界多物体操作的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires accurate whole-body motion tracking in the world frame for physical interaction. While local references preserve motion structure, they lack explicit constraints on absolute spatial placement, leading to accumulated global errors. Existing globally aware approaches augment teleoperation policies with global observations but do not explicitly integrate global correction with local motion guidance, limiting autonomous tracking accuracy. We present GLoRI, a closed-loop whole-body controller that integrates structured global reference and feedback with local motion guidance. Its GLoRI-Net uses Global-Local Cross Attention(GLCA) to refine local keypoint features with global target and pose-difference features, preserving motion structure while correcting world-frame placement. GLoRI achieves 100% completion and a g-MPJPE of 6.44cm on held-out HuMoTo motions. This accuracy remains robust under direct Isaac Gym-to-MuJoCo transfer without fine-tuning, demonstrating strong generalization. Furthermore, such accuracy and generalization enable autonomous loco-manipulation with a single policy on a real Unitree G1 interacting with diverse unseen objects, extending beyond prior systems that primarily rely on teleoperation or focus on single-object interactions.

</details>


### [139] [A Brain-inspired Hierarchical Framework for Zero-Shot Robot Task Reasoning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05985)
*Guangming Wang,Pengfei Ye,Qizhen Ying,Yixiong Jing,Yuxiang Ma,Haonan Chen,Haibing Wu,Olaf Wysocki,Molong Duan,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本分层框架，通过显式物体状态推理、原子动作库组合、成本排序和闭环验证，提升开放指令跟随机器人的长时任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉-语言-动作策略缺乏显式长程分解、物理验证和恢复机制，难处理开放指令与物理交互。

Method: 仿脑分工：视觉感知和状态推断→语言基元和原子动作序列生成→成本计划选择→真实执行验证；组合原子动作，按成本排名，从观察刷新验证中间状态。

Result: 擦板10/10、拾放10/10、金字塔堆叠4/5，任务进度均值99.03%、100.00%、96.67%，超越ReKep、Dream2Flow、π0.5。

Conclusion: 显式状态推理+组合原子动作+成本计划+闭环验证能有效提升开放指令机器人成功率。

Abstract: Robots that follow open-ended language instructions need to connect semantic intent to visual scene understanding, geometric feasibility, object states, and physical interaction conditions. End-to-end Vision-Language-Action policies have improved cross-task generalization, but they typically map visual and language inputs directly to robot actions, leaving limited explicit structure for long-horizon decomposition, physical verification, and recovery. We present \method, a zero-shot hierarchical framework functionally inspired by the division of roles in the human brain, comprising visual perception and state inference, language grounding and action-sequence generation from a shared atomic action library, cost-based plan selection, and real-robot execution and verification. The framework grounds commands in explicit object states, composes reusable atomic actions into task-conditioned sequences, ranks alternative sequences by execution cost, and verifies intermediate physical outcomes from refreshed observations. In the evaluation, \method{} completes 10/10 clean board trials, 10/10 pick-and-place trials, and 4/5 pyramid stacking trials for both the flat and irregular initial-layout conditions; the corresponding mean task progress is $99.03\%$, $100.00\%$, and $96.67\%$ respectively. Across all evaluated conditions, \method{} achieves higher success rates than ReKep, Dream2Flow, and $\pi_{0.5}$ benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining explicit object-state reasoning, compositional atomic actions, cost-based plan selection, and closed-loop execution verification.

</details>


### [140] [Observation Design for Certified Control Authority: Projection--Estimability Separation and Active-Face Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05962)
*Guangxi Wan,Hualong Du,Yuqi Liu,Qingwei Dong,Qingxin Li,Hongfei Bai,Peng Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究运行时准入门控中观测设计如何最大化可安全准入的动作集合，揭示了投影-可估计性分离与活动面等价定理，表明该问题并非经典实验设计问题的简单改写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时准入门控只执行可认证的动作，而认证依赖于观测数据。关键问题是如何设计观测以最大化可安全准入的动作集合。作者希望证明这一问题具有独特的结构，不能仅归结为经典的最优设计问题，从而为安全关键系统的观测资源配置提供理论基础。

Method: 采用理论分析与凸优化框架。首先引入信息矩阵的投影分解，将约束法向量分解为值域与核方向，分析两类方向在预算增长下的判别能力与鲁棒准入可行性。其次建立活动面等价定理，证明最优正则性设计等价于一个由瓶颈活动面内生产物的L-最优设计，并利用Carathéodory论证给出瓶颈面数量上界。此外，推导了精确可认证性、常数κ≈3.29、契约无关设计的代价以及信息-松弛传递定理，并通过审计实验验证。

Result: 得到投影-可估计性分离：c_Range非零时可任意可靠判别，c_kernel非零时任何预算下都无法鲁棒准入；混合方向在秩亏时普遍存在，满秩时由Kantorovich比界定且随条件数发散。活动面等价定理表明误许可最优设计是L-最优的，单面退化恢复c-最优与目标导向设计。还给出最多r(r+1)/2+1个面的瓶颈证书，以及κ≈3.29等精确结果。审计发现：即使设计满足所有边际要求，仍存在一个动作面的认证成本超过其测试成本十倍。

Conclusion: 观测设计在准入控制中具有全新且非平凡的结构：最优设计必须面向活动瓶颈面而非简单判别准则，且经典设计（如判别最优）会落入不可行的核方向区域。该工作为安全运行时验证提供了新的最优设计准则与理论界限，并强调了在复杂约束下观测资源分配需要专门考虑。

Abstract: A sound runtime admission gate executes only actions it can certify, and certifies only what its observations support. This paper asks how observations should be designed to maximize the set of actions that can be safely admitted, and shows the question is not a re-vocabulary of classical design problems. First, a projection--estimability separation: decomposing a constraint normal as $c=c_{\mathrm{Range}}+c_{\ker}$ relative to an information matrix, two-point discrimination along $c$ becomes arbitrarily reliable as the budget grows whenever $c_{\mathrm{Range}}\neq 0$, while robust admission of an action with normal $c$ is impossible at every budget whenever $c_{\ker}\neq 0$; such mixed directions are generic at any deficient rank, and at full rank the decoupling is bounded by the Kantorovich ratio and diverges with the condition number. Discrimination-optimal designs, being corner solutions of a linear criterion, land in exactly this regime. Second, an active-face equivalence theorem: the permissiveness-optimal design is $L$-optimal for a target matrix generated endogenously by the action faces that become certification bottlenecks, weighted inversely by their remaining slack; single-face collapse recovers $c$-optimal and goal-oriented design exactly, and a Caratheodory argument yields a bottleneck certificate of at most $r(r+1)/2+1$ faces. Around these we assemble exact certifiability per convex contract mode, for which $\kappa\approx 3.29$ is derived rather than calibrated, the $\sqrt{r}$ price of contract-agnostic design, an information-to-slack transfer theorem with a curvature-budget corollary, and a two-part audit in which a design meeting every margin requirement still leaves an action face at a certification cost above ten times its testing cost, in every probe library tested.

</details>


### [141] [Moment-Matching Probabilistic Data Association for Optimization-Based SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05941)
*Khoa Nguyen,Mitchell Turton,Florian Meyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将概率数据关联（PDA）集成到基于优化的SLAM中的方法，通过将PDA更新转换为等价的线性高斯测量更新，使iSAM2在存在误检和漏检时提升定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化式SLAM通过回环检测减少累积误差，但传统数据关联将每个测量与单一地标匹配，在虚假检测或漏检情况下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 在优化式SLAM的非线性最小二乘求解之外增加处理阶段：概率性地将多个测量分配给地标；通过矩匹配计算地标分布的均值和协方差；构造等效的虚拟地标测量及其线性高斯模型，使PDA更新无缝嵌入任意优化式SLAM。

Result: 初步数值评估显示，在含假阳性和假阴性场景中，结合所提PDA方法的iSAM2相比传统iSAM2提高了智能体定位性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效增强基于优化的SLAM对错误数据关联的鲁棒性，可作为现有优化式SLAM框架的通用增强模块。

Abstract: Optimization-based simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) makes it possible to reduce accumulated navigation errors of sensing platforms by returning to known areas (loop closure). In this paper, we present an approach to combine probabilistic data association (PDA) with optimization-based SLAM. Instead of associating a single measurement with each landmark, we follow the PDA paradigm from the multiobject tracking community. In particular, in a processing stage performed in addition to the nonlinear least-squares solver of optimization-based SLAM, our method (i) assigns multiple measurements to landmarks probabilistically, (ii) computes the mean and covariance of landmark distributions via moment matching by taking multiple measurement-to-landmark associations into account, and (iii) establishes a virtual landmark measurement and a corresponding linear-Gaussian measurement model that leads to the mean and covariance matrix as moment-matching PDA in (ii). By converting the PDA update step into an equivalent linear-Gaussian measurement update step, PDA can be performed effectively within any optimization-based SLAM method. Our preliminary numerical evaluation in a scenario with false negatives and false positives indicates that incremental smoothing and mapping 2 (iSAM2), combined with the proposed PDA approach, can improve agent localization performance compared to conventional iSAM2.

</details>


### [142] [GIF: Agentic Generation of Interactive and Functional Object Compositions for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05927)
*Long Xu,Zhiqi Zhang,Mi Yan,Shengliang Deng,Chong Xia,Mingyu Dong,Jiayi Chen,Jiangran Lyu,Fei Gao,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GIF，一个用于生成交互式和功能性物体组合的智能体框架，通过解耦重建和相对位姿恢复，结合2D/3D生成模型、几何与物理引导的位姿精化以及VLM验证，显著提升资产质量和关系匹配，并将碰撞率降至1%以下，同时支持策略学习的数据扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作基础模型需要可扩展的评估和数据生成，模拟环境是重要途径。现有自动场景生成主要关注粗粒度场景布局，而忽略了细粒度的功能性物体组合（如接触几何关系），导致生成场景缺乏交互真实性和功能有效性。

Method: GIF框架包含三步：1) CoGen模块利用2D和3D生成模型的互补优势，生成实例解耦的网格并估计粗略初始位姿；2) GPRM模块在联合几何和物理引导下精化相对位姿，确保交互合理性；3) VLM验证器根据结构化规范筛选最佳候选。同时构建了涵盖八类代表性接触几何类别的基准。

Result: 与最先进的生成器相比，GIF在资产质量和关系匹配上均取得提升，碰撞率降至1%以下。合成的策略学习数据在仿真和真实部署中均展现出多样性扩展的有效性。

Conclusion: GIF为自动化生成细粒度功能性物体组合提供了可行方案，兼顾几何真实性与物理合理性，有望成为机器人操作基础模型规模化数据生成与评估的重要工具。

Abstract: Robot manipulation foundation models require scalable evaluation and data generation across diverse scenarios, with simulation providing an environment for both. Automated scene generation offers a promising path, yet prior work has largely emphasized coarse-grained scene layouts rather than fine-grained functional object compositions. Motivated by this gap, we present GIF, an agentic Generation framework for Interactive and Functional object compositions. In this framework, we recast this problem as disentangled reconstruction followed by relative pose recovery. CoGen produces instance-disentangled meshes with coarse initial poses leveraging complementary strengths of 2D and 3D generative models. GPRM refines the relative pose under joint geometric and physical guidance, and a VLM verifier selects the candidate that best matches the structured specification. We further construct a benchmark spanning eight representative contact-geometry classes and compare with state-of-the-art generators; GIF improves both asset quality and relation matching, while reducing collision rate to below 1%. Finally, we synthesize data for policy learning, revealing diversity scaling in both simulation and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [143] [A4A: Cross-Embodiment Transfer of Action-Oriented 4D Affordances from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05892)
*Yifan Han,Litao Liu,Yuqi Gu,Ye Lu,Hanqing Wang,Sidney Wai,Ishaan Myrie,Qi Zhang,Jingjin Yu,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动作导向的4D可供性表示，通过预测交互相关3D点的语言条件未来轨迹，将人类演示中的操作知识迁移到机器人控制，并构建A4A框架进行预训练，提升多种VLA策略的操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可供性表示只关注交互位置（如2D掩码、3D区域等），忽略了交互相关几何在任务执行中的演化方式，难以有效迁移操作知识。

Method: 定义动作导向4D可供性，表示交互相关3D点的语言条件未来轨迹；基于该表示构建大规模数据集，并开发A4A框架，利用4D轨迹预测预训练机器人策略后再进行操作微调。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，A4A预训练一致提升了多种VLA策略的操作性能，验证了动作导向4D可供性的有效性。

Conclusion: 动作导向4D可供性是一种有效的跨具身表示，能从人类演示中迁移操作知识到机器人控制。

Abstract: Human demonstrations contain rich manipulation knowledge, but it remains unclear what information can be transferred effectively to robot control. Existing affordance representations are typically formulated as 2D masks, 3D regions, contact points, or actionability scores, and therefore primarily identify where interaction may occur. However, effective manipulation also requires modeling how interaction-relevant geometry evolves during task execution. To bridge this gap, we introduce action-oriented 4D affordances, which represent the language-conditioned future trajectories of interaction-relevant 3D points. These trajectories capture task-conditioned geometric evolution rather than embodiment-specific actions, enabling transferable interaction priors across humans and robots. Based on this representation, we construct a large-scale action-oriented 4D affordance dataset from existing human--object interaction video data and complementary RGB-D demonstrations, and introduce A4A, an affordance-to-action framework that uses 4D affordance trajectory prediction to pretrain robot policies before manipulation fine-tuning. Experiments in both simulation and the real world validate the effectiveness of A4A, showing that pretraining with action-oriented 4D affordance data consistently improves the manipulation performance of diverse VLA policies. These results establish action-oriented 4D affordances as an effective cross-embodiment representation for transferring manipulation knowledge from human demonstrations to robot control.

</details>


### [144] [CR-VLA-Force: Learning Control-aware Compliance VLA Model for Robust Contact-rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05832)
*Zhaohong Mai,Chao Wang,Chao Zeng,Sitong Mao,Heng Zhang,Shunbo Zhou,Chenguang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种控制感知的柔顺VLA框架（CC-VLA），融合力/力矩感知与视觉语言信息，通过多模态混合专家、多阶段训练和自适应柔顺控制器，提升机器人操作中的力跟踪精度与实时响应能力，在挑战性力感知任务和部分域外位姿偏移下显著提高成功率和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有力感知VLA模型在精确力跟踪和快速连续调整方面能力有限，主要受限于动作块执行策略和感知到控制的大延迟，导致过度接触力无法及时修正，造成任务失败和安全风险。

Method: CC-VLA框架包括：多模态混合专家（MoE）编码力信号序列和视觉-语言融合特征；多阶段训练策略增强视觉语义空间鲁棒性和稀疏采样下力感知；VLA引导的自适应柔顺控制器，实现无接触运动中的精确位置跟踪和接触丰富任务中的最优力-位置跟踪；对抗共享遥操作策略用于获取高精度力/力矩演示数据。

Result: 大量真实世界实验表明，CC-VLA在挑战性力感知任务中显著提升成功率，增强力控制精度，并在测试的部分域外位姿偏移设置下提供多层次安全性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CC-VLA有效克服了传统动作块执行和延迟问题，实现了更精确、更安全的力感知操作，为力觉VLA模型在精细操控中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Integrating visuomotor policies or Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with force/torque (F/T) perception has demonstrated significant progress in imitation learning for robotic manipulation. However, existing force-aware VLA models frequently exhibit limited capability in precise force tracking and rapid successive adjustments. This deficiency stems from the limitations of action-chunk execution strategies and the substantial latency between perception and real-time control. Such limitations can lead to task failures and safety risks, particularly when the execution of an action chunk exerts excessive interaction forces without timely adjustment. To overcome this challenge, we propose the Control-aware Compliance VLA (CC-VLA) framework for reactive control. The CC-VLA model employs a multimodal mixture-of-experts (MoE) to encode force signal sequences and vision-language fused feature. Furthermore, it utilizes a multi-stage training strategy to ensure robust perception within the visual-semantic space and effective force perception under sparse sampling conditions. Additionally, a VLA-guided adaptive compliance controller is designed to facilitate precise position tracking during contact-free motion and optimal force-position tracking for contact-rich tasks. To facilitate high-precision F/T data acquisition, we also implement an adversaria shared teleoperation strategy for contact-rich demonstrations that bolsters system safety and interactivity. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that CC-VLA significantly improves success rates in challenging force-perception tasks and enhances force-control precision, while providing multi-level safety and robustness under the tested partial-OOD pose-shift settings.

</details>


### [145] [Closed-Loop Evaluation of Bird's-Eye-View Maps from Cross-View Transformers as Inputs to Behavior-Cloning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05783)
*Felipe Carlos dos Santos,Eric Antonelo,Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在CARLA模拟器中使用Cross-View Transformer预测的鸟瞰图（BEV）作为行为克隆策略的输入，提出六通道BEV表示和核密度估计（KDE）加权损失，发现KDE加权模型虽未取得最高IoU，却是唯一能无违规完成完整路线的预测BEV智能体，表明全局分割指标不能代表驾驶性能，预测质量在几何关键位置更重要，且路线通道是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶中，BEV表示是行为克隆策略的关键输入。模拟中可使用真值BEV，但现实部署需用相机预测的BEV来替代，这种替代引入的感知误差对闭环驾驶性能的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 采用CVT预测的BEV地图作为BC智能体的直接策略输入；提出一个六通道BEV表示，涵盖路面、规划路线、车道边界、车辆、行人和交通灯；并引入KDE加权方案，重新平衡分割损失，使其更关注曲线、交叉路口等代表性不足的驾驶操作。

Result: 在两个CARLA城镇的闭环评估中，KDE加权模型是唯一能在无违规情况下完成完整路线的预测BEV智能体，尽管其综合IoU不是最高。全局分割指标与驾驶性能不一致，成功导航取决于几何关键位置的预测质量，其中路线通道是预测BEV输入下可靠导航的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了预测BEV输入下BC智能体的性能关键：不应以全局IoU为优化目标，而应关注关键位置的感知精度及路线通道的可靠性，这为实际部署提供了指导。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, Bird's-Eye View (BEV) representations provide a structured, top-down abstraction of the vehicle's surroundings and have become a key input modality for Behavioral Cloning (BC) policies.
