While ground-truth BEV maps are readily available in simulation, real-world deployment requires replacing them with camera-predicted counterparts - a substitution that introduces perceptual errors whose downstream impact on closed-loop driving performance is not well understood. In this work, we investigate the use of Cross-View Transformer (CVT)-predicted BEV maps as direct policy inputs for a BC agent in the CARLA simulator. We propose a six-channel BEV representation covering road surface, planned route, lane boundaries, vehicles, pedestrians, and traffic lights, and introduce a Kernel Density Estimation (KDE) weighting scheme that rebalances the segmentation loss towards underrepresented driving maneuvers such as curves and intersections. Closed-loop evaluation across two CARLA towns shows that the KDE-weighted model is the only predicted-BEV agent to complete a full episode without infractions, despite not achieving the highest aggregate IoU. This discrepancy reveals that global segmentation metrics are poor proxies for driving performance: what determines navigation success is prediction quality at geometrically critical locations, and the route channel emerges as the primary bottleneck for reliable agent navigation under predicted BEV inputs.

</details>


### [146] [Adaptive Cost-Sensitive Machine Learning for Autonomous Robot Navigation Failure Prediction: When Not All Errors Are Equal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05593)
*Rifa Ferzana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将导航失败预测重构为后果敏感的预测问题，通过状态依赖的后果函数（结合严重性、速度、障碍物距离和感知不确定性）自适应调整训练权重，在模拟和真实数据集上显著降低了漏报代价，但主要收益来自更保守的操作点而非排名能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人导航失败不仅因类别严重性而异，还取决于物理环境（如高速、近障碍、感知退化），传统固定严重性分类无法捕捉后果差异，导致风险分配不准确。

Method: 先建立固定基线（按类别严重性调制训练权重），再引入自适应扩展：定义状态相关的后果函数，融合严重性、归一化速度、障碍物接近度和感知不确定性，并加入随条件恶化而升高的风险敏感项。采用2000条模拟差分驱动回合（约100万时间步）进行episode-level GroupKFold评估，并在UCI SCITOS G5数据集上外部验证。

Result: 固定加权使Logistic回归高严重性召回率从0.851提到0.985，漏报代价从1940降至313；自适应扩展达到0.998和82。但匹配假阳性条件下判别优势很小（0.986对0.984），证明主要增益源于更保守的操作点。在600个含碰撞回合的受控扩展中（108次碰撞），固定与自适应的碰撞召回率分别从0.850升至0.966和0.984，漏报代价从1000降至105，假阳性率分别为0.413和0.799。效果在所有五折和跨三倍上下文系数下稳定。

Conclusion: 上下文依赖的后果建模提供了一种按物理风险分配保守性的原则性机制，能显著降低漏报代价，但需注意在提升实际安全性时，其优势主要体现为调整决策阈值而非改进基础排序能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation failures differ not only in categorical severity but also in the physical context in which they occur. A near-miss at low speed under reliable sensing is not equivalent to the same event during rapid motion, close obstacle approach or degraded perception. This paper reframes navigation failure prediction as consequence-sensitive forecasting. We first establish a fixed baseline in which training weights are modulated by categorical severity, then introduce an adaptive extension defining a state-dependent consequence function combining severity with normalised velocity, obstacle proximity and sensing uncertainty, together with a risk-sensitivity term that rises as conditions deteriorate. We evaluate on 2,000 simulated differential-drive episodes (~1,000,000 timesteps) using episode-level GroupKFold, with external validation on the UCI SCITOS G5 dataset. Fixed weighting raises Logistic Regression high-severity recall from 0.851 to 0.985 and reduces missed consequence cost from 1,940 to 313; the adaptive extension reaches 0.998 and 82. Under matched false-positive conditions, however, the discriminative advantage is modest (0.986 versus 0.984), so most of the gain reflects a more conservative operating point rather than better ranking. The effect is consistent across all five folds and stable across a threefold span of context coefficients. Because the primary simulation produced no collisions, we add a controlled extension in which 108 of 600 episodes terminate in contact: collision recall rises from 0.850 to 0.966 (fixed) and 0.984 (adaptive), with missed collision cost falling from 1,000 to 105, at false-positive rates of 0.413 and 0.799, respectively. Context-dependent consequence modelling thus provides a principled mechanism for allocating conservatism by physical risk.

</details>


### [147] [GE-Act 2.0: Pretraining and Scaling a World-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05588)
*AgiBot Research Team,Renhang Liu,Wenzhi Zhao,Zhuo Yang,Liliang Chen,Pengfei Zhou,Shengcong Chen,Guanghui Ren,Youlun Peng,Rongjun Jin,Nan Wang,Sukai Wang,Xindong He,Jinyuan Feng,Ziyu Xiong,Linqing Zhong,Yifei Wei,Feng Han,Long Zhang,Da Huang,Nanshu Zhao,Chenghao Yin,Mo Wu,Zhaodong Yan,Kongtao Hu,Yuxiang Yan,Aogelijiang Niyazi,Yu Fang,Jia Zeng,Lizhu Meng,Daizhen Lv,Haoyu Cao,Zhiwen Hou,Lianjin Ye,Yuehan Niu,Zhikai Cai,Xuan Hu,Hui Min,Xiongfeng Cai,Yue Liao,Jing Wu,Soujanya Poria,Ye Li,Sanping Zhou,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从零训练的具身世界-行为模型GE-Act 2.0，结合控制导向自编码器、单步视觉规划器和逆动力学模型，以及知识对齐的选择性优化，实现大规模跨本体零样本操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-行为模型大多继承预训练视频生成器，导致对世界模型自身的预训练和扩展性研究不足，缺乏从操作数据中直接学习可扩展的世界动态与动作表征的方法。

Method: GE-Act 2.0从操作数据中从零初始化生成与动作组件；采用控制导向自编码器（CoAE）在强压缩下保留动作和指令相关信息，单步视觉规划器（SVP）通过单次可微前向生成完整未来状态，使视觉规划与逆动力学模型可在互补数据上独立预训练；随后利用知识对齐选择性优化（KASO），仅选择与记录动作行为相容的预测未来进行联合训练，减轻监督不匹配。

Result: 在100个操作任务、20个技能组上直接评估预训练检查点（无任务微调）；将共训练数据从300小时扩至30000小时后，G1-OP成功率从17.1%升至44.1%，G2-90D从13.4%升至31.1%，且G2-90D数据仅占2%仍提升17.7个百分点，表明跨本体迁移；技能覆盖与零样本分布外成功率强相关（Pearson r=0.80, Spearman rho=0.85）；对象、颜色、形状、位置指令遵循率≥90%，并能遵循与已承诺行为或常规场景关联相冲突的显式指令。

Conclusion: 大规模跨本体具身共训练数据与面向控制的世界模型组件设计，能够显著提升世界-行为模型的零样本泛化能力和指令遵循能力，即使模型完全从零训练也无需任务级微调即可掌握多样化操作技能。

Abstract: World-action models (WAM) predict future states to guide robot actions, enabling learning from both action-free video and action-labeled interaction. Most inherit pretrained video generators, leaving WAM pretraining and scaling underexplored. We introduce Genie Envisioner Act 2.0 (GE-Act 2.0), a world-action model whose trainable generative and action components are all initialized from scratch on manipulation data. It combines a control-oriented autoencoder (CoAE), a single-step visual planner (SVP), and an inverse dynamics model (IDM). CoAE retains action- and instruction-relevant information under aggressive compression, while SVP produces a complete future state in one differentiable pass, so visual planning and inverse dynamics can be pretrained separately on complementary data. The components are then jointly trained with knowledge-aligned selective optimization (KASO), which reduces mismatched supervision by selecting only predicted futures judged behaviorally compatible with the recorded action. We evaluate pretrained checkpoints directly, without per-task fine-tuning, on 100 tasks across 20 manipulation skill groups with held-out scenes, backgrounds, lighting, and object instances. Scaling co-training data from 300 to 30,000 hours raises success from 17.1% to 44.1% on G1-OP and from 13.4% to 31.1% on G2-90D; despite comprising less than 2% of the co-training data, G2-90D improves by 17.7 points, suggesting cross-embodiment transfer. Gains span 19/20 and 18/20 skill groups, and skill-specific coverage strongly correlates with zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) success (Pearson r=0.80; Spearman rho=0.85). Under the same protocol, the model grounds object, color, shape, and position references in at least 90% of trials and follows explicit instructions even when they conflict with an already-committed behavior or a conventional scene association.

</details>


### [148] [Benchmarking Dexterity of Multifingered Robot Hands: A Review and Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05585)
*Anthony Shilati,Anunth Ramaswami,Luke Batteas,Sylvia Tan,Anthony Barcio,Sairam Umakanth,Preksha Rao,David McDougall,Landry Graves,Ahmet A. Ozkan,Yunsoo Yoon,Arushi Pradhan,Michael G. Henry,Rohan Kota,Damian Gonzalez,Gray C. Thomas,Gary K. Fedder,J. Edward Colgate,Kevin M. Lynch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 综述了多指机器人手的灵巧性基准测试，提出了一个包含三个层次（对应系统复杂度递增）的基准框架，并针对现有文献的不足提出了新的基准和指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手是AI与物理世界交互的关键接口，而多指手相比简单夹爪在操作上具有更大的潜力。然而，灵巧性是一个复杂多维的概念，现有基准测试缺乏系统性的框架来评估多指手的灵巧性。

Method: 从美国国家科学基金会HAND工程研究中心的视角出发，聚焦精细手内操作，提出由三个基准层次组成的框架（对应递增的系统复杂度），并回顾每个层次具有代表性的基准，同时提出新的基准和指标以弥补现有研究的不足。

Result: 给出了一个系统的多指手灵巧性基准测试综述，识别了现有文献的局限性，并提出了新的基准和度量方法，相关资源已公开。

Conclusion: 该框架和提出的新基准有助于更全面地评估和推动多指机器人手的灵巧性研究，为物理AI的发展提供支撑。

Abstract: Robot hands are a key interface between AI and the physical world, making advances in robotic dexterity essential to realizing the vision of physical AI. While impressive dexterity has been demonstrated with simple grippers, multifingered hands offer the potential for substantially greater versatility, precision, and adaptability in manipulation. In this review, we survey the state of the art in benchmarking the dexterity of multifingered robot hands. Recognizing dexterity as a complex and multifaceted concept, we present the perspective of the U.S. National Science Foundation HAND Engineering Research Center, with a particular focus on fine in-hand manipulation. We introduce a framework consisting of three benchmark levels that correspond to increasing system complexity, review representative benchmarks at each level, and propose new benchmarks and metrics to address limitations in the literature. More information can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [149] [Information-Guided Safe Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Gas Source Localization using sUAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05569)
*Sachin Giri,Thomas Zhao,Matthew Huynh,YangQuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种信息引导的安全强化学习框架，结合经验观测格拉姆矩阵规划器与SAC探索策略，通过KL散度动态混合及鲁棒控制屏障函数保障安全，在GPU加速3D仿真中针对湍流羽流源定位实现近80%成功率且零违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机自主定位气体排放源在湍流边界层中是不适定逆问题，标量浓度场高度间歇，破坏梯度导航假设，导致现有估计器易受噪声和虚假局部最小值影响，且确定性信息寻求规划器存在Gramian偏差，即贪婪依赖早期错误估计而缺乏空间多样性。

Method: 构建信息引导的安全强化学习框架，集成经验观测格拉姆矩阵(EMGR)规划器与SAC探索策略，由元监督者基于KL散度动态权衡确定性利用与学习探索，采用渐进课程训练，并强制使用鲁棒控制屏障函数(RCBF)保证安全。

Result: 在复杂移动源定位任务中，该框架实现近80%定位成功率，显著优于经典基线(~30%)，且零安全违规。

Conclusion: 通过打破Gramian偏差并融合智能探索与安全约束，所提方法显著提升复杂湍流场景下的自主源定位性能与安全性，为sUAS气体监测提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: The autonomous localization of fugitive gas emissions using small Unmanned Aircraft Systems (sUAS) constitutes a fundamentally ill-posed inverse problem. In turbulent atmospheric boundary layers, highly intermittent scalar concentration fields violate the assumptions of classical gradient-based navigation, causing data-driven estimators to suffer from severe noise and spurious local minima. To address these challenges, we introduce an Information-Guided Safe Reinforcement Learning framework evaluated within a custom, GPU-accelerated 3D simulation environment coupling an Eulerian wind solver with a Lagrangian puff dispersion model. We identify a critical vulnerability in deterministic information-seeking planners - a Gramian bias where agents act greedily upon flawed early estimates, starving the estimator of spatial diversity. To systematically break this degeneracy, our architecture integrates a classical empirical observability Gramian (EMGR) planner with a learned Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) exploratory policy. A deterministic meta-supervisor actively monitors estimator reliability via Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, dynamically blending deterministic exploitation with learned exploration to steer the sUAS into high-information zones. Trained via a progressive curriculum and safeguarded by a strictly enforced Robust Control Barrier Function (RCBF), our RL framework achieves nearly 80% localization success on complex, mobile sources - drastically outperforming classical baselines (~30%) - while ensuring zero safety violations.

</details>


### [150] [Situation Awareness for Intelligent Data Distribution in Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05521)
*Falk Dettinger,Akshay Narla,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于BEV图像的车辆情境识别方法，通过交通场景理解优先处理相关数据，实现高效传感器数据管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主驾驶中车载传感器受限且遮挡造成感知盲区，协作感知虽可增强但数据传输量大，需根据当前交通情境选择性地获取和使用数据。

Method: 采用Cam2BEV投影网络将RGB和语义分割图像转换为鸟瞰图，结合情境识别神经网络进行交通上下文分类；在CARLA中训练，并在Cityscapes和nuScenes上验证领域迁移性。

Result: 情境识别概念在CARLA模拟器中得到验证，并证明在城市真实数据集上具备可移植性，能够区分不同交通情境以辅助数据管理策略。

Conclusion: 所提情境识别方法能优先处理与当前交通相关的数据，减少无效传输，提升感知效率，且可能适用于真实场景。

Abstract: The limitations of on-board sensors and blind spots caused by occlusion cause the reduction of perception quality in autonomous vehicles. In such cases, cooperative perception provides additional data via Vehicle-to-Everything communication to enhance local perception, causing a large volume of data transmission. The vehicle can focus on acquiring and utilizing relevant data according to the prevailing road context by identifying the current traffic situation. To achieve this, we propose a concept for the situation identification of the vehicle using Bird's-Eye-View images. Firstly, the situation around the vehicle is identified using object detection with semantic segmentation, followed by understanding the context of the traffic using a situation identification module consisting of an open-source projective transformation network Cam2BEV and a situation identification neural network. The concept was evaluated and validated by running the software on the CARLA simulator using the in-built RGB camera and the semantic segmentation camera. Additionally, the portability of the situation identification module for real-world applications was verified on Cityscapes and nuScenes urban driving datasets. Overall, the proposed situation identification approach enables efficient sensor data management by prioritizing relevant data to the current traffic situation. The source code is available in the following link: this https URL

</details>


### [151] [Robots Influencing Humans to Reveal their Goals during Collaboration and Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05519)
*Debasmita Ghose,Oz Gitelson,Michal Lewkowicz,Jake Brawer,Marynel Vazquez,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一策略，通过在关键决策点引导人类行为，实现人机交互中快速、准确的目标推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标推断方法通常被动观察，速度慢且准确性不足，无法满足实时人机协作或对抗的需求。

Method: 利用目标条件策略散度定义关键决策点（CDPs），并集成到滚动时域规划器中，通过平衡任务进度与信息获取的代价函数搜索未来动作序列，主动引导人类经过最具信息量的状态。

Result: 在协作烹饪和对抗捉迷藏两个场景（含仿真与实物）中，所提方法相比基线策略能更早、更准确地推断人类目标。

Conclusion: 该统一策略通过主动生成信息性交互，能显著提升不同交互模式下的目标推断性能，具备良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: We propose a unified strategy for fast goal inference in human-robot interaction. The core idea is to drive the human toward Critical Decision Points (CDPs)-states where competing human strategies prescribe different next actions and thus maximally reveal the goal. We formalise CDPs using a goal-conditioned policy divergence measure and incorporate them into a Receding-Horizon Planner that explores future action sequences while optimizing a cost function balancing task progress and information gain. We evaluate this approach in both a collaborative, fully observable cooking task and a competitive, partially observable hide-and-seek game, each in simulation and on real robots. In both scenarios, our method infers human goals more accurately and earlier than baseline strategies.

</details>


### [152] [Multi-robot Learning-based Informative Path Planning Using Spatio-Temporal Gaussian Process Kalman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05515)
*Muqing Cao,Yunwoo Lee,Junbin Yuan,Lorenzo Schenk,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A decentralized grid-based GP-Kalman filtering framework for learning-based multi-robot informative path planning, improving target uncertainty and visitation in simulation and real-world UAV experiments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing learning-based multi-robot IPP methods for persistent monitoring rely on simplified sensing models and centralized belief updates, limiting scalability and real-world applicability. A decentralized approach that handles spatial uncertainty, temporal evolution, and practical sensor/communication constraints is needed.

Method: Introduces a grid-based spatio-temporal GP-Kalman filter representing anonymous target presence as a single latent field. It recursively updates beliefs from all visible cells under arbitrary camera footprints and range-dependent noise, inflates uncertainty over time to handle moving targets and stale data, and fuses received decentralized belief summaries via diagonal covariance intersection to remain conservative. A reinforcement-learning policy is integrated for graph-based neighbor selection.

Result: Simulation benchmarks show about 20% lower average target uncertainty and improved target visitation compared to learning-based and classical auction/coverage baselines. Real-world two-UAV experiments demonstrate transfer to outdoor multi-robot search over a field larger than 7000 square meters.

Conclusion: The proposed decentralized framework effectively combines spatio-temporal GP-Kalman filtering with learning-based planning, offering a practical and scalable solution for multi-robot persistent target monitoring under realistic sensing and communication conditions.

Abstract: Multi-robot informative path planning (IPP) for persistent target monitoring requires robots to reason about spatial uncertainty, temporal evolution, and practical sensing and communication constraints. Recent learning-based multi-robot IPP methods use Gaussian Processes (GPs) for target uncertainty, but often rely on simplified sensing models and centralized belief updates. We propose a grid-based spatio-temporal GP-Kalman filtering framework for learning-based multi-robot IPP. Instead of maintaining one GP per target, we represent anonymous target presence as a single latent field over a discrete workspace grid. The proposed recursive update considers all visible cells inside a camera footprint and supports arbitrary fields of view and range-dependent noise. A GP-consistent temporal process update accounts for moving targets and stale information by inflating uncertainty over time. For decentralized deployment, each robot maintains its own mapper and exchanges compact belief summaries rather than raw measurements. Received beliefs are fused using diagonal covariance intersection to remain conservative under unknown inter-robot correlations. We integrate the mapper with a reinforcement-learning policy for graph-based neighbor selection. Simulation benchmarks show about 20% lower average target uncertainty and improved target visitation compared with learning-based and classical auction/coverage baselines. Real-world two-UAV experiments demonstrate transfer to outdoor multi-robot search over a large field of more than 7000 square meters.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Decentralized Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via Predictive Shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07618)
*Yacine El Yamani,Hanna Krasowski,Elena Vanneaux*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种结合预测性护盾与基于模型的有限时域Q学习的去中心化框架，使多机器人系统能在部署中安全适应环境变化并解决对称场景下的活锁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在部署环境与训练数据存在分布偏移时，策略性能下降且安全性受损；现有安全护盾多为反应式且集中式，在未知障碍物附近性能下降且可扩展性差。

Method: 提出去中心化框架，集成预测性护盾（predictive shielding）与基于模型的有限时域Q学习（model-based finite horizon Q-learning），使机器人能在部署中安全调整预训练策略；并提出无通信的冲突消解协议以缓解对称场景中的活锁。

Result: 该框架预期能实现在未知环境中对预训练策略的安全在线适应，并通过无通信协议避免对称场景下的活锁，同时克服集中式方法在可扩展性上的局限。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种去中心化、主动式（而非反应式）的多机器人部署安全机制，兼顾策略适应能力与冲突消解，适用于先验知识有限的动态多智能体环境。

Abstract: Environments are increasingly populated by multiple robots performing independent tasks with limited prior knowledge of each other. Deploying such multi-agent systems presents significant challenges. Specifically, shifts in deployment states compared to training data can lead to poor policy performance and compromised safety. While safety shields exist to mitigate these risks, they are typically reactive, which degrades performance near unseen obstacles,and centralized, limiting their scalability. To address this, we propose a decentralized framework that integrates predictive shielding with model-based finite horizon Q-learning. This approach allows agents to safely adapt their pre-trained policies during deployment. Furthermore, to mitigate livelocks in symmetric scenarios, we introduce a communication- free protocol for conflict resolution

</details>


### [154] [Dynamic System Emulation: Fixed Wing Dynamics on a Multicopter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06520)
*Abdelhakim Amer,Andriy Sarabakha*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种控制框架，使搭载双轴云台的多旋翼飞行器能够模拟固定翼飞机的飞行动力学，用于飞行训练与仿真，并通过动态反馈线性化实现状态-输入映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼飞行器训练与仿真受空气动力学约束（如最小空速和非完整约束）影响，操作复杂；希望利用多旋翼平台提供操作简单、不受固定翼气动限制的模拟方案。

Method: 推导多旋翼与固定翼平台之间的状态-输入映射，采用动态反馈线性化方法构建控制框架，并在代表性固定翼机动上评估。

Result: 在标称条件下仿真显示高保真模拟效果，证明了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 虽在仿真中验证，但该方法为硬件部署用于飞行员培训、自主研究和控制器基准测试提供了实用途径。

Abstract: This work presents a control framework that enables a multicopter equipped with a two-axis gimbal to emulate the flight dynamics of a fixed-wing aircraft. The goal is to provide an operationally simple platform for training and simulation that avoids the aerodynamic constraints of fixed-wing vehicles, such as minimum airspeed and nonholonomic constraints. A state-input mapping between the two platforms is derived using dynamic feedback linearization. The framework is evaluated on representative fixed-wing manoeuvres. Results show high-fidelity emulation under nominal conditions. While evaluations are conducted in simulation, the approach establishes a practical path toward hardware deployment for pilot training, autonomy research, and controller benchmarking.

</details>


### [155] [Rethinking Safety for Generalist Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06326)
*Rohan Sinha,Anushri Dixit,Ran Tian,Anirudha Majumdar,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出具身AI安全的新范式，主张通用机器人的安全需超越传统碰撞/力安全，涵盖情境、用户意图和难以建模的物理后果，并给出风险分类与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人带来远超传统碰撞和力安全的风险，其安全须考虑情境（如断电仅在计划维护时安全）、用户意图（如清洁厨房隐含不应混合危险清洁剂）、难以建模的物理后果等，现有安全概念不足以应对。

Method: 通过论证提出'具身AI安全'概念，拓展机器人全生命周期需考虑的 hazards，并给出新兴风险分类法和全栈式研究议程。

Result: 形成了一份风险分类学（taxonomy）和指导通用机器人安全部署的全栈研究议程（full-stack research agenda）。

Conclusion: 未来机器人安全必须整合信息（bits）与物理（atoms）的安全，推动具身AI安全新纪元以支撑通用机器人的可靠部署。

Abstract: Generalist robots promise to transform our society: the same system that prepares a meal or folds laundry might also repair a car, inspect infrastructure, or care for a loved one. Yet this versatility introduces risks far beyond the collision- and force-based safety notions that have long dominated robotics. Notions of safety must now consider context (e.g., turning off a building's electricity is only safe during scheduled maintenance), user intent (e.g., asking the robot to ``clean the kitchen'' includes unspoken expectations that the robot should not mix dangerous but powerful cleaning agents like bleach and ammonia), hard-to-model physical consequences (e.g., burning food during meal preparation), and more. We argue the need for a new era of robot safety---embodied AI safety---that broadens the hazards considered across the robot's lifecycle while recognizing that the safety of bits cannot be separated from the safety of atoms. We present a taxonomy of emerging risks and a full-stack research agenda to guide the safe deployment of generalist robots.

</details>
